Chapter 1: He’s Just a Speck of Dust [Start of Vol 1]

Choo choo! Choo choo!

A pitch-black steam locomotive chugged along the tracks, spewing white steam.

Clang! Clang!

The crankshaft drove the connecting rods, spinning the wheels at high speed as they struck the gaps in the railway tracks with rhythmic clanks.

A shrill whistle pierced the air.

Tooooooot!

A train with just five carriages passed through a tunnel and into a mountain valley. It was headed toward the vast wilderness of Puluo Province.

Inside the third carriage, a beautiful woman was drawing her eyebrows at a dressing table.

Some might wonder, why was there a dressing table in the train carriage? And why was she even doing her makeup on a train? Well, not only could she do her makeup in the carriage, she could even dance in it. That was because she had the entire carriage to herself.

This woman was Zhuo Yuling—the second concubine of Lu Dongliang, the head of the Lu Family. In Puluo Province, the Lu Family was affluent and influential, and it owned this train. That meant Zhuo Yuling was the mistress of this train.

The first carriage carried coal. The second carriage was filled with luggage and train supplies. The third carriage was partitioned into a corridor and two private cabins for guests. The fourth carriage served as a dining car and game room. The fifth was the bedroom of the train's owner.

Zhuo Yuling combed through her luscious hair, pushing with her other hand to form finger waves[1]. Her eyeshadow was heavy, but her blush was light. Leaving her cheeks fair made her vivid red lips stand out even more.

She wore a blue-green high-collared qipao[2] made of indanthrene-dyed cloth[3], the hem just reaching her knees. There was a slit on both sides of the qipao, revealing the lace of the petticoat she wore underneath. With a snug waistline and subtly padded shoulders, she exuded a glamorous allure.

Zhuo Yuling was nearing forty, but her exquisite attire and makeup made her appear as if she had yet to even reach thirty.

Creak...

Someone pushed open the carriage door. Under the soft yellow glow of the wall lamp, Lu Dongliang, the head of the Lu Family, stepped inside.

Seeing him, the fragmented smile lines on Zhuo Yuling’s face drew together, rising with the beauty mark at the corner of her lips. Her sweet smile was reflected in the mirror on her dressing table.

Zhuo Yuling disliked wearing shoes. She walked barefoot across the dark yellow floral carpet and came to stand before Lu Dongliang.

Gazing into his eyes, she said affectionately, "My lord."[4]

Compared to her, Lu Dongliang was much older. He was over fifty and dressed in a Western business suit.

He gently pinched Zhuo Yuling’s cheeks. To him, she still looked as charming and lovable as the day they first met. No matter what time it was, seeing her always brought him a bit of comfort. However, it was only a bit, and that was it.

Feeling exhausted, Lu Dongliang took off his jacket, unbuttoned his waistcoat, and loosened his tie. He sat down on the sofa, opened a bottle of foreign alcohol, and poured himself a glass. Halfway through the drink, the gloom in his heart emerged on his face, and he let out a deep sigh.

Zhuo Yuling sat beside him and pulled out a cigarette from a small metal box. She held it between her red lips, struck a match, and lit it. Then she offered the cigarette to Lu Dongliang.

She asked, "What’s troubling you?"

Lu Dongliang accepted the cigarette and took a long, deep drag. "He Jiaqing is coming back tomorrow. Earlier, I was playing mahjong with his eldest aunt, He Yuxiu. And judging by her tone, I think they might be calling off He Jiaqing’s engagement with Xiaolan."

He Jiaqing was the eldest son of the He Family, a very powerful and tyrannical family in Puluo Province. Lu Xiaolan was Zhuo Yuling and Lu Dongliang’s daughter. If the He Family hadn’t fallen on hard times in recent years, they would never have even entertained the possibility of their eldest son marrying a woman born of a concubine, even if she was from the affluent and influential Lu Family.

Despite the bad news, Zhuo Yuling appeared unworried and smiled nonchalantly. "My lord, didn’t we already discuss this? Xiaolan won’t let He Jiaqing make it back to Puluo alive."

Lu Dongliang bit down on the cigarette, frowning deeply. "The He Family’s boy is exceptionally gifted. He’s got the Arcane Crimson Lotus. He won’t be easy to deal with. I’m worried that Xiaolan—"

Zhuo Yuling shook her head. "You’re overthinking it. That boy isn’t exceptional; he’s mediocre. Instead of spending his youth on cultivation, he went off to study in Huan. Four whole years, wasted. And what did he gain from it?"

Lu Dongliang stroked the handlebar mustache above his lip. "He’s young, so he’s too focused on having fun right now. But once he takes over as the head of the He Family…"

Zhuo Yuling stood up and walked over to the gramophone[5]. "Someone who can’t suppress their desire to have fun is a fool. And a fool that acquires a treasure is a dead man walking. As long as he boards the train, he’s as good as dead."

The gramophone was around one-third of a meter taller than the bedside drawers and had three golden horns extending upward, one large and two small. Zhuo Yuling picked up a metal cup and used it to pour two cups of water into the small horn on the left.

Lu Dongliang looked at Zhuo Yuling and said, "I heard the young master of the Jiangxiang Gang is after the Arcane Crimson Lotus too. He’s a prominent figure in Huan. He won’t be easy to deal with either."

Zhuo Yuling sneered. "My lord, you mean Xiao Zhenggong? That idiot isn’t even on par with a mediocre person. He’s just a cockroach—a disgusting little cockroach. Without the protection of his father’s name, he would’ve been stomped dead long ago. How could he possibly outwit Xiaolan with that tiny brain of his?"

She bent down and opened the gramophone cabinet, revealing a metal box filled with solidified fat. There was a wick sticking out of the fat, and she set it alight.

Fwoosh!

A pale blue flame flared to life. Soon after, the left horn released a thin puff of steam.

Hisssssss!

The gramophone was powered by steam.

Puluo Province was a special place. Fuel couldn’t be stored in large quantities, nor could electricity be used on a large scale. Consequently, steam engines were used to their fullest potential there.

Driven by gears and powered by bursts of steam, the gramophone’s turntable began to spin. Zhuo Yuling picked up a vinyl record and placed it onto the turntable.

Lu Dongliang stubbed out the cigarette and leaned back into the sofa. "It’s not that I don’t trust Xiaolan. I’m just worried that—"

"But you don’t trust her," Zhuo Yuling refuted him with a helpless smile. "Ever since I gave birth to her, you’ve never once looked her straight in the eye."

As she spoke, Zhuo Yuling lowered the gramophone’s stylus onto the record.

Amid the faint crackle of static, a smooth cello prelude began to play. Once the prelude was done, the distinct notes of a piano took over, accompanied by the soft stirrings of a violin. The gentle melody and relaxing tempo of the instruments welcomed a deep and steady vocal.

「The moonlight~ is so faint and blurry.

The earth~ is shrouded in the foggy night.

The one who visits my dreams…

My dear~ where are you? ♪」

It was "Dream Lover," Zhuo Yuling’s favorite song.

This old gramophone needed neither electricity nor magnetic discs to play music; all it needed was friction between its stylus and the record.

Zhuo Yuling walked over to Lu Dongliang with slow, deliberate steps. She pulled him to his feet and buried her head in his chest. Wrapping her arms around him, she swayed slowly to the beautiful song.

Lu Dongliang wrapped his arms around Zhuo Yuling’s slender waist, but the song wasn’t enough to wash away his concerns.

Still wearing the same worried expression, he asked, "If He Jiaqing dies, wouldn’t the Arcane Crimson Lotus be lost in Huan?"

Zhuo Yuling furrowed her brows slightly. She didn’t want to talk about such trivial matters right now.

"Xiaolan has already looked into it. He Jiaqing has a friend named Li Banfeng. He knows where the Arcane Crimson Lotus is. Once He Jiaqing is dead, Xiaolan will find Li Banfeng and figure out a way to get her hands on the Arcane Crimson Lotus."

Lu Dongliang didn’t quite catch the unfamiliar name. "What did you say was the name of He Jiaqing’s friend? Li… what?"

"Li Banfeng."

"Banfeng? Half crazy?[6]" Lu Dongliang paused in surprise. "What a strange name."

Zhuo Yuling chuckled. "You don’t need to remember his name. It’s not important. Once Xiaolan gets the Arcane Crimson Lotus’ whereabouts out of him, he’ll be gone from this world."

"What kind of person is he? Another mediocre person?" Lu Dongliang asked. He was being very prudent, afraid that mishaps might appear at every stage of their plan.

Zhuo Yuling laughed coldly. "Mediocre person? He doesn’t even count as a person."

Lu Dongliang gently caressed Zhuo Yuling’s hair. "So, he’s like Xiao Zhenggong then, just another cockroach?"

Zhuo Yuling shook her head. "He’s even worse than a cockroach."

That only piqued Lu Dongliang’s curiosity even more. "Just what kind of person is he?"

"How should I put it…"

Zhuo Yuling didn’t quite know how to describe Li Banfeng.

A wisp of black ash drifted from the gramophone’s fuel chamber and landed on Lu Dongliang’s collar. The gramophone’s filter probably needed cleaning.

Zhuo Yuling brushed the ash from Lu Dongliang’s collar with her finger. Gazing at the smudge on her fingertip, she wore a small smile. "He’s as insignificant as this bit of ash. No parents, no relatives… He has nothing. He grew up in an orphanage and had to work various jobs so that he could go to university—moving bricks, hauling sacks, and even running a street stall. When someone like that dies… that’s all there is to it. It’s just like blowing away this bit of ash. No one would care."

Zhuo Yuling gently exhaled and blew the bit of ash off her fingertip.

Tooooooot!

The train whistle rang, accompanied by a sudden jolting of the train.

Despite that, Lu Dongliang’s steps remained steady. That was a foundational skill of Wanderlust Cultivators.

A fine layer of wool-like hair grew from the soles of Zhuo Yuling’s bare feet, anchoring her firmly to the carpet. That was something characteristic of Physique Cultivators.

Once the jolting was over, the couple continued their slow dance in each other’s arms.

The next day.

Room 606, Dormitory Building 6, Yuezhou University, Yuezhou City, Eastern Yue Prefecture[7], Huan[8].

Li Banfeng was in bed, having an afternoon nap and enjoying a sweet dream.

In his dream, he was out at sea on his very own yacht. He had a group of beautiful young women by his side, and they were enjoying the sea breeze together. The latest winner of the Golden Yuan Award for the best actress took his hand and led him into the yacht’s cabin…

Clutching his pillow, Li Banfeng drooled gleefully, about to have the greatest time of his life in his dream.

Then suddenly, a cry rang in his ear.

"Banfeng, save me!"

1. The finger wave is a hairstyle that was popular in China during the 1930s, influenced by the West. ☜

2. Also known as cheongsam. It’s a Chinese dress that was popular in China during the 1920s to 1960s. ☜

3. This symbolises the presence of Western influence and technology in China, as indanthrene dyes were mainly imported from Europe during the 1920s to 1930s and used in modern textile mills and dyehouses. ☜

4. The raw used for this is 老爷, which is a respectful form of address. ☜

5. Also known as a record player. Plays those big vinyl discs. ☜

6. In Mandarin, Li Banfeng’s given name is pronounced the same way as the characters for "half crazy". ☜

7. The character used here is 省, which means province. This "province" is the modern-day version. The "province" in "Puluo Province" is 州, indicating this "province" is the version used in olden times. However, since both 省 and 州 translate to province in English, we have no choice but to differentiate them by function rather than by literal meaning. To avoid confusion, 州 is translated as Province (since it represents a world-level domain), while 省 is translated as Prefecture (since it functions as a smaller regional division within that domain). ☜

8. Just to clarify, this is meant to be a country. Note on Huan: Interestingly, Huan was thought to be a mythical state of Korea, with mentions in historical documents dating back to the 17th century. However, the claims were largely dismissed in the 20th century. ☜

Chapter 2: Banfeng Is Not Crazy

"Banfeng! Save me!"

The voice came out of nowhere. It was loud and urgent, ringing right in Li Banfeng’s ears. He had been sound asleep, but the sudden cry jolted him awake and made him sit straight up.

Who said that?

All the beds were empty. The only other person in the room was He Jiaqing, and he was crouching on the floor as he packed his luggage.

He Jiaqing looked up and asked, "You're awake?"

Li Banfeng stared at him. "Did you just ask me to save you?"

He Jiaqing frowned. "Did sleeping knock your brain loose? Help me pack, please. I am running out of time."

During their four years of university, He Jiaqing had been Li Banfeng's closest friend. And now, he was about to leave. His train was leaving tonight.

Li Banfeng tapped his phone screen and glanced at the time. It was already 3:30 PM. They could grab a meal together, and by the time they were done, it would be time to see him off at the train station.

Li Banfeng jumped down from his bed and helped He Jiaqing pack the last of his things.

"Do you need toothpaste?" he asked.

"Yes!"

With perfect precision, Li Banfeng tossed the toothpaste into He Jiaqing's suitcase.

"What about your meal card?"

"You can have it."

Hearing this, Li Banfeng flicked the meal card into his drawer, landing it cleanly.

As long as it was within five meters, Li Banfeng never missed a throw.

"Want your graduation certificate?"

"Nope."

With perfect aim, Li Banfeng tossed the graduation certificate straight into the trash can three meters away.

This was what He Jiaqing admired about Li Banfeng. He was always so carefree, doing things without overthinking.

Li Banfeng didn't even ask He Jiaqing why he didn't want the graduation certificate. He just tossed it without a second thought. To him, "yes" meant "yes," and "no" meant "no." Li Banfeng would never overcomplicate simple things.

It was because He Jiaqing admired this part of Li Banfeng's personality that Li Banfeng became his only friend throughout all four years of university.

As for Li Banfeng, he only had one friend as well. The reason was his history of mental illness and those who knew about it wouldn't approach him easily.

He opened a cardboard box and found it stuffed with envelopes, each one packed with a stack of photos.

He Jiaqing loved photography and preferred shooting on roll film. Every time he finished a shoot, he would rent a darkroom and develop the photos himself. After four years of university, he had filled an entire box with photos.

Li Banfeng asked, "Still want these photos?"

He Jiaqing paused, feeling a bit hesitant. He then said, "Just pick a few good ones for me. Toss the rest if they're no good."

Li Banfeng picked out a few envelopes and passed them over. "These should be the best."

Without so much as a glance, He Jiaqing packed them into his suitcase.

Li Banfeng picked out a few more envelopes. "These are all photos of your girlfriend. They're all ruined. Better just throw them out."

He Jiaqing froze. "All of them?"

Li Banfeng nodded. "She's not even wearing clothes in them. Definitely ruined."

Without another word, He Jiaqing snatched the photos and shoved them into his suitcase.

At the barbecue joint near the campus gates, He Jiaqing poured Li Banfeng a glass of liquor and said, "There's something I've wanted to ask you for all four years of college… but I could never bring myself to say it."

Li Banfeng shook his head and stated, "I'm not into guys."

Hearing this, He Jiaqing choked on his baijiu[1]. He coughed for a while before saying, "That's not what I was about to ask. I was going to ask you why you were named Banfeng[2]? Is there some special meaning behind it?"

When he heard this question, Li Banfeng's brows relaxed, and the corner of his lips curved up into a smile that appeared a little stiff.

Typically, others would try to keep their distance from Li Banfeng at this very moment, as this expression was usually a sign of Li Banfeng becoming emotionally unstable.

However, He Jiaqing wasn't worried at all. Li Banfeng would also smile like this when he was in a good mood. And right now, he was clearly in a good mood. He jumped into the story with obvious interest.

"It all started with Granny Wu from our orphanage. She took care of the seven of us and was the most cultured person there. She's the one who gave us all our names.

"The seven of us were named Banshan, Banluan, Banling, Banyai, Bangang, Banyue, and Banfeng. You know how the peak is the highest point of a mountain? Because I was the most outstanding of the seven, she gave me the name Banfeng[3]."

He Jiaqing thought for a moment. "I remember Granny Wu. She visited you when you were in the hospital. I don't remember her calling you Li Banfeng, though. She called you Li Bai Sha."

Li Banfeng nodded. "Yeah, that's because she always sent me out to buy her cigarettes. Her favorite brand during that time was Bai Sha, so she started calling me Li Bai Sha.

"Before that, she called me Li Hongmei. Even earlier, I was Li Yingchun. There was a time when things were better, and she could afford a better brand of cigarettes. She would call me Li Furong."

He Jiaqing picked up a skewer of grilled lamb kidney and asked, "So why's your surname Li?"

Li Banfeng replied, "Because the old man who guarded the orphanage was surnamed Li."

He Jiaqing nodded. "Then he must have been a man of virtue and respect."

Li Banfeng shook his head. "He was Granny Wu's husband. He loved smoking a foreign brand of cigarettes, so he used to call me 'Camel Li.'"

As the sky turned a soft shade of evening blue, they walked out of the barbecue place.

Li Banfeng had a napkin in his hand. He wiped his mouth with the napkin, then flicked it cleanly into a trash can four or five meters away.

He Jiaqing exclaimed with admiration, "You have never missed a throw within five meters! We have been friends for four years. Why haven't I learned this skill of yours?"

Li Banfeng looked at the trash can and said, "It's all about mindset. That's all that matters. Just keep it steady…"

Li Banfeng's gaze shifted into the distance. A woman in her early thirties, wearing a gray suit skirt, was approaching them with a smile.

It was Ms. Song.

The two of them quickly greeted her.

Ms. Song was a math teacher at the School of Science. She had chin-length hair and a fair, round face. Behind her round glasses were a pair of intelligent, soulful eyes.

At the sight of the two, her lips lifted into a smile, soft dimples settling into her cheeks. Those dimples had sparked countless fantasies among the male students in the School of Science. Most of those fantasies ended with a tissue. She was as radiant as the morning sun, full of vitality, and exuding a warmth that made people feel safe.

While Li Banfeng was still staring at Ms. Song's back, He Jiaqing lowered his voice and said, "She's got a walnut-shaped birthmark on the left buttcheek."

Li Banfeng froze. "You have seen it?"

He Jiaqing let out a proud burp, heavy with booze. "Did you ever see me fail calculus, linear algebra, and probability? And have you not seen how Ms. Song looks at me in class? You think it's the same as everyone else?"

"I have never paid attention to how she looks at you," Li Banfeng replied, his eyes narrowing as his tone grew serious. "But I remember the birthmark being on her right buttcheek."

He Jiaqing was stunned. "No way it's on the right. You must be remembering it wrong… Wait, what? You have seen it?"

Li Banfeng shook his head again and again. "I'm a man of propriety."

Yet, He Jiaqing absolutely would not yield. He grumbled, "How can you be considered a man of propriety if you have seen Ms. Song's buttcheek?"

Li Banfeng answered with complete seriousness, "I gave it a proper look, so that makes me a man of propriety."

"How could it be on the right…" He Jiaqing muttered, visibly disappointed.

The truth was, he had never actually seen Ms. Song's buttcheek himself. He'd only heard about it from his girlfriend, Lu Xiaolan, who claimed she had caught a glimpse in the locker room.

He Jiaqing looked at Li Banfeng very seriously, feeling very disappointed.

"Did Lu Xiaolan lie to me… or did I just remember it wrong? I think she lied. She's always lying to me."

Li Banfeng and He Jiaqing continued chatting as they walked. To be precise, one was walking while the other was doing all the talking. The two eventually arrived at the girls' dormitory, where they picked up He Jiaqing's girlfriend, Lu Xiaolan.

Lu Xiaolan had her hair tied up in a ponytail, which highlighted her delicate features and brought out a youthful innocence.

She wore a tight-fitting dress that accentuated her petite yet curvy figure.

Lu Xiaolan was widely regarded as the most beautiful girl in the School of Science—a title acknowledged by at least eighty percent of the male students.

Even though the three of them were already acquainted, He Jiaqing couldn't help but show off to Li Banfeng. "Look at my girl… She's like one of those calendar poster beauties[4]?"

Calendar poster beauties? What kind of ancient reference is this?

For some reason, He Jiaqing always had a particular fondness for these old-timey things.

However, Li Banfeng felt nothing for Lu Xiaolan. He couldn't even be bothered to give her a second glance.

"If I must speak from the bottom of my heart, I still prefer Ms. Song's figure," Li Banfeng said seriously as he stared at He Jiaqing.

He Jiaqing replied, "If I must speak from the bottom of my heart, I would tell you that my girl has a heart. When it comes to the assessment of one's heart, one must not judge it solely by its size. It requires a comprehensive comparison…"

The three of them then took a cab together to the East Station.

At the train station, Li Banfeng walked with He Jiaqing to the turnstile. The moment of farewell had arrived.

"Bro, you drank a lot," He Jiaqing said. "Call me when you get back to the dorm."

Li Banfeng chuckled. "Why would I call you? Are you afraid that I'll get lost or something?"

He Jiaqing shook his head. "No, it's not that. I had a lot to drink too. I might doze off on the train. Call me and remind me that I should pee."

"You need a reminder to pee?" Li Banfeng asked, clearly confused.

"What if I wet my pants?" He Jiaqing replied in a dead serious tone.

"You have got your girlfriend with you. You think she would let that happen?"

He Jiaqing shook his head. "She sleeps harder than I do. She sleeps like a pig. Even if the train derailed, she wouldn't wake up!"

Hearing that, Lu Xiaolan shot He Jiaqing an annoyed look and gave him a punch.

Li Banfeng grumbled, "You are about to board, and you are talking about the train derailing? That's bad luck."

They chatted a bit longer until the station broadcast came on: "Train 1160 is now boarding. Please proceed to Platform No. 96 with all your belongings."

He Jiaqing looked at Li Banfeng. "I'm gonna go."

Li Banfeng let out a sniffle, as if about to cry.

He Jiaqing shook his head. "Drop the act. You are really bad at crying. You don't know how to cry."

During their four years of university, Li Banfeng had cried many times. He had used these tears to win the sympathy of the academic counselor and the trust of the academic affairs office. However, He Jiaqing knew they were all fake.

And He Jiaqing was right. Li Banfeng couldn't cry at all.

Even Li Banfeng felt that it wasn't right to fake cry at this moment. However, as He Jiaqing’s best friend, he felt this was a moment where he ought to say a few more words.

After a brief silence, Li Banfeng said only, "Be well."

"You too," He Jiaqing replied. He picked up his suitcase and walked through the turnstile with Lu Xiaolan.

As they walked, Lu Xiaolan asked casually, "Why did you ask Li Banfeng to call you?"

He Jiaqing grinned. "What, are you jealous?"

Lu Xiaolan snorted. "Get lost. If you love him so much, why don't you just marry him?"

He Jiaqing pinched her nose and consoled, "Don't be mad. You know he's got mental health issues. He drank a lot tonight, and I just get worried if he goes back by himself."

Lu Xiaolan hooked her arm through his and whispered, "Honestly, Li Banfeng's not a bad guy. He just creeps me out a little with how unpredictable he is."

He Jiaqing explained with a serious expression, "Banfeng's not crazy. We went to see a psychiatrist at the psychiatry department of Third Hospital. Dr. Wang said he didn't meet the criteria for a mental illness, and they discharged him after just a week."

Lu Xiaolan chuckled in amusement and followed He Jiaqing toward Gate 96.

He Jiaqing repeated once more, "Banfeng really isn't crazy. You'd better remember that."

1. Baijiu (literally “white liquor”)is a Chinese liquor usually distilled from fermented sorghum. It is a colorless, clear liquid typically coming in between 35% and 60% alcohol by volume. ☜

2. His name is 伴峰 (Bànfēng), which means "companion of the peak" or "accompanying the mountain peak." However, it sounds identical to 半疯 (Bànfēng), which means half crazy. ☜

3. Banshan (伴山) means "Companion of the mountain," Banluan (伴峦) means "Companion of the mountain range," Banling (伴岭) means "Companion of the mountain ridge," Banyai (伴崖) means "Companion of the cliff," Bangang (伴岗) means "Companion of the hillock or mound," Banyue (伴岳) means "Companion of the great mountain," and Banfeng (伴峰) means "Companion of the peak or highest point of a mountain." ☜

4. Explained in thoughts. ☜

Chapter 3: Bro, Save Me

After leaving the train station, Li Banfeng went to board a subway train.

By taxi, the trip to the train station from the university had only taken half an hour. However, Li Banfeng took over an hour to get back by subway.

He lived in Dormitory Building 6, which was a fair walk from the university entrance. When he finally got back to his dormitory building, the old man guarding the entrance looked at him in surprise.

The old man raised his eyebrows and asked, "You haven't left yet?"

Li Banfeng lifted the corners of his mouth and gave the old man a wooden smile before heading straight upstairs.

The university hasn't even issued a notice to drive out the remaining students. What is it to you whether I stay or leave?

The residents of Dormitory Building 6 had just graduated. It appeared most of them had already left, leaving the building flooded with silence.

Li Banfeng climbed up to the sixth floor and opened the door to his room, only to find it was very dark inside. According to the rules, lights-out wasn't until 11 PM. It wasn't even 9 PM yet, but that old man had already cut the power.

Dorm Gramps sure is enthusiastic about saving electricity for the university.

Li Banfeng's roommates were all gone. Those who had found jobs had left to start work, and those who were still jobless had gone home to wait until they found employment. He Jiaqing hadn't found a job, so he and his girlfriend went home. Now, only Li Banfeng remained in the dormitory.

Li Banfeng couldn't get a job because he had a history of mental illness. However, unlike his peers, he did not have a home he could return to. It wasn't like he could move back into the orphanage where he had grown up.

How am I supposed to live from now on? Should I go back to doing hard labor on a construction site?

Then Li Banfeng thought about the bright prospects he had for his future, and his furrowed brows relaxed. The corners of his lips raised into a small smile.

This time, he was actually smiling because he was emotionally unstable, but anyone would have had a hard time staying calm if they had been in his shoes.

There's another job fair tomorrow. I'll go try my luck again.

Tonight, I've got the room all to myself. At least I'll get a good night's sleep.

Li Banfeng went to the communal bathroom and had a cold shower. Just as he was about to get into bed and play a game on his cell phone, he remembered something. He had to give He Jiaqing a call.

What if he really wets his pants? And what if he catches a chill because of that and then comes down with a cold? That would be terrible.

So, Li Banfeng made the call. It rang for a long time, but no one picked up.

It seems he really did fall asleep.

Li Banfeng called again. After another long wait, the call was finally answered.

—Banfeng…

He Jiaqing's voice was deep and husky, like he still wasn't fully awake.

"Jiaqing, time to get up and pee!"

He Jiaqing sounded utterly confused.

—Pee? Pee where? Where am I…?

Where am I? You slept really peacefully, huh!

Li Banfeng didn't say anything else; he just hung up.

In the dark room, he groped his way toward the bunk bed and climbed up the ladder to his bed. However, just as he reached the top, he noticed his blanket wasn't lying flat on his bed. Instead, it looked like it was wrapped around a person.

There's someone under my blanket…? Is there really someone there?

Li Banfeng lifted the blanket.

There really is someone there!

The person was lying completely silent with their back to Li Banfeng.

Who the heck is sleeping in my bed?

Then Li Banfeng caught a strong whiff of alcohol lingering on the person's clothes, suggesting they had been drinking heavily.

Even if you're wasted, you can't just crash on someone else's bed!

"Get up! Who are you?? Get the heck up!"

Li Banfeng pushed the person, but they didn't react at all.

The window curtains weren't drawn, so the moonlight shone through, dimly illuminating the person lying on Li Banfeng's bed. Despite only seeing a faint outline of the mysterious person, Li Banfeng suddenly realized they looked rather familiar.

Li Banfeng grabbed the person's shoulder, making them lie on their back instead. But the moment he saw the person's face, he nearly fell off the ladder.

Jiaqing!?

Just an hour and a half ago, Li Banfeng had sent He Jiaqing off at the train station.

And now he's lying in my bed!

"Jiaqing, what's wrong? Jiaqing, wake up."

Li Banfeng shook He Jiaqing a few times, but to no avail. Then he suddenly pulled his hand back.

Hang on, something's not right! I just talked to Jiaqing on the phone. Isn't he on the train right now? Isn't he supposed to be going to pee? If the person sleeping here is Jiaqing, then who picked up the phone?

Li Banfeng broke out in goosebumps. The hot, stuffy room suddenly felt rather chilly.

Is this man really Jiaqing?

He turned on his phone's flashlight and shone it at the man's face for quite a while.

It really is him!

He Jiaqing was Li Banfeng's closest friend at university. There was no way Li Banfeng would mistake someone else for him.

Bam! Thud!

Li Banfeng fell off the ladder, knocking over the trash can on the floor. He calmly got to his feet, set the trash can upright, and quickly exited the room.

After closing the door behind him, he leaned against the corridor wall and stood there in silence.

It appeared the building's voltage supply was quite unstable. The dim yellow lights in the corridor flickered constantly.

Li Banfeng forced himself to calm down. He took out his phone and rang He Jiaqing's number again. The suffocating silence on the line lasted for more than ten seconds, then a woman's voice cut through.

—Sorry, the number you have dialed is currently unavailable.

Li Banfeng called the number several more times, but no one picked up.

Should I try calling Lu Xiaolan?

However, Li Banfeng didn't have Lu Xiaolan's number. They had never really spent much time together after all.

The corridor was completely silent. It seemed like all the residents of this floor had already left.

Li Banfeng turned back to look at his room door, trying to figure out what he should do next.

Is the person lying in my bed really Jiaqing?

Moments ago, Li Banfeng had been perfectly certain it was He Jiaqing. Now, he wasn't so sure.

Should I go back and check again?

No, that's nuts. If I'm going back in there, I should first find someone from another floor to help me! But where am I going to find anyone right now?

Well, worst-case scenario, I could call Dorm Gramps up here. But what's the point of that? He won't be able to help. He'd only get in the way. In that case, I might as well just do it alone!

Li Banfeng was arguing with himself. Any normal person wouldn't dare go back in there alone.

However, Li Banfeng wasn't normal. He had been assessed at the hospital. He wasn't clinically insane, but his mental state wasn't exactly normal either.

Li Banfeng's lips lifted slightly into a small smile.

Despite his unstable emotions, he was now pretty cheerful. This was because Li Banfeng had a special way of regaining his composure. He came up with a perfectly reasonable explanation for the situation in his room.

A hallucination. It has to be a hallucination!

I have a mental disorder. It makes total sense for me to hallucinate from time to time.

I'll walk back into that room right now. As long as I stay aware that it's not real, I definitely won't see Jiaqing in there again.

Li Banfeng opened the door with a smile and stepped back inside.

A hallucination. It's just a hallucination.

Yet, the closer Li Banfeng got to the bunk bed, the stronger the smell of alcohol became, as if warning him that this was no hallucination.

Once again, Li Banfeng climbed the ladder, raised his phone, and shone its flashlight at his bed. He Jiaqing was still lying there. His eyes were shut tight, and his face was deathly pale.

Li Banfeng patted He Jiaqing's face. It was warm. He then placed his finger under He Jiaqing's nose to check if he was breathing. He was. However, his breathing was very weak and irregular. Li Banfeng climbed down the ladder and decided to call for an ambulance.

After informing the emergency services of the situation and his location, Li Banfeng ended the call and waited quietly for the ambulance to arrive. As he stood beside the bunk bed, he looked up at He Jiaqing—his dear friend who had somehow appeared out of nowhere and was now lying unconscious in his bed.

An uneasy silence settled over the room.

Ka-da-da~

It was broken by a notification sound from Li Banfeng's phone. He had received a new text message. It was from He Jiaqing.

Li Banfeng had been watching He Jiaqing this whole time. He was sure He Jiaqing couldn't possibly have sent it. Yet, he had indeed received a text message from He Jiaqing.

It contained only three words: Banfeng, save me!

Chapter 4: He Jiaqing's Mouth

Li Banfeng sat in the hospital room with his eyes fixed on He Jiaqing, who lay motionless in the hospital bed.

After two full days of emergency treatment, the doctors managed to save He Jiaqing's life. However, he had slipped into a deep coma, and no one knew when he would wake up.

The cause of the coma remained a mystery. The hospital hadn't proposed a treatment plan yet. According to them, the next step in treatment would depend not only on He Jiaqing's condition but also on how much money he and Li Banfeng had left.

Li Banfeng had poured all the money he had reserved for his living expenses into He Jiaqing's medical bills. A portion of the bills was covered by insurance, but there was still so much left to pay that his contribution was like a drop in the ocean. It wasn't nearly enough.

He Jiaqing had some savings on his card, but Li Banfeng couldn't withdraw any of it because he didn't know the PIN. He thought of reaching out to He Jiaqing's family, but even though the two of them were close, he didn't have any of their contact information. He didn't even have Lu Xiaolan's number.

If this went on, Li Banfeng wouldn't even have money for his next meal. They might not be able to keep He Jiaqing on oxygen either.

Fortunately, things took a turn on the third day. A man named He Haisheng appeared and said he was He Jiaqing's third uncle. Li Banfeng had never met him before, but that didn't matter because he paid off the medical bills upfront.

Someone who took the initiative to pay was most likely not a scammer—assuming, of course, that the payment really went through.

"You're Li Banfeng, right? The university said you've been taking care of Jiaqing these past few days. It must've been tough on you," He Haisheng said to Li Banfeng as he peeled an apple and handed it to him.

Li Banfeng took a bite of the apple and began to explain the situation. "Well, here's what happened—"

However, He Haisheng cut him off.

"We'll talk about that later." He Haisheng pulled out his wallet and handed Li Banfeng a stack of cash. "This is just a small token of appreciation. I hope it's not too little."

"I'm not short on money. Jiaqing needs it more right now," Li Banfeng replied.

Actually, he really needed the money. He didn't even have enough for his next meal. Nevertheless, he didn't want to accept it.

He Haisheng shoved the money firmly into Li Banfeng's coat pocket. "This is just a token of appreciation. I have no other intentions. If you don't take it, I'll think you're saying it's not enough!"

He Haisheng then sat in silence for a moment before letting out a sigh. "The school called home and said that something unfortunate happened to Jiaqing. I thought Second Brother and his wife would come right away, but the two of them turned out to be completely unreliable. It's my fault for showing up late and putting you through so much. Banfeng, go home and get some rest. I'll take care of things here."

With that, Li Banfeng walked out of the hospital room. He hadn't really slept in the past three days and was indeed tired. As he made his way down the corridor, his legs felt weak and unsteady.

Just as he was almost at the elevator, his phone suddenly buzzed.

Ka-da-da~

It was a message, and the sender was He Jiaqing.

Li Banfeng frowned at once.

The message was pretty much the same as before. It read "Bro, save me."

Li Banfeng quickly typed a reply.

Li Banfeng: Who are you?

He Jiaqing: I'm He Jiaqing.

Li Banfeng: Where did you get this phone?

He had seen He Jiaqing off at the train station, watched him board the train, and then somehow found him back in his own bed. Before that, he had called He Jiaqing. After that, he received a text from him.

The whole situation was so bizarre that Li Banfeng couldn't make any sense of it. But no matter how bizarre the events were, the outcome was the same.

The fact was that He Jiaqing was lying in a hospital bed. He had no ID or phone on him, but Li Banfeng was absolutely sure. That was definitely He Jiaqing, and there was no way he could be mistaken.

That raised the question: Who had answered the phone that night, and who was texting him just now?

The only explanation that made sense was that He Jiaqing's phone had been lost. Someone must have picked it up, answered the call that night while imitating He Jiaqing's voice, and fooled Li Banfeng. And now, that same person was texting him again.

But why would someone who picked up the phone still try to contact me? Isn't this classic phone scam behavior? I should report this to the police.

Li Banfeng was convinced that He Jiaqing's coma had something to do with this person.

There was no reply for a long time.

Then, the elevator arrived.

Just as Li Banfeng put away his phone and was about to head downstairs, a middle-aged man stepped in front of him. He had a broad forehead, long cheeks, and a thin frame.

The man smiled and pulled out an ID from inside his jacket. "I am with the police. You're Li Banfeng, right? I have a few questions about He Jiaqing."

He looked to be in his fifties and spoke in a raspy voice, like he had phlegm caught in his throat.

I was just about to report this, and now the police show up?

Li Banfeng really wanted to get a better look at the ID, but the man slipped it back into his jacket and leaned over to whisper something to a nearby doctor in a white coat.

The doctor seemed to know the man well. He led both of them into an empty hospital room. After closing the door behind them, he left, leaving just Li Banfeng and the middle-aged man inside. They sat facing each other on two hospital beds placed side by side.

"Don't be nervous," the man said with a friendly smile. "My surname's Chen. Just call me Old Chen. I only have a few questions. You were the one who brought He Jiaqing to the hospital, right?"

"Yes, I did," Li Banfeng replied with a nod.

"How did you realize something was wrong with him?"

"He was lying in my bed. His complexion and breathing didn't look right, so I called emergency services right away."

Old Chen nodded and gave Li Banfeng a quick glance from head to toe. There was a slight change in his expression as he studied his face. For some reason, he was not staring at Li Banfeng's eyes, but focusing on his forehead.

"I heard you two were drinking together that day?"

Li Banfeng nodded. "Yes! He was going back home, so I saw him off."

"You saw him off?"

"Yes. I took him to the train station, then took the subway back on my own. But when I got back to the dorm, I found him asleep in my bed."

What Li Banfeng said didn't make any sense. If He Jiaqing had gone to the train station, how could he have ended up back in the dorm? And in Li Banfeng's bed, no less?

Yet, Old Chen didn't seem the least bit surprised. He seemed relaxed and still kept his eyes fixed on Li Banfeng's forehead.

"Did you and He Jiaqing have any conflicts?"

Conflicts? Li Banfeng gave it some thought. I've looked through some of his girlfriend's pictures. Does this count as conflict?

Regardless, it was clear that wasn't the point. The real issue was the implication behind the question.

Li Banfeng looked at Old Chen calmly and said, "Are you suspecting that I did something to He Jiaqing?

Old Chen shook his head. "I wouldn't say I suspect you. It was just a question. If I really suspected you, we wouldn't be having this chat here. I heard you have some kind of mental condition?"

Li Banfeng nodded. "I do have a minor mental health issue, but I'm not crazy. You're welcome to check my medical records. I saw He Jiaqing and Lu Xiaolan off at the train station. They were going home together. You can confirm that with Lu Xiaolan."

"Lu Xiaolan…" Old Chen muttered. He took out a cigarette, glanced at the hospital room, decided it wasn't the right place to smoke, and put it back in the pack.

Old Chen continued questioning. "And who exactly is this Lu Xiaolan you mentioned?"

"She's He Jiaqing's girlfriend. We're from the same university," Li Banfeng replied. "I brought them both to the train station, and she got on the train with him. She can vouch for me."

"If they both got on the train, then how did He Jiaqing end up back at your dorm?"

"I don't know," Li Banfeng answered honestly. "You should ask Lu Xiaolan. They were together the whole time."

"Do you have her contact information?"

"I don't, but the school should have it. You should also be able to find her through the train ticket records."

"Which train were they on?"

"How would I know…" Li Banfeng muttered. He paused for a moment, then suddenly, a string of numbers came to mind.

Train 1160.

Recalling the voice of the train station announcer, Li Banfeng said, "Train 1160. They were on train 1160."

Old Chen rubbed his fingers against his upper lip. His thoughts had hit a dead end, and he wanted a cigarette. "Sounds like Lu Xiaolan won't be easy to find either."

Li Banfeng didn't understand. "Why not?"

Still rubbing the stubble above his lip, Old Chen said slowly, "The night this horrendous incident happened to He Jiaqing, train 1160 derailed and fell off a cliff. So far, no survivors have been found."

Li Banfeng blinked. It felt like thunder was rolling through his mind, echoing with He Jiaqing's voice. "She sleeps harder than I do. She sleeps like a pig. Even if the train derailed, she wouldn't wake up!"

And now the train indeed derailed, and Lu Xiaolan might really be dead.

Does He Jiaqing's mouth have some kind of divine power? Like he can speak things into existence? Or had he already known something would happen?

He said Lu Xiaolan had lied to him. Maybe it wasn't just about Ms. Song's buttcheeks after all.

Why exactly did he ask me to call him once I got back to the dorm?

Old Chen continued staring at Li Banfeng's forehead as he asked softly, "What are you thinking about?"

Li Banfeng shook his head. "Nothing. Just worried about Lu Xiaolan."

Old Chen seemed intrigued. "What was your relationship with Lu Xiaolan like? Was there something going on between the three of you?"

"Are you trying to say it was some kind of love triangle?" Li Banfeng asked as the corner of his mouth curled into a slight smile. He was feeling a little angry.

Chapter 5: They're All Liars

Old Chen suspected that Li Banfeng, He Jiaqing, and Lu Xiaolan were involved in a love triangle. That made Li Banfeng a little angry. No, he wasn't just a little angry; he was furious. Nevertheless, he restrained himself. He suppressed his rage and even put on a small smile.

Old Chen was deliberately provoking Li Banfeng, thinking he could get the information he wanted this way.

He might even try pushing me to have a psychotic episode so that he can treat me as a mentally unstable suspect and arrest me.

Enunciating each word very clearly, Li Banfeng stated flatly, "Lu Xiaolan and I aren't even friends, let alone anything more. You can ask my university roommates or other classmates to verify that."

Old Chen asked, "Is there really nothing between you two?"

Li Banfeng replied calmly, "If there was something between us, I would tell you there is. But there's nothing, so I'm telling you there's nothing. If you don't believe me, just go investigate. It's that simple. Don't make this more complicated than it needs to be."

It really was that simple.

Old Chen nodded. "I believe you."

Li Banfeng continued, "It's true I was the one who brought He Jiaqing to the hospital, but you can't possibly pin me as the culprit because of that, can you? His third uncle is here too. Why don't you call him over? Let's clear everything up together."

"I'm not treating you as the culprit. If I were, I'd have taken you back to the station already." Old Chen took out a pack of cigarettes from his chest pocket but resisted the urge to light one. "As for He Jiaqing's third uncle, you're talking about He Haisheng, right? He was the one who called the police, brought me to the hospital, and told me to look for you."

Li Banfeng was speechless. Jiaqing's third uncle is pretty sinister.

Old Chen put his pack of cigarettes back into his chest pocket. "Before He Jiaqing's incident, he had a meal with you and some drinks. After a round of drinks, he passed out. You said you took him to the train station. We'll check the surveillance footage there. I believe you're telling the truth, and I hope this matter has nothing to do with you. But let me remind you that things aren't looking good for you right now. We'll be investigating the matter, and you should do your best to cooperate with us. Additionally, try not to leave Yuezhou."

Ka-da-da~

It was Li Banfeng's phone.

The corner of Li Banfeng's eye twitched. It's probably a message from He Jiaqing.

He wondered whether he should tell this police officer about it. Li Banfeng felt very conflicted.

"Did someone contact you?" Old Chen gave a knowing smile, as if he had seen through Li Banfeng's hesitation. "There are things you might not want to tell me right now, and that's fine. When you're ready to talk, come find me."

Old Chen walked out of the ward with Li Banfeng. When they reached the hospital entrance, the two men parted ways.

Old Chen got into a black sedan. There were two passengers inside.

Sitting in the back seat was a woman in her early twenties, dressed in office attire. Brushing aside the fringe of her short bob, she straightened her rimless glasses, crossed her legs neatly, and turned slightly toward Old Chen. Only a third of her perfectly round, peach-shaped bottom was resting on the seat.

This young woman was You Xuehan, codename Tangyuan[1]. She was their team's secretary.

Holding an elaborately decorated notebook, You Xuehan asked respectfully, "Captain Chen, how did it go?"

Old Chen lit a cigarette and took a deep drag. The crow's feet at the corners of his eyes and the deep smile lines on his face smoothed out at the same time, like the tension was melting away from his body.

"That kid's got good mental fortitude; he knows how to hold back his emotions. But I don't think he's a Dark Ability User. He's probably just a Bleater. But he definitely knows something. Has anyone contacted him by phone in the past few days regarding what happened with He Jiaqing?"

You Xuehan opened up Li Banfeng's file. "I just checked his phone records. He's only made one call, and it was to emergency services. The university called him twice. There were no other calls. He didn't get any text messages either—not even one."

"I'm guessing it's not that there's none; it's that we just can't see it in his phone records," Old Chen said, exhaling a puff of smoke. "He tensed up the moment he heard his phone ring with a new message. Someone's contacting him, but he doesn't want anyone else to know."

The man in the front passenger seat turned toward Old Chen and offered a suggestion. "If he's suspicious, why not just arrest him?"

The man appeared to be around thirty. He had thick brows, big eyes, contoured cheekbones, and a sharp jawline. He was fairly handsome. However, his hair was thinning, so he had it slicked back with shiny hair grease in an extremely neat hairstyle.

"Arrest him?" Old Chen frowned as he turned to look at the man. "On what grounds?"

The man didn't seem to realize there was a major problem with his suggestion. "Since he has a motive, it would be best to keep him in custody."

Old Chen's tone grew heavier. "What motive does he have?"

The man remained oblivious. "If this has nothing to do with him, then why did he take He Jiaqing to the hospital?"

Old Chen snapped and said furiously, "What the fuck is this nonsense that you're spouting?!"

The man didn't respond. He simply turned away and gazed out the car window.

His name was Xiao Zhenggong, but the members of his team called him Deputy Xiao.

He kept his eyes on the scenery outside, the disdain on his face sending a clear message. Xiao Zhenggong wasn't afraid of Old Chen, nor did he think he had done anything wrong. He just didn't want to waste words arguing with the old guy.

Even though Xiao Zhenggong had his back to Old Chen, Old Chen could see his face clearly in the side mirror.

Old Chen let out a sigh. "From the very day you joined the bureau, I've been telling you the same thing. Complete your tasks, follow the rules, and don't forget your damn conscience. And yet, even after all this time, you still don't fucking get it!"

You Xuehan pursed her lips. The tense atmosphere made her uncomfortable, so she quickly changed the subject. "I've done a rudimentary check of Li Banfeng's background. He was abandoned as a baby and grew up in an orphanage. He has no criminal record, but he has been investigated a few times for acting in self-defense."

"Self-defense?" Old Chen's interest was piqued. He took the file from her. "Took on three people, left two seriously injured and one with minor injuries. Took on five people, left one seriously injured and four with minor injuries… He's that good in a fight? Sure didn't look it. He's got a pretty average build!"

He flipped through Li Banfeng's file seriously. Halfway through, he laughed. "This kid… he's really nuts."

You Xuehan asked, "Captain Chen, do we still need to watch Li Banfeng?"

Old Chen thought about it for a moment and said, "Have Lightbulb keep an eye on him."

You Xuehan reminded him, "Lightbulb's only been on the job for a month. It would be better to let Blockhead handle it."

Old Chen took another deep drag of his cigarette. "There's no need to send Blockhead to watch a Bleater. It's a good chance for Lightbulb to get some practice. Send Blockhead to watch He Haisheng instead. Now that guy won't be easy to deal with."

After boarding a subway train, Li Banfeng took out his phone. Just as he had expected, he had gotten a new text message. However, it wasn't from He Jiaqing.

Old Chen: Hello, this is Old Chen.

Old Chen? He’s the one who messaged me? That old guy… was he testing me earlier? What's the point of sending this message? Is it just to test me?

Oh, I guess not. By sending this message, he gave me his number. He really does want me to contact him of my own accord.

Li Banfeng had no intention of contacting Old Chen, but there was one thing he was very curious about. During their conversation in the ward, Li Banfeng did not see Old Chen take out his phone.

So how had he sent the text message? Had someone helped him send the text message in secret? Or did this Old Chen have some sort of special ability?

And what about He Jiaqing? He was lying unconscious in the hospital. If he really was sending Li Banfeng text messages, then how was he doing it?

This wasn't in line with the world he knew.

But so what if it's different? What's that got to do with me? Jiaqing is now under the care of his third uncle, so I don't need to get involved anymore. I'll just stick to moving bricks and live a quiet and peaceful life. None of this has anything to do with me anymore.

Li Banfeng wanted to forget everything as quickly as possible. He unlocked his phone and began scrolling aimlessly through news articles.

This was the headline of the first one.

"8.83 Seconds—Renowned Sprinter Qiao Yuesheng Makes a New 100m World Record Again! What He Said in the Interview Will Shock You!"

What did he say?

Li Banfeng opened up the article.

The Diamond League has just concluded. Renowned sprinter Qiao Yuesheng has made waves once again with a record-breaking 8.83 seconds, breaking the world record he set just last month. Who could have imagined that this legendary athlete once nearly gave up on his dream due to financial hardship? Born into a humble and ordinary farming family…

The article was very long, detailing Qiao Yuesheng's life from his birth all the way to his championship win at the Diamond League. At the very end of the article, Li Banfeng finally found what Qiao Yuesheng said in the interview: "Dreams have nothing to do with skin color."

It was a very inspirational quote. However, Li Banfeng remembered that this was a famous quote from the previous year, rather than this year. Despite being an Asian, Qiao Yuesheng had been dominating the 100-meter track since then, and now, he had even shattered the nine-second world record.

Li Banfeng opened up the next article.

"Famous Celebrity Chu Zikai Reveals the Secrets of the Entertainment Industry."

Ten years ago, Chu Zikai took the Chinese music scene by storm with his hit single "I Am Your Guokui"[2]. His pure and sincere singing voice has touched countless fans.

This musical prodigy made shocking revelations to the public, opening the eyes of countless people to the reality of the entertainment industry.

Now thirty-one, Chu Zikai was born into a regular working-class family and has loved music since childhood…

Li Banfeng thought, This one outlines his life from birth too.

He couldn't be bothered reading it. He skipped to the next article.

"Shocking News! The Sun Is Not an Only Child. Astronomers Discover It Might Have a Younger Brother!"

Recently, astronomers made a shocking discovery. Our sun may not be an only child. It might have an undiscovered companion star.

As the master of the solar system and the star upon which all life depends, the sun was born 4.6 billion years ago…

Li Banfeng was annoyed. Even the sun has to have its story start from its birth. All these clickbait writers must have had the same damn teacher!

His phone's notification sound rang again.

Ka-da-da!

He received a new text message. This one was from He Jiaqing.

He Jiaqing: Bro, don't trust them. They're all liars.

Li Banfeng: Who do you mean by "they"?

He Jiaqing: Third Uncle, the cops, even the He Jiaqing you saw—every one of them is a liar.

Li Banfeng put away his phone. All of a sudden, the subway train felt much colder than usual.

1. Tangyuan is a kind of food. It's boiled or deep-fried balls of glutinous rice flour, usually eaten during the Lantern Festival. ☜

2. Guokui is a kind of Chinese flatbread, and there are various regional versions. ☜

Chapter 6: Eliminate Me?

Lying on the dormitory bed, Li Banfeng silently stared at the ceiling.

The bed was set high off the ground, leaving him uncomfortably close to the ceiling above. Up above, patches of mold spotted the surface like clusters of eyes, each one fixed on him in silent scrutiny.

And indeed, there were eyes watching his every move.

Ka-da-da!

His phone buzzed again with another message.

Who keeps messaging me?

Whoever it is… they picked up He Jiaqing's phone. They knew I met with his Third Uncle. They knew I talked to the police. They even knew he was lying in a hospital bed.

Who could it be? My professor? One of those doctors? That man, Old Chen, who claimed to be a cop?

Or maybe… Dorm Gramps, the old man who lives downstairs?

The phone wouldn't stop buzzing. Li Banfeng picked it up and glanced at the screen. Dozens of messages had come in, all from He Jiaqing's number.

He Jiaqing: Banfeng, in all four years of college, you were my only brother!

He Jiaqing: When there was only one drink left, we shared it. You took a sip, then I did.

He Jiaqing: When there was only one cigarette left, we split it. You smoked half, I finished the rest.

He Jiaqing: The first time I took my girl out to dinner, you were right there with me. The first time I tried to sleep with her, we were halfway through when you called. Because of that one phone call, I ditched everything and spent the whole night with you at the internet cafe.

He Jiaqing: When you got sick, I went with you to see the doctor. When you ran out of money, I covered your expenses. And when I ran out too, I hauled stones at a construction site just to keep paying the bills until you were discharged.

He Jiaqing: During an exam last year, I tried to sneak you the answers. I ended up failing the class and almost got put on academic probation. All four years of university, you were the only one I ever called a brother. The only one!

A dozen or so messages confirmed one thing: the sender really was He Jiaqing. Some of the memories mentioned were things only the two of them could have known.

The last message read, Bro, save me. Only you can save me.

Li Banfeng took a deep breath and called. He wanted to confirm it was really He Jiaqing's voice. But the call didn't go through, so he sent a message instead.

Li Banfeng: How did you get back from the station? And why did you end up on my bed?

He Jiaqing: That. Is. Not. Me.

Li Banfeng: If it isn't you, then who is it? And where are you now? Why were you able to see me?

Li Banfeng had a thousand more questions for He Jiaqing, but for now, that was all he could manage.

Ka-da-da!

He Jiaqing: I really want to answer you, but there's no time. The train is about to depart. If you don't come save me today, who knows when the next one will come. You may never see me again.

Li Banfeng rolled out of bed and quietly looked out the window, the words echoing in his mind.

The train is about to leave? Go save you today…

He's the only friend I've made in all four years of university. Come to think of it, how many friends have I really had since I was born? Probably just this one.

Outside Dormitory Building 6, the air felt cold and still. The clotheslines hung bare, with nothing left to sway in the wind. Yet, beneath the large willow tree out front, someone had been standing there all along.

He was a slim young man, around the same age as Li Banfeng. His eyes were large and unusually bright, almost like glowing lightbulbs. Even from the third floor, Li Banfeng could see them clearly.

The young man looked like he was waiting for someone, but aside from Li Banfeng and Dorm Gramps, the entire dormitory was empty. If he wasn't waiting for Dorm Gramps, then he was definitely here for Li Banfeng.

Li Banfeng pulled out his phone and sent a message to He Jiaqing.

Li Banfeng: Someone's watching me.

He Jiaqing: It must be someone from the Dark Star Bureau.

Li Banfeng: What's the Dark Star Bureau?

He Jiaqing: I really can't explain right now. The Dark Star Bureau isn't something you can mention casually. They can sense it. Bro, you have to save me. If you don't come soon, it'll be too late.

Li Banfeng sat in his chair and fell into deep thought. He didn't move for over ten minutes. During this time, Dorm Gramps came upstairs and glanced at him. "Why are you still here?"

Li Banfeng didn't respond, because he didn't even see Dorm Gramps. His thoughts had sunk deep, circling through every memory from the past four years with He Jiaqing.

Yes. He really is the only one I can call brother.

After a long silence, Li Banfeng typed out a long message.

Li Banfeng: Bro, through all four years of university, you are the one that I can acknowledge as my brother. If there was one drink left, we'd each take a sip. One cigarette left, we'd split it. If you ever needed money for treatment, I'd haul bricks at a construction site without a second thought. But this time… I can't do it. I don't want to be involved in this.

Li Banfeng set his phone down. His chest felt tight.

He didn't want to get involved because none of this made sense. This defied everything he knew about the world.

The timing and location of He Jiaqing's appearance didn't make any sense. Whether it was He Jiaqing lying in the hospital bed or the one texting him, none of it added up.

As far as he knew, He Jiaqing had never shown any signs of supernatural ability in the past four years. If he had, he wouldn't have been caught cheating on exams.

Li Banfeng prided himself on being able to handle a lot, but something like this could make a normal person crazy.

And besides... I already am crazy, he muttered inwardly.

Ka-da-da!

After a long silence, another message from He Jiaqing finally came in. The message had only one word.

He Jiaqing: Okay.

Li Banfeng lay back down on his bed, staring blankly at the ceiling. He hadn't slept well in three days.

Maybe a short nap would make everything go away, he thought. Jiaqing, I have done all I could for you.

Ka-da-da!

He then got another message from Jiaqing.

He Jiaqing: Take care of yourself.

Take care of yourself… The words echoed in Banfeng's mind. They sounded so familiar.

Suddenly, he realized that this was the last thing he had said to He Jiaqing. It was the final thing he had ever said to him in person.

Take care… I told him to take care, but he's clearly not okay. Not doing well means not doing well. It's really that simple.

Li Banfeng sat up and sent another message.

Li Banfeng: How can I save you?

He Jiaqing: Banfeng, my brother, I'm begging you. Please get something for me and bring it to my house.

Li Banfeng couldn't see He Jiaqing's face, but he could tell from the tone of the message that He Jiaqing was very agitated and emotional.

Li Banfeng: What is it?

He sent the message and looked toward He Jiaqing's locker. As far as he remembered, it had been empty for a long time.

He Jiaqing: It's not in the dorm. It's at the campus convenience store.

Li Banfeng walked to the window and looked outside. He glanced first at the man still crouched beneath the willow tree, then at the campus store in the distance.

Li Banfeng: When you say bring it to your house... you don't mean your hometown, right?

He Jiaqing: Yes, my hometown. It's not far. I already bought you a ticket. The train leaves this afternoon. Please, go to East Station now. I'm begging you!

Li Banfeng took a deep breath and jumped off the bed. He headed to the sink and washed his face, letting the cold water wash away some of the exhaustion that had built up over the past few days.

After drying off, he changed into a short-sleeved T-shirt, loose sports pants, and a pair of sneakers.

Since he was going on a trip, he needed to pack properly. He grabbed his wallet, slipped his ID inside, and took the two thousand yuan that He Jiaqing's "Third Uncle" had given him.

Then he pulled a backpack out of the closet, the same one He Jiaqing had bought from a street stall. It cost thirty yuan for one or fifty for two. He Jiaqing had bought the pair and given one to Li Banfeng.

Li Banfeng packed a change of clothes and got ready to leave.

Just before stepping out, he glanced out the window again. The man with wide lightbulb eyes was still there.

Li Banfeng: Since the guy watching me isn't a cop, I'm guessing a bit of physical contact won't be an issue, right?

Ka-da-da!

He Jiaqing: Don't be rash.

Li Banfeng: Relax. I know my limits. I'll keep it within the bounds of self-defense.

He Jiaqing: That's not the point. He's a Dark Ability User. You can't beat him. You need to find a way to lose him.

Li Banfeng: What's a Dark Ability User?

He Jiaqing: I can't explain that to you yet.

Li Banfeng: Once I get to your place, you better explain everything, down to the last detail.

With that, Li Banfeng left the dorm.

The man with wide lightbulb eyes didn't move right away. He just stood there, watching from a distance.

It wasn't until Li Banfeng had walked a few dozen meters that the man quietly started to follow him. Li Banfeng acted as if nothing was out of the ordinary. He walked into the campus convenience store like he usually did when grabbing a bag of spicy sticks.

The man trailing him was named Dong Xiaoning. His codename was Lightbulb. He had only joined the Dark Star Bureau last month and still lacked professionalism in many aspects of the job. For example, he was not very professional at tailing someone, and he was painfully aware of that.

At this moment, he stood outside the convenience store, feeling torn. He didn't know whether to go in or not. The store was small. If he went in, Li Banfeng would spot him right away. But if he stayed outside, he might lose track of him.

Ka-da-da!

Li Banfeng received a message from He Jiaqing.

He Jiaqing: The item is in a locker at the convenience store.

Li Banfeng walked up to the lockers. The convenience store only had face-recognition lockers. There was no other way to open them.

Li Banfeng: How am I supposed to unlock it? Use a photo of you?

He Jiaqing: No need. Just take it.

Just... take it?

He pressed the retrieval button. The screen lit up and scanned his face.

What's the point? I'm not the one who stored it… Li Banfeng thought.

Clack!

Locker number six popped open. Li Banfeng stood there stunned, unable to make sense of it. What is going on? It scanned my face and still gave me He Jiaqing's stuff? What's the logic behind this?

Inside was a package, tightly wrapped in layers of tape.

It wasn't large, but it was surprisingly heavy. He shoved it into his backpack and was about to leave when he spotted Lightbulb at the entrance, standing there with a phone to his ear.

Li Banfeng: The guy tailing me is on the phone. Can you hear what he's saying?

If he was calling for backup, Li Banfeng had to figure out a way to handle it.

He Jiaqing: I can hear a bit, but it's too fuzzy. I can't do it alone. We need to work together. Can you get a little closer?

Li Banfeng grabbed a shopping cart and loitered near the entrance, pushing it casually as if he were just passing the time.

Lightbulb's voice grew louder. Gradually, Li Banfeng could hear him more clearly.

It seemed like He Jiaqing had lent him some of his supernatural ability. Li Banfeng could now hear Lightbulb clearly, and even the voice on the other end of the call came through without trouble.

"Deputy Xiao, Li Banfeng just took something from a locker in the convenience store. I'm not sure what it is yet."

On the other end of the line, Xiao Zhenggong responded.

—Did you report this to Captain Chen?

"I tried calling him, but I couldn't get through."

There was a long silence. Then Xiao Zhenggong gave the order.

—Eliminate the target. Bring whatever he took back to the Bureau.

As soon as Lightbulb heard that, his entire body trembled. Li Banfeng, who was standing beside the shelf, was trembling too. In fact, he was shaking even harder.

Eliminate me? You've got to be kidding. What did I even do for you guys to want me dead?

Chapter 7: Never Misses

He's going to kill me!

Li Banfeng couldn't accept this reality.

The large-eyed man couldn't accept the order either. "Deputy Xiao, all he did was take something. You want to kill him just because of that?"

—This is an order, Lightbulb. You don't understand how important that thing is. You have no idea how many people will end up dead because of it! Don't forget how your parents lost their lives. And never forget what kind of enemy we're dealing with. For the safety of the majority, we must kill him immediately. That's our duty, our mission.

"But Deputy Xiao…"

—No buts. Find a secluded spot and take him out. Grab the item and bring it to me. Hide the body. I'll send someone to get rid of it.

Xiao Zhenggong ended the call.

Lightbulb stood there silently for a moment, his eyes wide and blank. He didn't know what to do.

A minute or so later, he steadied his breathing, walked to the entrance of the supermarket, and peered inside.

Li Banfeng bought a bottle of Coke, two cans of chips, and two packs of latiao, the extremely spicy kind. Then he casually walked to a checkout counter.

After paying, Li Banfeng exited the campus supermarket and stepped onto the paved pathway between Academic Building 2 and the library. It was lunchtime and close to summer break, so there wasn't a single person on the pathway.

The pathway would lead him close to the university's east gate. Then, from the gate, the subway station was less than eight hundred meters away.

Li Banfeng was walking briskly along the path when Lightbulb suddenly blocked his way. They were less than five meters apart and stared at each other for a moment.

Lightbulb took a deep breath and recited in his mind the words he was supposed to say.

Put your hands on your head and squat down. Please cooperate with our investigation.

Once Li Banfeng squatted down, that would be the best moment to kill him.

Yet, Lightbulb said something entirely different. "I don't want to hurt you, but if you don't follow my orders, don't blame me for being ruthless."

That wasn't something an agent of the Dark Star Bureau should say. However, Lightbulb was very nervous, and the words just slipped out of his mouth. He truly didn't want to kill anyone.

Lightbulb continued giving Li Banfeng instructions. "Put your backpack on the ground first, then hold your hands out. You will stay here with me and wait for someone."

Then he pulled out a pair of handcuffs.

Lightbulb's plan was to restrain Li Banfeng and wait for Deputy Xiao to arrive. If Deputy Xiao still insisted on killing Li Banfeng even after meeting him, then it would prove that Li Banfeng truly deserved to die.

Li Banfeng nodded, took off his backpack, and slowly placed it on the ground.

Lightbulb kept his eyes fixed on the backpack. When it hit the ground, he finally relaxed.

He's just a student, after all. He doesn't know anything. Capturing him is easier than catching a chicken. When Deputy Xiao sees him like this, he'll probably let him live.

As Lightbulb approached with the handcuffs, he didn't notice Li Banfeng pulling a strip of the extremely spicy latiao from his pocket.

With a flick of his wrist, Li Banfeng hurled the strip of latiao at Lightbulb. And of course, if the target was within five meters, he never missed.

The strip of latiao struck Lightbulb's left eye, causing searing pain to shoot through it. He immediately placed his left hand over that eye.

At the same moment, Li Banfeng flicked out a second strip of latiao and smacked Lightbulb square in the right eye.

Lightbulb had not expected Li Banfeng to attack him. He clamped his right hand over his right eye. He couldn't see from his right eye at the moment.

It was true that Li Banfeng never wanted to get involved. However, it was also true that since he was involved now, there would be no turning back.

Grimy, oily slime oozed out from all over Lightbulb’s body. He braced himself, ready for a brutal fight with Li Banfeng.

Lightbulb was very nervous. This was his first real battle since joining the Dark Star Bureau. Faced with this sudden turn of events, he instinctively activated his Dark Ability.

How ruthless! This guy's tactics are so ruthless and underhanded!

Lightbulb had underestimated Li Banfeng. Now that he had lost his sight, he absolutely couldn't act rashly. Otherwise, the battle would surely end disastrously for him. He had to stabilize his footing first, set up a solid defense, then counterattack!

Is Li Banfeng skilled in close combat or ranged attacks? I'm not afraid of fighting him at close quarters. After all, Li Banfeng is just a Bleater. If he dares to get close, he'll be the one who suffers. Ranged attacks though… those will be a problem for me...

Lightbulb waited for a long time, but Li Banfeng never made a move.

Gritting through the pain, Lightbulb opened his eyes… only to find that Li Banfeng was gone.

Did he run?

Yes, he did.

With his backpack in hand, Li Banfeng had already reached the east gate.

Lightbulb quickly concentrated the slime under his feet. He bent his knees, lowered his posture, and began alternating his feet forward, gliding as if he were ice skating. He gave chase at lightning speed.

Nevertheless, Li Banfeng wasn't slow either. He ran at full speed for eight hundred meters, dashing straight into the subway entrance. As Lightbulb neared the subway entrance, the number of pedestrians kept increasing. He reabsorbed the slime and took on a normal running posture. He didn't want to draw attention or hurt any innocent bystanders.

By the time Lightbulb entered the subway station, Li Banfeng had already boarded a subway train. Lightbulb rushed toward the doors of a subway car, but he was too late.

Beep, beep, beep, beep!

The subway train's warning chime sounded, and the doors slid shut.

Lightbulb could only watch as the subway train took off with Li Banfeng in it, vanishing into the tunnel. He waited anxiously for the next subway train to arrive.

Hang on. What am I even doing? My car's parked at the university, so why the heck am I standing here waiting for the subway?!

Let's think… Where would he go?

Ugh, how would I know where he's headed? How would I know which stop he'll get off at?? Trying to chase someone on the subway is too difficult!

With no other option left, Lightbulb called Xiao Zhenggong.

"Deputy Xiao, I lost him."

Xiao Zhenggong was irate.

—How could you lose him?! Didn't I tell you to kill him on the spot?

Lightbulb lied, "I couldn't find the right moment to do it."

He did not dare to admit that Li Banfeng had managed to escape because he had underestimated him.

—Where did he go?

"He just boarded the subway."

—Which line?

Lightbulb remembered the details very clearly. "Line Four. It left University Town at 11:45 and is heading toward Beautiful Cloud Plaza."

Xiao Zhenggong fell silent.

Lightbulb grew increasingly nervous. He knew Xiao Zhenggong was furious, so he quickly thought of an idea and suggested it. "Deputy Xiao, if we contact the relevant departments right now and shut all the exits on Line Four, we can definitely catch Li Banfeng."

Xiao Zhenggong snapped.

—Shut the subway exits? Are you out of your mind? Do you want news of this incident to spread across all of Yuezhou? Do you want the whole city to know you're an incompetent piece of trash?!

Lightbulb gritted his teeth in silence, afraid to make a sound.

Xiao Zhenggong spent a moment thinking about what to do.

—Line Four connects to Yuezhou East Station. If Li Banfeng's trying to leave the city, you'll be able to intercept him there.

Lightbulb said quietly, "We could tell the subway station not to let him board—"

Xiao Zhenggong got angry again.

—How many times do I have to tell you?! We're dealing with an incredibly important matter. We cannot have any information leaks! We cannot cause the public to panic! The train station is full of eyes and ears. If you spot him, do not engage right away. Wait for the right moment to kill him, but you absolutely cannot get caught doing it!

—Lightbulb, your parents were heroes. I believe you're not useless. This is a simple task. I know you can handle it. Do not shame the Dark Star Bureau again, and stop disgracing your parents!

Xiao Zhenggong ended the call.

Lightbulb ran all the way back to his car at the university and fired it up.

On the way to the Yuezhou East Station, Lightbulb gripped the steering wheel tightly.

"Incompetent piece of trash!"

"Stop disgracing your parents!"

Lightbulb's eyes turned red, threatening to tear up. He rubbed them and gritted his teeth.

Isn't he just a university student?

Isn't he supposed to be mentally unstable?

How did he escape from me?

How could I let him get away?

I'm not trash.

If I find Li Banfeng at the east station, I'll kill him right then and there, without the slightest hesitation.

Xiao Zhenggong changed into casual clothes and made his way from the second floor of his villa down to the garage. He got into his car and started it up.

Lightbulb, do not let this matter get out of hand. You must not let anyone find out.

You will kill Li Banfeng and obtain the item.

Then I'll kill you, and this whole thing will be over.

The subway train that Li Banfeng was on arrived at Rockwall Station. The next stop was Yuezhou East Station.

On Li Banfeng's left, there was a burly man. His dark blue shirt was speckled with ash gray clay.

Li Banfeng smiled at him.

On Li Banfeng's right, there was a beautiful woman. She had long, shoulder-length hair and earphones in her ears.

Li Banfeng flashed her a smile too.

He hadn't expected the subway train to be so crowded at this hour. Nevertheless, Li Banfeng unzipped his backpack and began undressing right there.

Alarmed, the passengers around him shot him strange looks and quickly moved as far away from him as they could. Despite being in a crowded subway train car, they instantly cleared a wide berth around Li Banfeng.

After removing his top, Li Banfeng started taking off his pants.

The beautiful woman screamed.

The burly man screamed too.

An elderly passenger covered a child's eyes.

Many of the passengers stared at him and shouted in unison, "Pervert!"

Li Banfeng pulled a long-sleeved white dress shirt and a pair of black dress pants from his backpack and quickly changed into them. Then he took out a hiking hat and put it on. The entire process took less than a minute.

The subway train arrived at the east station, and Li Banfeng immediately alighted from the subway train, leaving before the chaos inside the subway train car could fully erupt. He exited the subway station and stopped near the train station's turnstiles[1] to send a message to He Jiaqing.

Li Banfeng: Have you bought the ticket?

He Jiaqing: Yeah. You can board now.
