Li Banfeng swiped his ID and was about to step through a turnstile. Right then, he spotted Lightbulb standing on guard beside the turnstiles. However, Lightbulb didn't notice him.

Lightbulb was concentrating hard, but he was also very nervous. He was trying his best to scrutinize every person going through the turnstiles. Nevertheless, that was, of course, an impossible feat. Passengers were flooding into the Yuezhou East Station at an overwhelming rate. There were too many people entering, and he was only watching for those wearing T-shirts.

Leaving Lightbulb behind, Li Banfeng passed through the turnstile and felt more at ease. He received a new text message from He Jiaqing.

He Jiaqing: Why were so many people calling you a pervert on the subway earlier?

Why is he asking that? Did he not see what happened? Can he only hear but not see?

Li Banfeng: I changed clothes on the train.

He Jiaqing: Are you crazy?

Li Banfeng: I'm not crazy.

He Jiaqing: Were you not afraid of causing a scene?

Li Banfeng: It didn't even take a minute. It's not that serious.

He Jiaqing couldn't wrap his mind around it.

He Jiaqing: You changed that fast?

Li Banfeng thought it was perfectly normal.

Li Banfeng: How do you think I got out of the hospital back then?

He Jiaqing: You ran away? They didn't catch you and drag you back there?

Li Banfeng couldn't be bothered to explain.

Why would they catch me? That was just the psychiatric ward of a hospital, not a psychiatric hospital.

After standing by the turnstiles for nearly half an hour, Lightbulb still hadn't caught sight of Li Banfeng, and he was growing increasingly anxious.

Right then, Xiao Zhenggong suddenly appeared in front of him.

Lightbulb asked, "Deputy Xiao, what are you doing here—"

"Just how long were you planning to wait here?"

Xiao Zhenggong pulled out his ID, said a few words to the train station staff, and led Lightbulb through a turnstile.

After doing a quick scan of the train station's departure lounge, Xiao Zhenggong and Lightbulb walked to the far end of Waiting Area 2. They went past the restrooms and into an underground passage.

1. The machine that reads your card and then lets you through. ☜

Chapter 8: The Lucky Star

East Station, Yuezhou City, Eastern Yue Prefecture.

East Station was the oldest train station in Yuezhou. It had six waiting areas and handled every kind of train, from high-speed rail to express lines to the slow green trains that crawled through the countryside.

Li Banfeng lowered his head to check the information on his ticket.

Train 1160. Why is it this train again?

Li Banfeng typed out a message.

Li Banfeng: Didn't this train derail before?

Ka-da-da~

He Jiaqing: The accident's been handled. Service resumed.

Handled already? It was such a serious accident. And now it’s all resolved?

There wasn't even a "K" in front of the train number. That meant it wasn't a regular express train. It was the slowest kind.

Hopefully, his hometown isn't too far… Li Banfeng muttered inwardly.

The ticket stated "Platform 96." Li Banfeng made his way through all the waiting areas. He passed by middle-aged men in suits and casually dressed young people.

What stood out most were the elderly folks in plain clothes. They were loaded with bags, carrying them on their backs, over their shoulders, and in their hands. Many even had large enamel tea mugs[1] tied to the outside of their luggage. It was the most common sight at the station, yet somehow the most striking.

However, even after checking every corner, Li Banfeng still couldn't find Platform 96.

He sent He Jiaqing another message.

Li Banfeng: Where's Platform 96?

He Jiaqing: The location changes. Go ask the staff.

Li Banfeng asked five different station attendants. The first four all gave the same answer, saying that no such platform existed.

The fifth staff member took one look at his ticket and led him toward the Waiting Area 2. They passed the restrooms, entered a corridor, opened a metal door, and stepped into an underground passage.

Underground passages weren't unusual in train stations, but this one was different. It was long and dimly lit, and a thick, rotting stench hung in the air. The floor was full of potholes, and stagnant puddles had collected in several places. Tiny damp insects wriggled about in the murky water.

It reminded Li Banfeng of the kind of grimy underground passage he had only seen back in his childhood. When he was a child, he used to follow Granny Wu to an abandoned train station where they didn't go to catch trains but to steal scrap metal.

After they passed through the long underground passage, the staff member led Li Banfeng straight to the platform and pulled out a pitch-black box shaped like a mailbox. A crank stuck out from the right side of the box.

The staff member turned it, and a strip of the train ticket slid out from a slit at the top. It was a strip of ticket about ten centimeters long, made of soft paper. This was different from the stiff, hard paper tickets Li Banfeng was familiar with.

The staff member stamped the ticket and handed it to Li Banfeng. The front of the ticket listed every stop along the route, along with their expected arrival times.

Three-Headed Fork, the Ridges of Bottomless Stomachs, Greenwater Bay, Girdle-Slipping Hollow...

However, he didn't recognize a single location. At the bottom of the ticket was the name of the destination, and it was this station called Medicine King Gully.

Is He Jiaqing's hometown in Medicine King Gully? Li Banfeng wondered. He had never been there, and he had never heard of this location before.

He looked at the departure time: June 28th, 1:53 PM.

The train would arrive in fifteen minutes.

Then he checked the arrival time: June 30th, 7:00 AM.

Nearly two full days. This trip is way too long, Li Banfeng complained inwardly.

Next, he looked at the seating type: Carriage Seven, Seat Seven. First-class Sleeper.

Li Banfeng thought, Oh. This means it's a soft berth. I can somewhat accept this.

There was a line of small print on the ticket. Li Banfeng squinted at it and saw that it read: Hot water is available on this train. No food will be provided. Passengers, please prepare accordingly.

He grumbled inwardly, This train doesn't sell any food when it's a two-day journey?!!! What about sunflower seeds, drinks, and bottled water? Will the train not have any of those basic snack carts?

Why didn't Jiaqing mention this earlier? What am I supposed to eat during the trip? There's food for sale at Waiting Area 2. Should I run all the way back there from the platform? But the train leaves in fifteen minutes. I probably can't make it in time…

Not only is the route too complicated, I will also have to get through a metal gate. And if I happen to run into the man with those lightbulb eyes, I would surely be killed on the spot.

Should I maybe buy a little something from someone here?

Those thoughts run through his mind, but he then realized that he was the only one waiting at this old, worn-out platform.

Should I just board the train and look for someone later? Would anyone even be willing to sell to me?

At this critical moment, Li Banfeng's phone buzzed. It was a message from He Jiaqing.

He Jiaqing: Bro, did you buy food?

Li Banfeng: You're only telling me this now? Why didn't you tell me back at the supermarket?

He Jiaqing: It was urgent back then, and I forgot. Once you get on the train, make sure you don't leave your berth compartment."

Seeing this, Li Banfeng thought, Why can't I leave the berth compartment? Does going to the restroom count as leaving? There's no way they want me to hold it in for two whole days, right?

Just as Li Banfeng stood there in confusion, he suddenly saw an old lady approaching along the platform, carrying a cardboard box in her arms.

"Young man, want some cup noodles?"

Li Banfeng eyed her warily. People actually sell things on this platform?

To be fair, it wasn't unusual for vendors to show up at green train platforms. He had taken green trains before. This wasn't anything out of the ordinary. The weird thing was that he did not see this lady on the platform earlier.

The old lady said, "The train is coming soon. There's no food on board. Better buy a few cup noodles to take with you!"

"How much per cup?"

"Four yuan."

Only four yuan? Li Banfeng thought.

This price would've been a bargain even in a regular supermarket in Yuezhou City, let alone at a train station.

"I'll take ten!" Li Banfeng answered. Inwardly, he thought, Ten boxes for two days should be enough.

Just as Li Banfeng pulled out his phone to scan the payment QR code, a tall man in uniform stepped onto the platform and snatched the box of cup noodles away from the old lady.

"Who gave you permission to sell here?" the man in uniform demanded.

"I talked to the stationmaster," the old lady replied in a trembling voice.

"The stationmaster didn't tell me anything! I'm confiscating these!" the man in uniform shouted. He hoisted the box onto his shoulder and turned to leave.

The old lady trembled as she watched him go. Her heart ached for that box of cup noodles, but she didn't dare say a word.

Li Banfeng stepped forward and grabbed the box, yanking it off the man's shoulder. The sudden force made the man stumble.

The man in uniform was startled and asked, "What do you think you're doing?"

"What do you think you're doing?" Li Banfeng retorted. "Robbing people in broad daylight?"

The man in uniform frowned and barked, "I'm a platform staff member! She's selling things illegally! I am simply following protocol!"

"Illegally selling what?" Li Banfeng muttered to the man in uniform. Then he turned to the old lady and continued, "This is my second aunt. She came to see me off and brought me a box of cup noodles. Is that not allowed?"

The man in uniform was left speechless. Li Banfeng turned to the old lady and said, "Second Aunt, you can just see me off here. I'll be boarding soon."

As Li Banfeng spoke, he quietly slipped a one-hundred yuan bill into the old lady's hand.

The old lady stared at Li Banfeng for a long moment without saying a word.

There are twenty-four cups in the box altogether, which is a bit too much. But it's whatever! Li Banfeng figured he'd just bring them to He Jiaqing's house and share them with his family.

Choo-choo~ Chug-chug-chug! Choo-choo~ Chug-chug-chug!

What is that sound? This was Li Banfeng's first thought. He followed the tracks with his eyes, looking toward the far end of the line.

The first thing he saw was a plume of white smoke. But on closer look, it wasn't smoke at all. It was steam.

Drifting within that steam were thick clumps of black haze. It was real smoke, rising from the soot of burning coal.

Ding-clang! Ding-clang! Ding-clang!

It was the sharp, mechanical rhythm of a crankshaft striking its connecting rods.

Tooooooooooooooot!

It was actually the whistle of a steam-powered engine!

Li Banfeng stood there in shock. His jaw nearly dropped to the floor. Is that… a steam train? Do steam trains still exist in this day and age?

The deep-black locomotive thundered in, billowing clouds of steam as it screeched to a halt at the platform. One by one, the green train cars swung open, and the conductors stepped out to lower the boarding ladders.

Li Banfeng lifted his box of cup noodles and made his way toward Carriage Seven.

At the entrance to the platform, Xiao Zhenggong and Lightbulb stood in silence, watching as Li Banfeng approached the train.

"Lightbulb. Take him out," Xiao Zhenggong ordered.

A shiver ran through Lightbulb. He hadn't expected Deputy Xiao to be here in person and still not act himself.

Is Deputy Xiao testing me? he wondered. No… this isn’t a test. This is my last chance.

In truth, Xiao Zhenggong had thought about doing it himself. However, when he saw the old woman on the platform selling cup noodles, he changed his mind.

The old woman was still watching Li Banfeng, and this sent a chill down Xiao Zhenggong’s spine. He knew exactly who she was. If she had any connection to Li Banfeng, then making a move here was out of the question. The consequence of killing Li Banfeng while she was still present would be catastrophic.

However, the thought of letting Li Banfeng walk away like this was something Xiao Zhenggong could not accept.

Is that old woman really watching Li Banfeng? Xiao Zhenggong wondered. Perhaps she has no connection to him at all.

Instead of letting Li Banfeng escape, Xiao Zhenggong decided to experiment by sending Lightbulb in to test the waters.

"Kill the suspect immediately. This is your last chance!" Xiao Zhenggong ordered again.

At Xiao Zhenggong's order, Lightbulb gritted his teeth and was about to rush onto the platform when someone grabbed him from behind. He turned around and found that it was Old Chen who had stopped him.

When did Captain get here? Lightbulb wondered, quickly snapping to attention and saluting.

Xiao Zhenggong pointed at Li Banfeng, who was about to board the train. "Captain! Li Banfeng is trying to escape. He might be carrying something important. This is our only chance to bring him back."

Old Chen nodded. "That makes sense. Go on, then. I'll wait for you here."

At those words, Xiao Zhenggong's cheek twitched, but he didn't move at all.

Lightbulb had no idea why Xiao Zhenggong was not making a move. He was very confused and thought, Was he really just trying to test me?

Old Chen looked at Xiao Zhenggong and smiled. "You're scared?"

Xiao Zhenggong did not defend himself. Indeed, he was scared.

Hearing that, Lightbulb felt even more confused now as he thought, What is Xiao Zhenggong scared of? Why would he be scared of a Bleater[2] like Li Banfeng?

Old Chen stared at the old lady on the platform. "Li Banfeng took Lucky Star's item, which means he's guaranteed to have good luck three times. If you send Lightbulb to capture him right in front of Lucky Star, aren't you just sending him to his death?"

That one line left Lightbulb utterly dumbfounded, and he stared at Deputy Xiao in disbelief. Who is Lucky Star? That old lady? I will die if I go? Then why did Deputy Xiao tell me to go?

However, Xiao Zhenggong kept a blank face and showed not even a trace of guilt. "I'm not sure if that's the Lucky Star. I'm not sure Lucky Star is connected to Li Banfeng in any way."

Lightbulb's face went ghostly pale. You're not sure? And you still told me to go… to be your guinea pig?

At that thought, his view of Xiao Zhenggong changed completely.

Xiao Zhenggong continued, "What Li Banfeng has is extremely important. We can't let him take it away. We have to take some risks to stop him."

Lightbulb stared at Xiao Zhenggong. "And you tried to make me take all the risk?"

"This is your job," Xiao replied calmly. "You were the one who lost track of Li Banfeng in the first place."

Old Chen patted Lightbulb on the back, trying to calm him down.

Once Lightbulb calmed down, Old Chen turned to Xiao Zhenggong. "Wouldn't it be better to let that thing go where it's meant to go?"

Xiao Zhenggong's expression turned cold. "Li Banfeng isn't a Dark Ability User. If he takes that thing to Puluo, it'll be like walking to his death. It'd be better to just…"

Old Chen kept the same smile as he said, "I'll tell you what I said before. If you want to go, then go. Quick! I won't stop you!"

Xiao Zhenggong stayed silent. He would never risk his life provoking Lucky Star.

Old Chen watched Li Banfeng's back and muttered inwardly, Young man, with Lucky Star's protection, you should make it to Puluo safely. But when you return… I wonder if you'll still be the same.

Tooooot! Toooooooot!

The whistle blew again, as if urging passengers to board quickly.

Li Banfeng reached the door of the green train car. The conductor checked his ticket, clipped a notch in it with pliers, and waved him aboard.

He stepped into the narrow corridor and found a door marked "Seven."

This… is the berth? Li Banfeng wondered.

He pushed open the door marked "Seven" and discovered that it was a small compartment.

A private room? Li Banfeng muttered inwardly. They have private compartments on trains in Huan?

Inside the room was a long bench. This kind of long bench was very commonly seen in green trains. In Li Banfeng's memory, they usually came in pairs, but here there was only one.

In front of the bench was a small dining table, a standard feature of green trains that Li Banfeng knew well.

Next to it was a train window, with handles and latches at the bottom corners for opening. Just as he was about to open it and let in some fresh air, he noticed two more doors inside the compartment.

He opened one of the doors, and a familiar scent wafted out.

It was a bathroom. Through the squat toilet, he could see the railway tracks rushing below[3]. A washbasin stood beside it. This was a standard restroom setup in a green train.

A first-class sleeper ticket came with a private bathroom?

Li Banfeng turned and opened the other door. There was another small room inside. To be accurate, this was the berth, as this room had one bed inside.

A bedroom, a living space, and a bathroom all in one compartment. Is this what a first-class sleeper gets to enjoy? Li Banfeng thought.

Li Banfeng was starting to worry that he had gotten the wrong carriage. He was just about to push the door open and step out to ask about it when the speaker in the living room crackled to life.

「Dear passengers, welcome aboard Train 1160. Passengers who have already boarded, please refrain from moving about in the train unnecessarily. First-time passengers, please keep your curtains drawn, and do not look out the window. Under no circumstances should you open the windows.

The train attendants are only responsible for ticket verification and arrival reminders. They will not disturb you at other times. Unless absolutely necessary, do not let strangers into your compartment.

We wish you a pleasant journey.」

Crackle~

The announcement ended. Li Banfeng was still puzzled by some of the peculiar rules, like not being allowed to open the window or having to keep the curtains drawn, when his phone buzzed with a new message from He Jiaqing.

He Jiaqing: Bro, you must absolutely and strictly follow the rules on this train.

1. These mugs were very common in China from the 1950s to the 1980s. They are typically made of metal with a handle and lid. Refer to translator's thought. ☜

2. This is a special term. As you read, you will know what it means. ☜

3. On older green trains in China, the squat toilets are essentially just openings directly above the tracks. ☜

Chapter 9: Three-Headed Fork

After receiving He Jiaqing's warning to abide by the train's rules, Li Banfeng quickly closed the curtains and locked the compartment door. He took out two cups of instant noodles and poured in some hot water from the vacuum flask in the compartment. He let the noodles soak for a while and then ate them.

The delicious taste seemed to leave an imprint on his soul. Instant noodles always tasted better on a train.

After finishing his meal, Li Banfeng got comfortable in his seat and messaged He Jiaqing.

Li Banfeng: Tell me now. What exactly is going on?

He Jiaqing: I'm trapped in Medicine King Gully. I'll explain in person when you get to my place.

Li Banfeng: Then tell me this first. Where exactly is Medicine King Gully? Is it still in Eastern Yue Prefecture?

He Jiaqing: It's not in Eastern Yue Prefecture. It's in Puluo Province. Without that package, I can't get out.

Li Banfeng: What is that place?

He Jiaqing: Bro, I'm exhausted. I can barely hold on. Let's talk when we meet.

Li Banfeng: What exactly is in this package?

He Jiaqing: Let me rest for a bit. I promise I'll tell you. Just remember, do not open the package.

That was his last text message.

Li Banfeng took the package out of his backpack and set it on the meal table. He stared at it quietly for a long while.

If he were to say that he wasn't curious, that would be a lie. If he were to say he wouldn't open it, that would also be a lie. He was most certainly going to open it. The question was when.

Li Banfeng was convinced that what lay inside the package was a rare treasure. However, he remembered the conversation that the man with wide lightbulb-like eyes had on the phone. He had called the man on the other end of the line Deputy Xiao, and Deputy Xiao had said that this item could cause the deaths of many.

Could it be some kind of poison or a biological weapon, like a pathogen or virus?

Li Banfeng had not received any new text messages from He Jiaqing. It appeared he really was exhausted.

Li Banfeng was really tired too. Back in the dorm, he hadn't been able to fall asleep no matter how hard he tried. Yet, here in the train carriage, an intense drowsiness washed over him rapidly; he found it hard to resist.

He pulled out his phone and searched for information about Puluo Province, Medicine King Gully, and Greenwater Bay. The search results were random and inconsistent, nothing definitive. He looked for a long time but didn't find anything useful.

The compartment was dimly lit. The light fixture used an old type of bulb, one that he had rarely seen during his childhood. It cast a hazy yellow glow that made his eyelids heavy. The window curtains were drawn tightly, without even the slightest gap. All Li Banfeng could see through the curtains were faint shadows of the scenery going by outside.

The steam train's huffing and puffing, the rhythmic clatter of its wheels on the train tracks, and the occasional whistle when it released some steam—these sounds formed hypnotic white noise that lulled Li Banfeng into a semi-conscious state. He stepped into the bedroom and dropped onto the berth, falling asleep instantly.

Li Banfeng had no idea how long he had slept. At some point, he woke up to drink water and use the restroom. Then he went back to sleep.

He kept sleeping until someone pounded on his compartment door, jolting him awake.

A woman called out, "Sir! Sir! Please open the door. Just let me stay inside for a bit, just a little while!"

She sounded very anxious, frantic, and on the verge of tears.

Then a child pleaded, "Mister, please open the door. Please open the door!"

The child began to cry. However, Li Banfeng didn't open the door.

The train announcement had made it clear that passengers were not to let strangers into their compartment unless absolutely necessary.

Additionally, there was one critical issue.

How does the woman know I'm a man? And how does the child know I'm an adult?

Li Banfeng hadn't left his compartment since he boarded the train. Aside from the train attendant who dropped by to check his ticket, he hadn't seen anyone else.

"Sir, I'm begging you. Just let me stay a while. I'll leave soon after!"

"Mister, please open the door…"

Knock, knock, knock!

The knocking grew more frantic, and the sobbing more pitiful. Nevertheless, Li Banfeng stayed silent.

Meanwhile, the steam train was slowing down. There was another train announcement.

「Dear passengers, we are arriving at Three-Headed Fork Station. If this is your stop, please pack up your belongings and prepare to disembark.」

The woman outside Li Banfeng's compartment shouted until her voice grew hoarse. "I'm begging you, sir! Let my child hide inside! I won't go in!"

What is going on? Why must they hide in my compartment of all places?

Li Banfeng stepped toward the door, intending to peek through the gap between the door and frame. However, he suddenly heard a man's voice.

"Just kick the door down. There's no way he'll open it!"

Li Banfeng quickly backed away from the door and looked around for something he could use as a weapon.

There are three people outside? There's even a man with them!

He had underestimated how dangerous it was to be on this train.

"Kick down the door now! If we wait any longer, it'll be too late!" exclaimed the man.

Would he really do it? Li Banfeng thought. I don't think so. If he was going to, he'd have done it already.

The inaction inside and outside the compartment led to a deadlock.

Hissssssss.

The train let out a long puff of steam before coming to a halt.

Clatter!

A train attendant opened the train car's outer door and lowered the step ladder for disembarking.

The woman outside Li Banfeng's compartment screamed furiously, "Open the door! Open it! I told you to open it! Are you going to open it or not?!"

The compartment door shook intensely. She had started kicking it.

Li Banfeng remarked inwardly, That's odd. Why is she the one kicking the door?

He couldn't see outside the compartment, but he could tell from the rhythm of the sounds that the person yelling and the person kicking the door were one and the same. It was the woman!

The compartment door was shaking so violently that it could give way at any moment. Li Banfeng gripped the only weapons he could find in the compartment: some latiao and the vacuum flask.

At this moment, his neck itched. But he couldn't spare a hand to scratch it!

Crap. It's so damn itchy. Why now of all times??

Li Banfeng put down the vacuum flask and gave his neck two good scratches. Then he picked the vacuum flask back up again.

Bam! Bam!

Just as the compartment door was about to give way, someone else spoke outside the compartment. "We've arrived at your stop. Please disembark immediately."

Li Banfeng recognized the person's voice. It's the attendant!

Knowing that the attendant was dealing with the woman, Li Banfeng relaxed a little. He set the vacuum flask down and scratched his neck again.

The woman shrieked, "I'm not getting off the train! I'll find a way to buy another ticket. I can't get off at the Three-Headed Fork. The Peddler left; he's not there anymore. I have to stay on until the next stop! I need to go to the Ridges of Bottomless Stomachs!"

Li Banfeng was dumbfounded. A peddler? Why does she need to go to the Ridges of Bottomless Stomachs just because a peddler left?

Then a scuffle broke out outside the compartment.

The woman screamed, "I'm not getting off!"

The child cried, "No! Not getting off!"

The man yelled, "We'll pay soon!"

The voices of the woman, child, and man all rang together amid the sounds of the scuffle. The noises gradually moved further and further away. It appeared the attendant was forcing them off the train.

Moments later, the attendant pulled up the step ladder and shut the train door.

Li Banfeng sat back down and wiped the cold sweat from his forehead. He scratched his neck and started piecing the situation together.

So those three wanted to get into my compartment to avoid paying for tickets. If I had let them in, they probably would've taken over the whole compartment, and that would've been the least of my worries. They had the nerve to kick my door and get into a scuffle with the attendant. And they had no money for tickets… If I had let them in, they definitely would've tried to rob me.

Then if I couldn't fight them off, they would have taken everything: my money, my cup noodles, my latiao, my chips, and even He Jiaqing's treasured item. Everything else I could live without, but the one thing I can't let anyone take is my cup noodles. I wouldn't be able to survive this trip without them!

Sitting by the window, Li Banfeng took a few gulps of water and tried to calm his nerves.

Choo choo~ Choo choo~

After a ten-minute stop, the steam train started moving again. Li Banfeng's neck just kept getting itchier. It seemed like he was getting a rash.

He took out his phone to check the time, but someone hit his window.

Who hit the window? Is it those three from earlier?

Heavy, muffled thumps echoed one after another.

Bam! Bam! Bam!

The train kept accelerating, yet someone was still hitting his window.

Someone's chasing the train and hitting my window? How fast is he running?!

Li Banfeng backed away from the window in alarm. He considered opening the curtains to look. However, he remembered the warning in the train announcement and decided against it.

The train passed by a streetlamp, and under its light, a silhouette appeared on the window curtains. The silhouette Li Banfeng saw had the palm of a large hand, muscular arms, a wide chest, and shoulders even broader than that chest. Resting on top of those extremely broad shoulders were three heads. He only saw the silhouette for an instant as it flashed past, but he was certain of what he saw.

Is that the same person that kicked my door earlier? It seems like it!

Li Banfeng had thought there had been three people outside his door earlier. But in reality, it had been one person with three heads! The conversation between the woman, child, and man had actually been between the three heads, each one speaking in turn.

This place is called the Three-Headed Fork. Is there a connection between this three-headed man and the Three-Headed Fork?

As the train passed a row of streetlamps, the silhouette of the three-headed man kept flickering across the window curtains. He gripped the exterior frame of the train window with one hand, leaving the rest of him to dangle in midair. The exception was his other hand. He just kept slamming it onto the window. The glass seemed like it could shatter at any moment.

Li Banfeng picked up his latiao and vacuum flask again, preparing himself for battle.

Suddenly, someone extended a pole down from the roof of the train and jabbed the three-headed man more than a dozen times, pushing him off the window. It was the train attendant.

After successfully pushing the three-headed man off the train, the train attendant retracted the pole, calmly leapt onto the chain link between the train cars, and went back inside.

Li Banfeng stood in the center of his compartment, not daring to stay close to either the door or the window.

Buzzz.

There was another train announcement.

「Dear passengers, welcome aboard Train 1160. Passengers who have already boarded, please refrain from moving about in the train unnecessarily. ...」

Li Banfeng's neck grew itchier and itchier. After scratching it a few more times, he realized that he had scratched through the surface of his skin, causing it to bleed.

What's going on? Why is it only my neck that itches? Am I going to grow two more heads too? What if I really do?

"Then let them grow! It's lonely being alone!"

"We've always been by your side. If you grow them, we can keep you company and chat with you!"

Li Banfeng just heard two people speaking right beside his ears.

Chapter 10: Girdle-Slipping Hollow

Li Banfeng didn't end up growing two extra heads. Less than an hour after the train left the Three-Headed Fork Station, the intense itching on his neck gradually subsided. He had scratched too hard earlier, leaving both sides of his neck covered in bloody scratches.

The extra heads hadn't physically emerged, but there were signs that they could have. If he had stayed at the Three-Headed Fork any longer, Li Banfeng suspected he really would have grown two extra heads. He was sure he had heard two voices speaking right beside his ears earlier. There was no way it had been him talking to himself.

Li Banfeng wasn't crazy. The doctor had said so. He really wasn't crazy.

In that case, what happened earlier? Why did I hear two people talking? Could I have developed split personalities? Well, if my personality has split, that means I won't have to worry about it splitting again. That's a good thing!

That thought brought a strange sense of relief to Li Banfeng.

However, there was still one thing he couldn't explain.

Do three-headed people exist in this world? I guess they're like conjoined twins. That makes sense! Then why did it seem like I was going to grow two extra heads earlier?

After thinking about it for a long time, Li Banfeng came up with two explanations he thought were reasonable.

This was the first explanation. I'm a conjoined triplet, but I've just never known it!

This was the second explanation. The Three-Headed Fork is a place that causes unusual changes in people.

There was no way to verify the first explanation right now, but the second one had a logical flaw. If the Three-Headed Fork could cause unusual changes in people, then why would the train stop there at all? Did it affect everyone or just people with special dispositions? Those were questions he could only try to verify online for now.

Li Banfeng retreated into his berth, accompanied by the rhythmic clatter and swaying of the train wheels colliding against the tracks. He took out his phone and searched for information on the Three-Headed Fork station, but all he got was a bunch of results on three-headed dragons.

Apparently, three-headed dragons aren't just monsters; they are also some kind of tool… Wait, why the fuck am I even reading this right now?

Li Banfeng then switched to searching for information about Train 1160. He found nothing. There didn't seem to be a single piece of information online about Train 1160. The train had a derailment incident recently, yet he couldn't find anything on it.

That's impossible! A derailment is a big deal. How could there not be a single news report about it? Could it be that there was never any derailment? Did Old Chen say that just to try and trick me into talking?

Li Banfeng wanted to try searching for it again, but his phone had lost its signal. He let out a sigh.

Then he opened up a novel he had saved offline on his phone beforehand and quietly waited for the signal to return. It turned out that there was a certain charm to reading a novel to the rhythmic pulse of steam and clangs of machinery. Li Banfeng ended up reading for several hours before drifting off to sleep at dawn.

When he woke up, it was already noon of the next day. The train had arrived at a station, and an announcement rang from the loudspeakers.

「Dear passengers, we have arrived at the Ridges of Bottomless Stomachs Station.」

Unlike the Three-Headed Fork, this station was bustling with life. Even through the glass, he could hear a cacophony of street vendors yelling.

"Freshly fried dough twists!"

"Soy milk, fried dough sticks, fried rice cakes!"

"Millet porridge, tea eggs!"

On his window curtains, Li Banfeng saw the silhouettes of large, round clusters passing by. He guessed that they were likely the street vendors pushing their carts back and forth as they tried to draw in customers.

The yells of the vendors drilled in from his ears to his mind, then they burrowed into his stomach.

Li Banfeng was hungry. He really wanted to open the window and buy something to eat—specifically, two tea eggs.

Despite that, Li Banfeng managed to curb his desires. He didn't open the window. Instead, he cooked two cups of instant noodles. However, even after finishing both cups, he still felt hungry. He couldn't stop himself from cooking another cup. Yet, after eating that one too, he was still hungry.

Should I cook another one? No, I can't wait any longer!

It felt like there were a hundred hands clawing at the inside of his stomach, trying to climb their way out of his throat in search of food. Li Banfeng ripped open another cup of instant noodles. He took out the block of uncooked instant noodles and dipped it in seasoning powder. He ate it dry.

The train's stopover at the Ridges of Bottomless Stomachs Station lasted for more than twenty minutes.

The train started moving again, and after an hour, Li Banfeng put down the block of instant noodles in his hand. His uncontrollable hunger had finally subsided. They had likely left the domain of the Ridges of Bottomless Stomachs.

Eight empty noodle cups sat in front of Li Banfeng. He had eaten the noodles from five of them dry. Li Banfeng's stomach felt bloated and distended. He wanted to vomit. He retched for a long time, but nothing came out.

His digestive system was having a hard time processing the excessive amount of food, leaving him feeling overwhelmed. He ended up lying down again.

Ka-da-da!

Li Banfeng's phone's notification sound chimed.

It appeared his phone signal had returned, and he received a new text message.

He Jiaqing: 21 Threaded Lane, Memorial Arch Street, Inner Gully, Medicine King Gully.

He Jiaqing had sent his home address to Li Banfeng.

He Jiaqing: Bro, just come straight to my place after you get off the train. I won't be able to pick you up tomorrow.

Li Banfeng: What's with the province you live in? Why are there people with three heads?!

He Jiaqing didn't reply.

Li Banfeng searched for "Medicine King Gully" and "the Ridges of Bottomless Stomachs," but he couldn't find any information on them online. Then he opened his phone's map app, hoping to at least confirm his location or which direction he was heading. However, the map was completely blank. The app could not locate him.

Feeling irritated, Li Banfeng tossed his phone aside. His phone battery was running low. There was a power outlet beside his berth, but he couldn't be bothered to charge his phone. He just turned over and slept.

As dusk approached, a cloyingly sweet fragrance drifted into Li Banfeng's compartment. That sweet scent made Li Banfeng strangely restless, rousing him from his sleep.

It's so sweet!

It was a cloying sweetness he could almost feel with his hands, something stretchy and very warm.

Li Banfeng took out his train ticket and checked the station name.

Is this Greenwater Bay? Is the water in Greenwater Bay sweet?

He checked the scheduled stop time.

No, it's not Greenwater Bay.

The train was scheduled to stop at Greenwater Bay at 3 PM. Steam trains often ran late, but it was already past 6 PM. That meant the train was likely at the next stop after Greenwater Bay—Girdle-Slipping Hollow. The name seemed quite indecent, but Li Banfeng couldn't figure out why a place called Girdle-Slipping Hollow would smell so sweet.

He stepped to the side of the berth and was about to try peeking outside through the window curtains.

However, someone knocked on his window right then. "Sir, would you like to employ our services?"

Li Banfeng stiffened in alarm. "What kind of services?"

"Services for train passengers. Two hundred yuan for twenty minutes. Specially designed to relieve travel fatigue."

Only two hundred?! That's a bargain!

The glow of the setting sun cast the silhouette of a slim and graceful woman onto the curtain. She had a sweet voice, sweetly scented cosmetic powder, and the sweet sincerity of a young lady.

Li Banfeng had found the source of that sweetness in the air.

Fighting his urges, Li Banfeng replied, "I don't need it, thanks."

Nevertheless, the young lady outside his window didn't leave. In addition to her sweet sincerity, she was sweetly patient. "Sir, I'm guessing it's your first time here? Don't worry, I don't have to enter your compartment."

Li Banfeng's imagination was rather limited. "If you don't come in, how are you going to provide the service?"

"Just crack open the window and give me access to the part you need me to take care of."

The part I need…

Li Banfeng pictured the scene in his mind. He would stand at the window… and stick out the part he needed her to take care of.

"That doesn't seem appropriate…"

"If the service isn't up to your standards, I won't charge you!"

The young lady had made such an earnest offer that it was weighing on Li Banfeng's conscience. How was he supposed to turn her down?

As he struggled with his inner turmoil, Li Banfeng stared at the thick window curtains and somehow saw the young lady standing outside. She was a shy girl-next-door, staring at him brightly with large, limpid eyes.

Her sweet, pure voice came through the window again. "Big Brother, I meant what I said, truly. If you think I'm no good, I won't charge you a thing!"

Li Banfeng nodded. "Big Brother knows. Big Brother knows you're sincere! Big Brother thinks you're great!"

He was on the verge of losing control. Just as he was about to open the window, he recalled the three-headed man from before.

What if this girl has three heads too? Triple the pleasure?

One head provides the service while the other two ask…

"Sir, are you satisfied?"

"Big Brother, I can do it."

Girl… I really can't picture it!

After giving it some thought, Li Banfeng proceeded to politely decline the young lady's offer of such magnificent hospitality. He even deliberately made his voice deeper and huskier so that he would sound like an older man. "We're not right for each other, Miss. You're too young. If fate wills it, we'll meet again."

Hearing that, the young lady gave up. The stopover had a time limit, so she had to quickly search for her next customer.

Just as Li Banfeng was regaining his composure, another woman knocked on the window.

She sounded older than the one before. "Sir, I'm just right for you. Right age, right price. One hundred yuan for fifteen minutes. I guarantee you'll be satisfied."

"Ma'am, I am a highly educated man. Such crude exchanges are beneath me," Li Banfeng said, making his final stand.

"Highly educated… I've got it!" The woman outside adjusted her tone. "Class, let's begin."

She now sounded intelligent and imposing, making Li Banfeng shiver in excitement. He seemed to be able to see the woman outside the window. She was a young teacher with glasses and dressed in a gray suit. She pointed at the writing on the blackboard as she taught every knowledge-hungry student with a gentle demeanor.

The teacher outside the window called out, "Class, repeat after me: service!"

The students echoed, "Service!"

The teacher wasn't quite satisfied. "Louder, class. One more time. Service!"

This time, the students' voices rang out loud and clear in perfect unison. "Service!"

The word rang resonantly over and over outside the window, driving Li Banfeng to retreat step by step.

Li Banfeng was about to cave.

With her alluring voice, the teacher called out to him, "My dear student, I assure you that I'm an excellent teacher. If you don't believe me, just open the window and take a look at my meticulously prepared teaching materials and lesson plans."

The teacher has spoken. I should obey the teacher!

Li Banfeng placed his hand on the window. His fingers crept slowly toward the latch. He was shaking with excitement.

Yet, at the last moment, Li Banfeng gritted his teeth and resisted once more. He adjusted his voice and said, "Big Sister, you've got the wrong customer. I'm actually a woman."

The ability to change his voice was one of Li Banfeng's many talents. He had once posed as a female streamer and earned quite a lot in tips.

The woman outside the window let out a cold snort and left.

Li Banfeng splashed cold water on his face, trying everything he could to extinguish his raging flames of desire.

I can't open the window. I have to follow the rules on the train.

However, no matter how much cold water he splashed on his face, the sweet fragrance in the air kept feeding the flames, making them more intense.

Li Banfeng was losing control. The temptation of Girdle-Slipping Hollow was too powerful. If one more person came to the window and named a price, Li Banfeng would open the window without hesitation and accept the offer.

Bam! Bam! Bam!

Someone really did come to his window again.

Li Banfeng's eyes turned bloodshot. He pressed both hands on the window latch.

No matter who it was, he had to have those services! Even if it cost him his life, he was going to have those services!

The person outside the window said, "Miss, would you like to employ my services? Twenty centimeters long. You pick the time. I guarantee you'll be satisfied before the train leaves. The price is just eighty yuan."

Li Banfeng withdrew his hands. He could not accept this offer.

Listening to the man outside knocking on the window nonstop, Li Banfeng thought the air wasn't all that sweet anymore.

He took out his train ticket and double-checked the itinerary.

This place really is called Girdle-Slipping Hollow. It's such a straightforward name… This is such a strange place...

Jiaqing, once I've handled everything for you, maybe I'll just settle down here for good.

The next stop is Medicine King Gully. Jiaqing, we'll be meeting again soon.

Chapter 11: Three Strokes of Good Luck

The train stopped at Girdle-Slipping Hollow for half an hour. During that time, five women and two men came to Li Banfeng's window to offer their services to him. Li Banfeng was a man of strong will, so he did not accept any of their offers.

However, it wasn't just them he had to deal with. Some of the other passengers had accepted the offers. Among them, some invited the workers into their compartments, and others did the deed at the window. Li Banfeng could hear them loud and clear.

Throughout that long half hour, Li Banfeng bit his finger but never opened his window.

Endure. I have to endure it. Come tomorrow morning, I'll get to Jiaqing's place. And he's gonna have to find someone to perform some services for me. And if he can't find someone to satisfy me, then he'll just have to do it himself.

The train whistle blew as it left the station. Li Banfeng let out a long breath and quietly praised himself for having strong willpower.

However, it wasn't long before the train came to a sudden halt.

Have we arrived at the next stop?

The ticket stated the scheduled arrival time was 7 AM. The time estimates for steam trains weren't very accurate. Nevertheless, the train couldn't possibly have arrived at the next stop so much earlier than scheduled.

Crackle~

The speaker came alive with a piercing sound, followed by the train announcer's voice.

「Due to a mechanical fault, the train has to stop here temporarily. All passengers are asked to remain in their compartments. Please do not move about in the train unnecessarily. On behalf of all the train staff, I sincerely apologize for the inconvenience.」

A malfunction had occurred; the train had broken down in the middle of nowhere. The lights suddenly went out, plunging the compartments into darkness. Li Banfeng tried to use his phone as a flashlight, but the torch function wouldn't turn on.

His phone was about to die. It hadn't had much battery left earlier, but Li Banfeng hadn't bothered to charge it. Now, it had less than five percent battery left and was about to shut down. He quickly plugged in the charger, but his phone didn't react the way it normally did when charging.

The lights were out, and the power outlets were down too. The electricity had been cut off.

Li Banfeng looked at his phone. A moment later, it displayed a warning that it was about to shut down due to low battery.

The battery's flat? What am I going to do now? How am I going to find He Jiaqing's place?!

In truth, the dead phone should be the least of Li Banfeng's worries. The thing he should really be worried about was when the train repairs would be complete. On this railway line, it could take anywhere from three to five hours or even three to five days to fix a train malfunction.

Nevertheless, Li Banfeng didn't actually need to worry too much since he still had cup noodles. He was better off than the other passengers. Most of them hadn't brought extra food.

A wave of panic gradually spread through the train.

At the Dark Star Bureau, Chen Changrui—the captain of the Public Security Team, also known as Old Chen—had on a massive pair of headphones as he half-reclined in his office chair with his eyes closed.

Over an hour later, two ten-centimeter-long shiny silver centipedes crawled out from the outer part of the headphones and lay stiffly on the table. The centipedes were exhausted, and so was Chen Changrui.

As he opened his eyes, he took his headphones off. Then he picked up a pack of cigarettes and pulled one out. Placing it to his mouth, he took a long drag and blew the smoke over the centipedes.

After finishing the cigarette, Chen Changrui looked like he had regained some energy. The two centipedes seemed a little more lively too.

You Xuehan, his secretary, was nearby, awaiting his instructions.

Two hours ago, the Intelligence Department had lost track of Li Banfeng's signal. Left with no other choice, the Public Security Team had resorted to using Chen Changrui's special ability.

Chen Changrui lit another cigarette and said to You Xuehan, "Tangyuan, tell the folks in Intelligence that the issue isn't with their equipment. Li Banfeng's phone is dead."

You Xuehan, code-named Tangyuan, asked, "Captain, isn't there any other way to track him?"

"What other way could there be?" Chen Changrui shook his head. "Train 1160 is untraceable."

You Xuehan thought about it for a moment. "We could try tracking the other passengers who are in the same train car as him."

"There's no need for that," Chen Changrui replied, putting away the two silver centipedes and getting to his feet. "We don't even know when he'll get off the train. For all we know, he might be gone by tomorrow morning."

You Xuehan reminded him, "Captain, the higher-ups are very concerned about the item Li Banfeng has."

Chen Changrui chuckled and lit yet another cigarette. "Li Banfeng took something from Lucky Star, so he's destined to receive three strokes of good luck. His phone dying—that's the first stroke. We know Lucky Star did that. If we keep trying to track Li Banfeng, won't we be openly defying Lucky Star? Why bother?"

You Xuehan was puzzled. "A dead phone counts as good luck?"

"If his phone is dead, those looking for him can't use it to track him anymore. And we're not the only ones after him. There are many people looking for him, and they don't just want that item he's carrying. They want his life."

You Xuehan was taken aback. "Who wants his life?"

Chen Changrui didn't answer; he just smiled mysteriously. He put on his coat and walked to the office door.

Then he turned back and said, "Tangyuan, go home. Rest up for a few days. Tell the others to do the same, especially Lightbulb. Everyone has off days during missions. Tell him to let it go."

"What about Deputy Xiao?" You Xuehan asked cautiously.

"Let him be."

Chen Changrui left it at that and walked out of the office with a cigarette dangling from his mouth.

The southern region of Yuezhou.

Inside a villa, Xiao Zhenggong pushed aside the blonde-haired, blue-eyed woman in his arms and answered the phone.

—Boss, we lost that guy's signal. Why not just have our people take him out on the train?

"We can't make a move on Train 1160!" Xiao Zhenggong furrowed his brows. "Wait until he gets off."

—The train broke down. No idea when it'll be fixed.

"When I say wait, you wait. If anyone makes a move on the train, don't blame the sect rules for being ruthless!"

The call ended there.

The thing on the other end of the line wasn't a phone. Instead, it was a man with an antenna stuck into his head.

The man pulled the antenna out of his skull, wiped the blood off it, and put it away in a case. Then he burst into a tirade.

"Motherfucker, who do you think you are, acting so arrogantly? You've been drifting around in the Outer Provinces for half your life. What the fuck do you know? If it weren't for your old man, there's no fucking way you could ever become the gang leader!"

A man wearing a Gatsby cap[1] stepped over to him and asked, "Hall Master, what now?"

"What else? Find a few quick-witted brothers and go wait for him at the station. Remember, get the goods first, then take his life."

The hall master had a hole in his skull just for plugging in the antenna. He had taken out the antenna, but the hole remained. He would need to use it again in the future.

The hall master grabbed a stopper and plugged the hole. Then he took two strips of tape and crisscrossed them over the plug.

Xiao Zhenggong walked over to the wine cabinet and poured himself a glass of red wine. He took a sip. Then he crushed the wine glass in his hand.

Li Banfeng, you're really lucky. You bought Lucky Star's instant noodles and got three strokes of good luck.

The train broke down. Everyone else has to go hungry, but you have noodles to eat. That's your first stroke of good luck.

Then your phone died, which let you avoid being tracked. That's your second stroke of luck.

What's your third stroke of luck going to be? Could it be the Peddler? But you shouldn't be able to run into him. He's still at Greenwater Bay.

But when will the train be fixed? Could this somehow allow you to meet him?

Xiao Zhenggong picked up another glass and poured himself a shot of liquor.

The blonde woman walked out of the bedroom and placed her hand on Xiao Zhenggong's shoulder.

Speaking in Mandarin with an Eastern Yue accent, she said slowly, "Boss Xiao, has some trouble occurred?"

"There is indeed a bit of trouble." Xiao Zhenggong sighed. "The thing I wanted ended up in Puluo."

The blonde woman kissed Xiao Zhenggong on the cheek. "Something big is about to happen in Puluo. I heard He Haiqin, the head of the He Family, can't hold out for much longer."

Xiao Zhenggong paused in deliberation. Then he said, "Have your younger brother step in and save He Haiqin. Get him out of there first."

The blonde woman smiled. "You know his price won't be cheap."

Xiao Zhenggong took a sip of liquor and pinched her cheek. "The price won't be a problem."

The blonde woman warned him, "You sure about this? Doing that will offend the Lu Family!"

"There's no other way! That asshole Li Banfeng ruined everything!"

Xiao Zhenggong gritted his teeth angrily and then downed the rest of the liquor in his glass.

The He Family Manor, Greenwater Bay, Puluo Province.

He Haiqin was lying in a hospital bed. He forced down a bowl of rice, extending his life just a little bit longer by relying on his cultivation power as a Gourmandism Cultivator. There were pea-sized blisters filled with intensely green pus all over his body. Each one was clear and glistening, seemingly delicate enough to rupture with a single breath.

His wife, Yan Yulin, tended to him with great care.

At this moment, his older sister, He Yuxiu, pushed open the room door, letting in a rush of air.

The blisters on He Haiqin's skin were truly delicate. That rush of air ruptured one of the blisters on his face, causing green pus to trickle down. He Haiqin's face crumpled in pain.

Yan Yulin reproached He Yuxiu, "Sister-in-law, please be gentle."

He Yuxiu sat beside the hospital bed and said to Yan Yulin, "Yulin, something happened to Physician Chu on the way here. I'm afraid he's unlikely to make it today."

"What happened?" Yan Yulin asked in alarm.

He Yuxiu's expression turned grim. "I heard he was intercepted by the Lu Family!"

Yan Yulin's voice shook as she spoke. "What do we do now? Jiaqing's not back yet. Sister-in-law, you have to think of something!"

He Haiqin had been tormented by these strange blisters for two whole years. He had been relying entirely on the medicine from Chu Peiying, a Medicine Cultivator, to keep the blisters at bay. However, his condition suddenly worsened today. Now that Chu Peiying was stranded somewhere, that meant there wasn't much hope left for He Haiqin.

He Yuxiu turned to Yan Yulin and said, "Yulin, the Jiangxiang Gang sent over a physician. Word is, he's pretty capable. He's waiting outside. Why not let him give it a try?"

Yan Yulin's eyes lit up with a glimmer of hope. "Who is the physician?"

He Yuxiu lowered her voice and replied, "He's not well known, but he's the real deal. His name is Cedric."

He Haiqin suddenly asked, "What's that name?"

"Cedric," He Yuxiu answered cautiously.

"A Westerner?" He Haiqin scowled in displeasure. "I don't want to see him!"

1. Gatsby caps, also known as newsboy caps, were popular in China during the 1920s-1930s, when there was greater Western influence in clothing choices. ☜

Chapter 12: Absolutely Delicious

Like how He Jiaqing despised the international students at his university, He Haiqin despised foreigners, particularly Westerners. In this regard, the father and son were shockingly alike.

He Yuxiu held He Haiqin's hand and tried to persuade him. "Little Brother, listen to me. This doctor is truly skilled. Xiao Zhenggong sent him. Just let him take a look."

He Haiqin shoved his older sister aside. "Get out!"

He Yuxiu turned to Yan Yulin. "Yulin, talk some sense into him!"

Yan Yulin took a deep breath and summoned her courage. "My dear, I've always obeyed you. Just this once, let me make the decision. Sister-in-law, go ahead and bring in the physician!"

He Yuxiu got up to bring the physician into the room. He Haiqin was furious, but he didn't have the strength to stop her.

Soon after, Cedric, a blond-haired, blue-eyed physician with a high-bridged nose, appeared at the door. When he saw the blisters all over He Haiqin's body, he was incredibly shocked.

Yan Yulin quickly explained, "Physician, please don't be afraid. These blisters aren't contagious. Our family has taken care of him for two years, and no one has ever been infected."

"Fucking foreign devil!" He Haiqin shot Cedric a glare. Then he turned to Yan Yulin. "What are you even saying all that for? You think this damn foreign devil can understand you?"

"I can!" Cedric replied in fluent Mandarin. "Not all foreign devils are bad. I'm a good foreign devil. I love Puluo."

He Haiqin forced a smile. "You've got quite the fucking mouth on you."

"It's not just talk," Cedric said as he unbuttoned his shirt, revealing his hairy chest. Five Chinese characters were tattooed on it. They translated to: I love Puluo.

Yan Yulin covered her eyes.

He Yuxiu chimed in from the side, "Wow, would you look at that! He's so sincere that he tattooed it right over his heart!"

He Haiqin turned his head away, unwilling to look at Cedric. "My home doesn't have any of that fancy equipment you Westerners use. Just leave."

Cedric shook his head and smiled. "Good sir, there is no need to worry. I don't use that equipment anyway. I am a Medicine Cultivator. I practice Puluo's ancient medical arts. Let me check your pulse first."

Cedric took He Haiqin's pulse earnestly. He Haiqin did his best to suppress his hostility, but he was unwilling to even look at Cedric.

After a few minutes, Cedric appeared to have reached a diagnosis. He picked up his satchel, pulled out a silver needle, and sterilized it over a candle flame. With it, he gently pierced one of He Haiqin's blisters.

Thick green pus oozed out. Cedric dipped a finger into the pus, brought it to his nose, and sniffed it. Then he licked his finger and tasted the pus.

Earlier, Yan Yulin had stressed that the blisters weren't contagious. Even so, watching Cedric put the pus-covered finger tip into his mouth made her retch uncontrollably.

And it wasn't just her. Even He Haiqin, the person whose pus it was, felt nauseated.

He Yuxiu had stronger nerves. Her cheek twitched slightly as she said, "Little Brother, look how dedicated Physician Cedric is! He just put the pus in his mouth! He's even savoring the taste!"

"Urk!" He Haiqin threw up.

Gourmandism Cultivators were not supposed to vomit. It was considered a grave insult to the food. However, He Haiqin simply couldn't hold it back.

Cedric's tongue was still moving, carefully savoring the taste of the pus.

A few minutes later, Cedric nodded. "I believe I've found the cause of this illness."

He Yuxiu blinked in surprise. "Let's hear it."

Cedric explained, "This illness does not exist naturally in this world. Someone created it."

He Haiqin was astonished and finally turned to look at Cedric. "You know who did this to me?"

He had long suspected the Lu Family was behind it.

He Yuxiu's eyes widened. "Was it those bastards from the Lu Family?!"

Cedric shook his head. "I don't know who it was, but I can find a cure. Good sir, please grant me a private room so that I may prepare the medicine for you."

He Haiqin's brows furrowed slightly. He wasn't keen on taking some Westerner's mystery drug.

Cedric knew what He Haiqin was worried about. "Please rest assured, good sir. I will remain in your residence until you recover. If anything goes wrong, you may kill me at any time."

He Haiqin glanced at Yan Yulin. She then gave the steward a look, and he left to arrange a private room for Cedric.

Less than half an hour later, Cedric emerged from the private room with a test tube containing a white powder.

"Good sir, please take this medicine. Once you do, you will have a swift recovery."

As He Haiqin stared at the white powder, his cheek twitched. He didn't want to take that medicine. He didn't trust Westerners. But even if he didn't take it, he would only last a few more hours at most in his current state.

Yan Yulin took the test tube. He Haiqin hesitated for a moment but ultimately nodded.

Yan Yulin poured the powder into He Haiqin's mouth. The inside of his throat was covered in blisters too, making it extremely difficult for him to swallow. That was why he hadn't been able to eat. Strangely, he managed to swallow the powder without water.

Cedric stood beside the hospital bed, looking upright and honest. He was making it clear he wouldn't leave.

He Haiqin had been on the verge of death, yet an hour later, he was able to sit up slowly. He was sweating profusely, and his blisters had subsided a little.

Yan Yulin's face lit up with joy.

He Yuxiu praised Cedric to the high heavens. "Little Brother, look how incredible Physician Cedric is! Just one dose, and you're already getting better!"

Cedric gave a small smile. "Good sir, do you believe me now?"

He Haiqin snorted coldly. "The medicine of foreign devils is always like this. Treats the symptoms but not the root cause!"

Cedric shook his head. "I will stay in your residence until you've recovered completely."

He Haiqin shot Cedric a sideways glance. "How much did Xiao Zhenggong pay you?"

Cedric held his hands up. "That's something I can't tell you. It's a matter of principle. Boss Xiao insisted I must cure you and said money is not an issue."

He Haiqin frowned. "Just tell me how much. Stop beating around the bush!"

Feeling helpless, Cedric gave in. "Boss Xiao gave me ten thousand silver dollars[1]."

He Haiqin nodded and said to the steward, "Give him ten thousand silver dollars and have him return to his room to rest."

Cedric shook his head. "There's no need to pay me. Boss Xiao already—"

"This is a reward from me! Just go!"

He Haiqin did admire Cedric's medical skill, but that didn't change his dislike for Westerners.

After Cedric left, He Haiqin told his wife, "Yulin, fetch pen and paper. I want to write my will."

"My dear, what are you saying?!" Yan Yulin wanted to cover He Haiqin's mouth to stop him from talking. "You're all better now, yet you're saying such inauspicious things."

"I don't trust that foreign devil. I might just be having a burst of vitality before my death. Quickly, go fetch pen and paper. I'll speak, you'll write. Elder Sister, be my witness."

He Haiqin was so insistent on writing his will that no one dared to oppose the idea. Once Yan Yulin was ready to write, the first thing he declared was to assign He Jiaqing as the new head of the family.

He Yuxiu spoke from the side. "Little Brother, I have to remind you that Jiaqing is still in a coma. We don't even know if he'll wake up."

He Haiqin shook his head. "I heard from Third Brother that Jiaqing's condition isn't that bad."

He Yuxiu snorted. "You're the only one who trusts Third Brother. Do you even have any idea what he's thinking? If something happens to you and Jiaqing, there's no way your wife and I can fight him. The He Family would fall right into his hands. For all we know, he might be the one behind what happened to Jiaqing!"

"Elder Sister, y-y-you—"

He Haiqin suddenly gasped for breath as the blisters on his body burst one after another without pause. Yan Yulin hurried to support him.

"Haiqin, I was just saying nonsense. Don't frighten me like that, Little Brother!"

He Yuxiu was about to run out to find Cedric, but He Haiqin stopped her. "Elder Sister… I'm hungry. Get me something to eat…"

Hungry? Haiqin's hungry!

He Haiqin was a Gourmandism Cultivator, so being able to eat again meant he had survived the ordeal.

He Yuxiu was overjoyed. "Physician Cedric is amazing! Just one dose of his medicine, and you pulled through!"

Was the medicine… effective? No, not at all. It was useless, completely useless! That white powder was no different from the dirt on the ground!

Sitting inside the guest room, Cedric stuck out his tongue in front of a mirror. There was a green blister the size of a pea on his tongue.

The thing that cured He Haiqin wasn't the medicine. The cure was Cedric transferring all of He Haiqin's blisters onto himself.

The moment Cedric licked the pus from the blister, the disease had already begun transferring to him. That was when He Haiqin started to recover, which was why he'd been able to swallow the medicine.

Cedric was not a Medicine Cultivator. He was one of the rarest kinds of cultivators in the world—a Disease Cultivator!

As he stared at his tongue, a smile spread across his face.

What a perfect blister! Who created this? Looks like there's still someone else on the same cultivation path as me.

I have to find him… and kill him!

Cedric let out a maniacal laugh. "Heheheheheh!"

He laughed so hard that the blister on his tongue burst.

The thick green pus choked him, sending him into a coughing fit.

"Cough, cough, cough!"

After the coughing fit ended, Cedric smacked his lips.

With a loud slurp, he swallowed every drop of the pus and exclaimed in genuine admiration, "Absolutely delicious!"

Girdle-Slipping Hollow, Puluo Province.

Train 1160 was still where it had stopped in the wilderness.

From time to time, noises rang out from the train cars. Passengers were screaming and fighting.

Meanwhile, Li Banfeng unzipped his backpack and carefully took inventory of his remaining food, one item at a time.

1. Foreign silver dollar coins (大洋), mainly Spanish, were introduced to China in the 16th century and used as part of everyday money because of global trade. From 1890 onward, China produced its own silver coins, starting with the Silver Dragon coin and then various coins featuring the faces of military leaders, the most one being the Yuan Shikai coin. China eventually phased out the use of silver coins in the 1930s in favour of paper money. | Pretty interesting stuff, but anyway, I assume 10k silver foreign dollars would be an incredibly massive sum of money during that time. ☜

Chapter 13: He Jiaqing's Treasure

With his phone dead, no data, and the train broken down, Li Banfeng simply sat in the carriage in silence.

Li Banfeng just felt a little bored, but the other passengers had it much worse.

In the morning, the other passengers could still hold on. At noon, they were barely hanging in there. By nightfall, they couldn't endure any longer.

There was a certain kind of person for whom a single day without food could be fatal. They were called Gourmandism Cultivators.

"To hell with your ancestors! Let me off the train! I'm starving to death!" a Gourmandism Cultivator shouted as he wrestled with an attendant.

Another Gourmandism Cultivator charged forward as he yelled, "Don't give me that crap about rules! I need food, and I'm not dying here!"

Though the scuffle sounded fierce, Li Banfeng could tell the attendants were holding back and keeping a reserved attitude.

"Sir, please return to your compartment."

Thud! A dull smack echoed, and the passenger no longer sounded as agitated as before.

"Sir, the train hasn't reached the next station. You can't get off halfway."

Thud! Another dull smack, and the other passenger seemed to calm down as well.

"Sir, the entire crew extends our deepest apologies."

Thud! A third muffled sound, and the last passenger fell completely silent.

The train attendants escorted the passengers back to their compartments and shut the doors one by one.

Clang! Clang!

On the first day alone, Li Banfeng heard at least six such incidents, which still wasn't all that many. Aside from the Gourmandism Cultivators, the other passengers could go a day without eating.

By dusk on the second day, things began to spiral out of control.

"Big bro, you are so handsome and kind. Just let me off for a moment, I will be right back!"

"Ma'am, the train hasn't reached the next station. You can't get off halfway."

"Come on, big bro, just five minutes. No, less than five!"

"Ma'am, these are the rules, and I follow the rules."

"Rules are dead, but people are alive. I really like men like you. Can't you just give me five minutes?"

"Thank you for the compliment, ma'am, but please return to your compartment at once."

"Motherfucker! I want to get off this train now! I can't starve to death here! I will fight you!" Clang! Clang!

"AHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH! SOMEBODY HELP! HE'S HITTING A WOMAN! ANY PULUO PERSON WITH BALLS BETWEEN THEIR LEGS? FIGHT HIM!!!!" Puluo Province! This is Puluo Province! Li Banfeng thought as he slurped his instant noodles. He really wanted to see what it actually looked like.

That night, after thirteen separate brawls, the attendants still managed to persuade all the passengers to go back to their compartments.

By the third day, things had actually calmed down, not because they didn't want to stir up trouble but because they no longer had the strength to do so. After three days without food, few had the energy to make a scene, and even if they did, they could not afford to waste it. They knew they were no match for the attendants.

Li Banfeng had stayed in his compartment the whole time. He barely moved, and two packs of instant noodles a day were enough to get by. However, this could not go on forever.

By nighttime, he had only five packs of noodles left, and he was starting to get nervous.

Just how long is this train going to stay stopped?

Knock knock knock!

A knock came at the door, making Li Banfeng raise an eyebrow and go on guard. He had gone hungry before, and he knew that hunger could drive people to do anything.

The knocking was soft, and the voice at the door sounded very gentle. "Hey, friend in there. Could you spare a bite of food? Just one bite. I will not eat it myself. I can still endure. I just want to give my wife and kid something."

Li Banfeng stood a step away from the door, holding his strip of latiao and a vacuum flask, waiting to see what the person at the other side of the door would do next.

The man outside did not make a move, and his tone stayed polite. "Friend, I know how precious food is. I will not ask for it for free. I will buy it."

Buy? In times like this, how much money would it take to get food? Li Banfeng thought.

The man paused, as if realizing how foolish he sounded, then hurried to correct himself. "I'll trade for it. Please… I'm begging you."

Li Banfeng finally spoke up. "If you already have food, why trade?"

"Because my kid's too scared to eat it."

"What is it?"

"I'll give it to you first. Just crack the door open a little."

Li Banfeng's heart pounded. After a brief silence, he turned the lock and eased the door open a crack.

This wasn't an act of recklessness but of caution. He had no intention of letting the man inside, yet he wanted to give him a chance to speak. Even without seeing his face, Banfeng could sense something unusual in his aura. Every word the man spoke sounded humble, yet a calm steadiness lay beneath that humility.

As an orphan who had faced bullies all his life, Li Banfeng had dealt with all kinds of people. He had a strong hunch this man could get into his compartment without any of the attendants noticing. This door would not stop him. Rather than hunker down behind it, it was better to show a little sincerity.

Through the gap, the man handed him the food first.

The moment Li Banfeng saw it, he quietly praised himself, Li Banfeng, you are a brave man. You didn't even scream!

It was a bluish-black thing, part flesh and part bare bone. Judging by the arrangement and number of bones, it was a human hand.

Li Banfeng asked, "Whose hand is this?"

The man replied calmly, "My own."

Li Banfeng stared at the hand, which was nearly gnawed in half, then turned and picked up two cups of instant noodles. He passed them through the door.

This was clearly more than the man had expected. He had not dared hope for even one pack, and he had truly only asked for a single bite.

"I don't need this much… You really don't have to give me this much…"

"Just take it. And take your hand too," Li Banfeng said, passing back the severed hand. "Find a hospital. Maybe they can reattach it. They probably can."

Li Banfeng locked the door. From outside came a stream of grateful words. "Thank you. Thank you. Thank you…"

The voice was still deep and polite, but the composure was gone. It trembled, as if the man were on the verge of tears.

As the voice faded into the distance, Li Banfeng returned to his bunk, opened his backpack, and took stock of his remaining food. He had three packs of instant noodles, one bag of chips, and one and a half packs of spicy sticks. It was enough for two days, maybe three if he cut back on each meal.

At dawn, a train whistle blared, rousing the sluggish passengers from sleep. The train was moving again.

Toooot! Tooooooooot!

As Li Banfeng listened to the steady rhythm of the steam engine, he felt that he was listening to the most beautiful music in the world.

Rumble!

The train suddenly jolted so hard that Li Banfeng was thrown from his bunk.

His instant noodles, chips, latiao, and backpack scattered across the floor.

Rumble! Rumble!

The train kept shaking. Thinking that the train was about to derail again, Li Banfeng curled up in a corner, protecting his vital areas with all his strength.

After a bout of turbulence, the train gradually steadied, and Li Banfeng heard the announcer's voice.

「The train has cleared all faults and is now traveling safely. Please refrain from moving about during this bumpy section.」

Li Banfeng let out a long sigh of relief, then hurried to gather his food.

The instant noodles, chips, and spicy sticks were all still intact. But his backpack was gone! And He Jiaqing's package was still inside!

Li Banfeng panicked and began searching frantically.

The power had not returned, leaving the compartment pitch-black. After feeling around for a while, he finally located the backpack under the bunk.

The space beneath the bed was narrow. Li Banfeng reached in and tugged hard, pulling the backpack out, only to find the package inside had gotten wedged beneath the frame.

He had pulled too hard and forgotten that the backpack's zipper was open. He reached back in for the package, but it was tightly stuck.

Shoving his entire arm underneath, he gave it a hard yank.

The package came free. However, the outer cardboard box was torn, and the bubble wrap inside had burst open.

Is the item inside still intact? Li Banfeng muttered inwardly as he held the package and walked over to the window.

Since the package was already torn, he figured he might as well open it. If the item inside was damaged, he would need to let Jiaqing know.

Using the flickering light seeping in around the edges of the curtain, Li Banfeng unwrapped the package. He took out the cardboard box, then the bubble wrap, and found another layer of aluminum foil inside.

Li Banfeng peeled off the foil to reveal yellow paper, then oiled parchment, and finally a wrapping of silk. When he removed the silk, he finally laid eyes on the item within.

What is this… thing? Li Banfeng wondered.

In the dim light, he could not tell at first glance. All he could tell was that it looked like a flower. It seemed to be a lotus flower cast in bronze.

Chapter 14: Heavenly Light

It was a bronze lotus flower with lotus leaves forming its base and a flower bud resting on top. The flower measured about ten centimeters across. Though it had yet to bloom, he could still feel the gaps between its petals.

I see! So it's a solid bronze piece. No wonder it's so heavy! Li Banfeng exclaimed inwardly. He wasn't an expert in antiques, but it was obvious to him that this was an exquisite ornament.

But is this ornament really that important? Why did Jiaqing insist that I take it back to his hometown? Why would Deputy Xiao want me dead over it? What is the secret of this lotus?

Having slept well over the past few days and with nothing else to do, Li Banfeng devoted himself to studying the bronze lotus flower. From dawn until two in the afternoon, he examined it without pause, yet he still couldn't open it.

Crackle~

The announcer's voice echoed through the speakers.

「The train is approaching Medicine King Gully Station. For passengers getting off at this station, please gather your belongings and prepare to disembark.」

We're here! Medicine King Gully! Li Banfeng thought excitedly.

He quickly gathered the silk, yellow paper, and other items, wrapped the bronze lotus securely, and stuffed it into his backpack. He added the remaining three cups of instant noodles, a can of chips, and half a pack of latiao, then pushed the door open and stepped out of the compartment.

Since boarding, this was the first time he had left his compartment. It was also the first time he had seen any other passengers on the train. Li Banfeng felt a little nervous, worried he might run into some three-headed creature or other strange beings.

A number of people were waiting by the door to disembark, and they all looked normal, at least on the surface. It seemed that some of them should not have gotten off at this stop, but they had no choice because they were going to starve to death.

A man in a suit stood next to Li Banfeng. He kept his left hand in his pocket and carried his suitcase with the other hand. Li Banfeng glanced at him and guessed he was at least 1.9 meters tall but weighed less than fifty kilograms. With such a scrawny frame, a strong gust of wind might really blow him away.

A woman followed behind him. Her physique was the exact opposite of the man's. She looked to be under 1.4 meters tall but weighed over seventy kilograms.

When the train jolted, the man quickly reached out to support the woman beside him. From their closeness and the way they moved, it was clear they were husband and wife.

The woman held a child who looked to be about one or two years old, though it was hard to tell which parent the child took after.

Screeeech!

Tooooooooooooooot!

With a long whistle, the train pulled into Medicine King Gully Station.

The attendant lowered the steps, and Li Banfeng joined the crowd as they disembarked.

The moment he stepped off the train, Li Banfeng felt slightly dizzy from the rush of fresh air. Though it carried the scent of coal dust and engine oil, it was still far better than the air inside the train.

The weather was a bit gloomy, but it was still better than the dim yellow light inside the compartment, which had gone out entirely in the past few days.

After stepping off the train, the first thing Li Banfeng noticed was how the people around him were dressed.

They were dressed very differently from him. Many wore suits in every style imaginable, including black, white, striped, and checkered. There were also many wearing Zhongshan suits[1] with either the turn-down or upright collar. Some wore vests over their shirts, with bowties fastened at the collar. Others wore changshan—traditional Chinese long gowns, the kind often seen on performers of crosstalk, storytelling, folk singing, clapper talk, or traditional opera.

Most people wore hats of various styles. Fedoras were common, but there were also plenty of baseball caps. Some wore hats that looked like flat caps but with slightly narrower brims and a button on top of the visor.
