It wouldn't have been so bad if he were just a normal Dark Ability User, but he had even begun cultivating two completely incompatible Daoist paths simultaneously.

What should I do next?

Return to Yuezhou?

Go find Jiaqing, who is lying comatose in a hospital bed?

But how am I supposed to buy a train ticket?

Li Banfeng had left his wallet, dress shirt, dress pants, and leather shoes behind in the He Family's old residence. There was no way he could go back for them.

This was everything he owned: a fedora, a hiking hat, a set of pajamas, a T-shirt, a pair of sweatpants, a pair of sneakers, two cups of instant noodles, a can of chips, a little more than one pack of latiao, and He Jiaqing's bronze lotus flower.

Should I sell the bronze lotus flower to get a train ticket back to Yuezhou?

But how much is the bronze lotus flower worth? It doesn't seem right to sell it as scrap metal.

But if I don't sell it, then what will I eat?

I don't have a single yuan.

Li Banfeng slept the night away in the Pocket Dwelling.

It was very quiet inside the Pocket Dwelling; there was almost no sound at all. It didn't have any windows, but the air seemed clean and fresh. Aside from a musty scent from the many years' worth of accumulated dust, there were no unpleasant odors.

That meant neither of those things woke Li Banfeng. Instead, it was his hunger and thirst.

He had only eaten one cup of instant noodles yesterday and hadn't drunk any water.

Once again, he took out a cup of instant noodles, but he didn't know how he was going to swallow it.

There was no way he could eat it dry this time.

Li Banfeng stepped out of the Pocket Dwelling. Upon returning to the alley, he retrieved his key and stored it away. Then he knocked on the door of a nearby house.

He had grown up in an orphanage and endured plenty of hardship… but he still found it difficult to ask for water from a stranger.

"Madam, could I trouble you for a bowl of water? I don't need any food, just water."

Li Banfeng repeatedly emphasized that he didn't want food, and the woman only gave him a bowl of water as requested. However, the hostile look in her eyes made him feel like his face was being stabbed by dozens of needles.

He was only asking for water now, but how much longer could he last before he would be begging for food?

Li Banfeng only had two cups of instant noodles left.

He had to find a way to make a living.

A Wanderlust Cultivator had to traverse at least ten kilometers a day. After eating a block of uncooked instant noodles, Li Banfeng wandered down the streets of the Inner Gully.

"Extra! Extra! The Chu Family's second daughter tried to kill her husband on their wedding night and fled in fear of punishment! The husband's family is offering a reward of three million! Sir, buy a paper! We've got a fresh headline today. It's big news! Just fifty mao!"

Fifty mao… I don't even have that.

Li Banfeng smiled as he looked at the paperboy.

Maybe I should sell newspapers with you.

However, it wasn't easy to get a job selling newspapers. Many kids were already in this line of work and relied on it to survive.

Li Banfeng had no idea what the job market was like for this line of work, nor did he know the tricks to getting people to buy newspapers. As far as he knew, newspapers had already faded from the public eye more than ten years ago.

In town, Li Banfeng found two shops that were hiring day laborers. One was a furniture store, and the other was a rice shop.

He didn't ask for high pay. If he could do some odd jobs for a few days until he had earned enough to buy a train ticket, he would be happy with that.

However, neither of the shops hired him… because he was dressed too strangely.

To Li Banfeng, pairing a short-sleeved T-shirt with a fedora didn't seem that strange, but to the shopkeepers, he didn't look like someone trustworthy.

By noon, his stomach was growling again. He begged for a bowl of water from another stranger and ate his last block of uncooked instant noodles. Now, all he had left was a can of potato chips.

This is hard, truly hard.

Since he could not find a job in the Inner Gully, Li Banfeng walked all the way to the edge of town and headed toward the Outer Gully.

The Outer Gully was much larger than the Inner Gully, and it was a much more complicated place.

Walking along a dirt road, Li Banfeng saw farmers working in the fields. He also saw short houses and stretches of mountain forests where not a single person could be seen for several kilometers.

By the time dusk arrived, Li Banfeng had long since digested the last block of instant noodles, and he had eaten the potato chips too.

The only good news was that Li Banfeng felt tremors in his head.

Cultivators felt special sensations when they made progress in their cultivation.

The tremors indicated he had met his daily requirement of walking ten kilometers for his fundamental cultivation as a Wanderlust Cultivator.

The indicative sensation for Homebound Cultivators was much milder. Whenever Li Banfeng stayed inside the Pocket Dwelling for two hours, he would feel a refreshing and pleasant coolness wash over him.

Li Banfeng was approaching a small restaurant, which had just five tables inside a dark-tiled room. There was a notice pasted at the entrance. It said they were hiring help. He decided to try his luck.

Right then, someone inside the restaurant called out, "Brother Li, is that you?"

Li Banfeng turned around and saw a stout and strong young man slurping noodles. There were three full bowls and three empty ones on the table in front of him.

Li Banfeng froze in surprise. Chubby Qin!

Chubby Qin waved him over. "Brother Li, come sit! It seems like fate's tying us together, huh? You like this place too? I love it. The food's cheap, and the portions are generous."

Li Banfeng didn't bother pretending. "Brother Qin, to be honest, I'm not here to eat. I saw the hiring notice. I'm here looking for work."

Chubby Qin blinked. "You're looking for work at a restaurant? Why?"

Li Banfeng's face twisted in agony. "I don't have a single coin left. If I don't find work, I'll be begging for food soon!"

Chubby Qin was a loyal and generous person. He patted his chest and said, "Waiter, two more bowls of noodles! Brother Li, if you don't mind, let's eat first!"

Li Banfeng felt a bit embarrassed. They had only met by chance. What right did he have to accept a free meal from Chubby Qin?

"Not eating means you're looking down on me!" Chubby Qin exclaimed. He dragged over a chair and pulled Li Banfeng into it.

A moment later, two steaming bowls of plain noodles in broth were brought to the table.

As a Gourmandism Cultivator, eating 2.5 kilograms of rice a day and five bowls of noodles per meal was part of Chubby Qin's fundamental cultivation.

Li Banfeng was starving. The noodles had no meat. There was only half a bowl of broth, a spoonful of soy sauce, and two leaves of baby bok choy. Nevertheless, to him, it was the most delicious meal he had ever had.

He devoured the two bowls of noodles in a flash.

After paying the bill, Chubby Qin said, "Brother Li, too many eyes around here. Let's find a place to talk."

Chubby Qin and Li Banfeng left the restaurant and found a quiet spot near the woods.

Chubby Qin lowered his voice and asked, "Brother, have you been initiated yet?"

Li Banfeng nodded. "I have."

Chubby Qin lowered his voice even more. "Which path did you take?"

Chubby Qin was a genuinely good person, and Li Banfeng already knew what Daoist path he followed. Li Banfeng felt like he'd be doing the guy a disservice if he were to lie now.

"I chose the path of a Wanderlust Cultivator."

He didn't mention the Homebound Cultivator part. It was hard to explain and not something he should reveal easily.

Chubby Qin scratched his cheek. "Logically, you should have plenty of options as a Wanderlust Cultivator. You could be a courier, a guard, a bodyguard… At worst, you could pull a rickshaw."

Li Banfeng smiled. He had genuinely considered pulling a rickshaw.

As a Wanderlust Cultivator, he'd be classified as a rickshaw puller with fire wheels. A single trip could earn him several hundred yuan, and it would be pretty easy work for him.

And when he put on his hiking hat, he really did give off the vibe of a rickshaw driver!

However, to pull a rickshaw, Li Banfeng needed a rickshaw, and of course, he didn't have one.

He could rent one, but he would need to put down a deposit, and he didn't have the money for that.

Additionally, he needed to have a permit to work as a rickshaw driver, but there was no way he could get one on short notice.

Most importantly, rickshaw drivers had to know the roads, but Li Banfeng didn't.

As a Wanderlust Cultivator, Li Banfeng had an incredible sense of direction, but he didn't know the names of places. He clearly did not meet even the basic requirements of being a rickshaw driver.

Seeing that Li Banfeng remained silent, Chubby Qin smiled awkwardly and said, "My bad. I'm not very good with words. What kind of men are we, anyway? There's no way you should be doing a job like rickshaw pulling.

"Brother Li, I've got a small business opportunity. It won't make you rich, but it'll cover your immediate needs. It just depends on whether you're willing to take the risk."

"Brother, I was just at a restaurant looking for work. I'm hardly in a position to be picky. But you said it's a business opportunity. I've got no money for the capital."

"Not needed. This is a business opportunity that requires no capital whatsoever. The only capital investment we need is our lives. Ever heard of Mount Bitterfog?"

Mount Bitterfog? Li Banfeng thought about it for a moment and shook his head. "Never heard of it."

Chubby Qin said, "In Medicine King Gully, everyone knows about Mount Bitterfog. That place is shrouded in thick fog all year round. That fog is highly toxic. If an ordinary person were to try climbing that mountain, they would end up dead for sure."

Li Banfeng frowned. That sounds like a naturally occurring gas chamber.

He asked, "What kind of business opportunity could we possibly have there?"

"There's a plant that grows on Mount Bitterfog. It's the speckled-snake chrysanthemum. Have you heard of it?"

Li Banfeng shook his head. He definitely hadn't.

Chubby Qin spoke a little louder. "The speckled-snake chrysanthemum blooms once a year, just for a single day. It's the main ingredient in Speckled-Snake Pills. Taking one pill gives you one day's worth of cultivation progress. I told you this, didn't I? That it normally takes ten years of cultivation to advance to the next level. But if you take one Speckled-Snake Pill a day, you'll only need five years.

"The speckled-snake chrysanthemums bloom the day after tomorrow. A few medicinal trading houses have already named their price. They'll pay eighty-five yuan per stalk. If you're lucky, you can usually find two or three speckled-snake chrysanthemums per hour. That means if you spend ten hours gathering the flowers, you could find twenty to thirty stalks, which would get you around two thousand yuan.

"You're a Wanderlust Cultivator, so you've got great stamina. If you spend ten hours on the mountain, you could probably gather enough to sell for three thousand yuan, maybe even five thousand. How about it?"

Three thousand yuan was more than enough for a train ticket. Li Banfeng could even enjoy a few carefree days once he returned to Yuezhou.

This is a great business opportunity!

Li Banfeng was about to agree, but he paused, realizing there was an important point he had forgotten. "Hang on, if the fog is toxic, wouldn't it be suicide to go up there and gather the flowers?"

Chubby Qin grinned. "For ordinary folks, yes. But we're not ordinary! We're cultivators. Our bodies are far tougher than those of ordinary folks. You're a Wanderlust Cultivator. You could easily last ten hours up there. If you grit your teeth, you might even make it fifteen. After you descend from the mountain, just spend a day vomiting. Clear everything from your system. Then rest for two more days, and you'll be fine. As for me, I'm a Gourmandism Cultivator. With a full stomach, I could easily last twenty hours up there."

This business opportunity really does take your life as the capital investment!

Li Banfeng thought about the profits and nodded. "Brother, I'm in."

Chubby Qin laughed. "You've got speed, I've got endurance. We're going to earn bucket loads with this business!"

Chapter 27: Buying a Weapon

After Li Banfeng and Chubby Qin had finalized their business deal, they made preparations to head up Mount Bitterfog to gather speckled-snake chrysanthemum.

That night, Chubby Qin went home to pack a bag and invited Li Banfeng to stay over. Li Banfeng politely declined.

As a Homebound Cultivator, he had to spend at least two hours a day inside his home. That was the most basic cultivation rule of Homebound Cultivation. With Qiao Yuesheng's fate as a cautionary tale, Li Banfeng didn't dare break the rules.

They agreed to meet at the noodle shop at eight the next morning. However, Li Banfeng had no watch or any sort of timekeeping tool. By the time he arrived, it was nearly noon.

He couldn't be blamed for oversleeping. In the Pocket Dwelling, there was no sense of day or night, and the sleep quality was just too good.

Chubby Qin was quite anxious. "Brother Li, why are you so late? We've got to hurry. We need to stop by the Inner Gully first."

"The Inner Gully? What for?"

"To buy something useful."

Li Banfeng was puzzled. "Aren't we just gathering herbs on the mountain? Aren't spades and sacks enough? We need to go to the Inner Gully to buy those?"

Chubby Qin shook his head. "There's no time. I'll explain once we get there."

He treated Li Banfeng to two bowls of plain noodles, while Chubby Qin ate eight. Brimming with energy, the two young men ran over to the Inner Gully.

Li Banfeng was running at only thirty percent of his maximum speed, but Chubby Qin was struggling to keep up with him. As a Gourmandism Cultivator, he just needed to eat until he was full, and his speed and stamina would far surpass that of ordinary people. Yet, his improved speed was barely fast enough to keep up with a Wanderlust Cultivator.

Previously, Li Banfeng had taken most of the day to walk from the Inner Gully to the Outer Gully. However, now that they were running from the Outer Gully to the Inner Gully, it took them less than two hours to reach it.

Chubby Qin was so out of breath that he couldn't speak. He led Li Banfeng straight to Memorial Arch Street.

When they arrived, Li Banfeng grew a little tense. The He Family's old residence was nearby, and Lu Xiaolan was still inside the house.

Nevertheless, Chubby Qin knew nothing about what had happened at the He Family's old residence. His destination was Feng's General Store.

When Li Banfeng and Chubby Qin entered Feng's General Store, its owner was dusting the shelves with a feather duster.

He hurriedly greeted them with a friendly smile. "Trader Qin, it's been a while. And this gentleman, I see that he is under your care as well."

He recognized Chubby Qin, and he also recognized Li Banfeng, who was still wearing the fedora he had purchased from this very store.

Chubby Qin wasn't in the mood to exchange pleasantries. He got straight to the point. "Shopkeeper Feng, got anything good? I'm rushing to leave on a business trip."

It seemed that whatever Chubby Qin was referring to wasn't meant to be discussed at the counter. Shopkeeper Feng called over his assistant to watch the store.

Then he turned to Chubby Qin and Li Banfeng and said, "Please, come into the back room for some tea."

Li Banfeng and Chubby Qin followed Shopkeeper Feng into the back room. The back room had even more shelves than the front, and they were all stacked to the brim, making the space feel extremely cramped. If not for the small tea table in the center, the room would've been indistinguishable from a storehouse.

The three of them sat around the tea table.

As he poured the tea, Shopkeeper Feng lowered his voice and asked, "Pardon my curiosity, but may I ask what you will be doing on that business trip?"

"Gathering flowers on Mount Bitterfog," Chubby Qin answered frankly.

Shopkeeper Feng considered it for a moment, then he pulled a small sickle from one of the shelves.

The blade of the sickle was no more than ten centimeters long. The handle was even shorter; it was just long enough for a hand to wrap around it.

Chubby Qin stared at the dainty little sickle for a while before saying, "Shopkeeper Feng, what am I supposed to do with this? You can't even harvest wheat with this thing."

Shopkeeper Feng was just as flabbergasted. "Didn't you say you're going up Mount Bitterfog to gather flowers? This sickle's perfect for that."

Chubby Qin furrowed his eyebrows. "I treated you like a friend and was honest with you, yet you're treating me like a fool. You think I'd need to come all the way here just to buy a sickle?"

With an exasperated expression, Shopkeeper Feng replied, "Trader Qin, you said you're going to gather flowers, so I brought out a tool for gathering flowers. What exactly did I do wrong?"

Chubby Qin grew agitated. "Do you truly not understand, or are you just pretending? Do you really think the flowers on Mount Bitterfog can be gathered so easily?"

Shopkeeper Feng shook his head repeatedly. "Then I really don't understand. If you want this sickle, take it. If not, then forget it."

As the two men argued, Li Banfeng came to a realization.

Chubby Qin didn't actually know much about the things that occurred on Mount Bitterfog. He also knew about the jianghu's rules, but his knowledge was very limited.

Li Banfeng had grown up in an orphanage and made it through university by working part-time jobs. He had met many people and seen many things, so it only took one glance for him to figure out that the real old hand here was Shopkeeper Feng.

Li Banfeng quickly stepped in to de-escalate the situation. "Brother, I don't understand either. What exactly are you trying to buy?"

Chubby Qin frowned. "A weapon!"

"What do you need a weapon for?"

Chubby Qin explained it in one sentence. "It's easy to gather the flowers, but descending the mountain is a whole other story."

That one sentence revealed everything he'd left unsaid.

Speckled-snake chrysanthemums were extremely valuable. Many people were likely heading up the mountain to gather the flowers, but just as many might be looking to steal them. Chubby Qin clearly wanted to buy a weapon for self-protection.

Of course, Shopkeeper Feng understood what Chubby Qin meant. He replied with one sentence as well. "It's easy to come down the mountain with a sickle, but do that with the Azure Dragon Saber, and the road gets rough."

Li Banfeng asked, "What do you mean by that?"

Shopkeeper Feng smiled. "A sickle belongs to a farmer. The Azure Dragon Saber belongs to Emperor Guan[1]. A farmer walks quietly. At most, he'll run into a stray bandit or two. He deals with each bandit in one stroke, and that's the end of it.

"But if you walk around carrying an Azure Dragon Saber like Emperor Guan, you'd better be prepared to fight your way past five fortified checkpoints and slay six generals.[2] Without his skills, how would you make it down the mountain?"

As Shopkeeper Feng spoke, he brushed his fingers along the edge of the sickle blade. Li Banfeng was pretty knowledgeable about this sort of thing and could tell that it was indeed a fine blade.

Li Banfeng asked, "How much for this sickle? I'll take it."

Chubby Qin glared at him. "What's the point of buying that? Let's go to another shop. I've got plenty of connections."

Shopkeeper Feng didn't try to stop them from leaving. "Then, please feel free to check other shops, Trader Qin. The kind of weapon you want, I don't have it here."

Li Banfeng remarked inwardly, Shopkeeper Feng is a good man. He doesn't want to put Chubby Qin in danger.

And so, he insisted on buying the sickle. Shopkeeper Feng asked for five hundred yuan, and Li Banfeng didn't even try to haggle for a lower price. He intended to borrow five hundred yuan from Chubby Qin.

However, Chubby Qin looked heartbroken to part with his money. "Five hundred for this thing?"

Shopkeeper Feng could tell that Li Banfeng was strapped for cash.

Just a few days ago, he had helped the young man with directions, and the young man had bought a fedora and a feather duster.

Shopkeeper Feng remembered Li Banfeng clearly. He's a sensible young man with good principles.

He asked, "Sir, how should I address you?"

Li Banfeng answered, "My surname is Li. You can call me Li Qi."

"Trader Li, I'll let you have the sickle on credit. You can pay me when you're back from gathering the flowers."

Li Banfeng was stunned. On credit?

Shopkeeper Feng's smile didn't waver in the slightest. "You're someone who gets things done. I trust you."

Li Banfeng thanked him and accepted the sickle.

Shopkeeper Feng then pulled out a trowel, about a dozen centimeters long, from beneath the counter and handed it to Li Banfeng. "Some plants are rooted deep and hard to gather. What do you think of this trowel?"

Li Banfeng took the trowel, studied its form, and brushed his fingers along the edge of its blade. He asked, "How much for this one?"

Shopkeeper Feng chuckled. "In terms of materials and craftsmanship, this trowel's even better than the sickle. Eight hundred would be a fair price, but since you've got a good eye for quality… If you like it, I'll give it to you for seven hundred."

"Seven hundred…" Li Banfeng was a little hesitant to accept the deal. The trowel was a good item, but he didn't feel right taking it on credit too.

Shopkeeper Feng could tell why Li Banfeng was hesitating, so he said, "Just take it. Go try it out. If it suits you, pay me later. If it doesn't, just return it."

Li Banfeng ultimately accepted the trowel as well. However, he wanted to buy a few sacks too.

Shopkeeper Feng gave him two. "Two sacks are plenty. The flowers are easy to gather, but they're not so easy to carry down the mountain."

Li Banfeng nodded, taking note of the advice.

Shopkeeper Feng smiled. "Anything else you need?"

The Pocket Dwelling had no windows. He couldn't see any sunlight or moonlight, so it was impossible to tell day from night. Li Banfeng wanted to buy a watch.

Shopkeeper Feng took out a pocket watch. It was about twice the size of a one-yuan coin, with a shiny silver case that reflected its surroundings.

Click!

He popped the case open and revealed the pocket watch's white watch face, embossed with twelve elegant markings for the hours.

The crown of the pocket watch, used for winding the watch, was at the twelve o'clock position. Shopkeeper Feng grasped it, wound it a few times, and then pulled it out two clicks.

Li Banfeng had seen mechanical wristwatches like this before. When the crown wasn't pulled out, it was used for winding the watch. To set the time, the crown had to be pulled out one click. Two clicks were for setting the date.

But there's no date displayed on the pocket watch. Why did he pull it out two clicks? Li Banfeng wondered.

The pocket watch played a music box tune.

This sounds really familiar. Think I've heard it before, somewhere…

A shopping mall? A restaurant?

An upstairs VIP lounge?

Li Banfeng had worked in all those places. The tune left a deep impression on him, but he couldn't recall its name.

It has been quite a while since I last heard some music.

The familiar music made him feel warm and relaxed.

"How much is this watch?"

Shopkeeper Feng lifted the watch by its chain. "This pocket watch is from the country of Geman. Authenticity guaranteed. If proven otherwise, you'll get your money back. Two thousand two hundred. I can't give you this one on credit, I'm afraid."

Li Banfeng was tempted to buy the pocket watch, but the price left him speechless.

Chubby Qin huffed impatiently. "What's the point of buying that? You're better off just buying a clock!"

Shopkeeper Feng smiled but said nothing.

Chubby Qin urged Li Banfeng to leave, so Li Banfeng gave the shelves a final glance to see if there was anything else that could be useful. He suddenly paused, resting his gaze on an unusual object.

It was a rosewood cabinet with a simple and plain design, about the size of two bedside tables stacked together. At the top of the cabinet, there appeared to be three morning glory flowers in full bloom, sculpted from metal.

To be precise, they weren't morning glory flowers but horns in the shape of morning glory flower[3]. One was especially large; its bell[4] wider than a large plate. The other two were much smaller, with bells roughly the size of a fist.

Li Banfeng's eyes lit up. "A record player?"

He'd seen record players on television before. They were machines that played music from vinyl records.

"Record player?" Shopkeeper Feng shook his head. "That's not what this is called."

It doesn't matter what it's called.

If it's a record player, that means there's electricity here.

And if there's electricity, that means I can charge my phone.

And if I can charge my phone, this world would be perfect!

Even if I can't get a signal here, at least I won't need to buy a watch.

However, Shopkeeper Feng's response left Li Banfeng deeply disappointed. "We don't sell anything that uses electricity. This is a gramophone; it doesn't run on electricity."

Li Banfeng couldn't wrap his head around it. How could it play music without electricity?

He had only just found out that the original version of the record player had nothing to do with electricity or magnets. It relied on only two things to play music: the stylus and the record.

Shopkeeper Feng placed a Bakelite record onto the turntable of the gramophone. Then he pulled a crank handle from a drawer underneath and inserted it into the side of the gramophone, turning it forcefully a dozen times.

He did that to wind up the gramophone's spring.

Once the spring was fully wound, he flicked a latch, and the record began to spin rapidly on the turntable.

Shopkeeper Feng gently lowered the needle onto the record. Amid the faint crackles of static, melodic vocals reverberated from the large horn.

「As the drifting clouds scatter,

The bright moonlight illuminates the path

Leading to a blissful reunion.

Tonight, we drink 」

The song it played was "Blossoms Under the Full Moon."

The mechanics behind this old gramophone were very simple.

Grooves of varying depth were etched into the record, created by the vibrations of a stylus during the original performance.

Now, as the stylus of this gramophone retraced those grooves, it made the same vibrations, reproducing the song from that performance.

That was the acoustic principle behind the gramophone.

The sound quality wasn't great. In fact, it was quite rough.

Nevertheless, Li Banfeng really liked the song. It made him feel relaxed and content.

Unfortunately, it ended too quickly. In less than three minutes, the stylus reached the edge of the disc, and the spring wound down. The record stopped spinning.

Chubby Qin looked unimpressed. "What's so great about that? Winding a crank just to hear one song? That's such a hassle."

"If you don't want to wind it, you can still listen to music," Shopkeeper Feng said as he placed another record onto the turntable.

He fetched a tin cup and used it to pour two cups of water into the small horn on the left side of the large horn.

Shopkeeper Feng struck a match and opened a small door on the bottom-right corner of the gramophone.

It revealed a hidden compartment holding an iron box that was fixed in place, half-filled with fat. A single wick stood upright in the center.

He lit the wick, and a small flame flared up, lighting up the entire compartment.

Li Banfeng had no idea what kind of substance was being burned, but he could feel its intense heat even from a distance.

Hiss! Hiss! Hiss!

The small horn on the right side of the large horn began rhythmically releasing bursts of steam.

The small horn on the left was for pouring in the water, and the one on the right was for releasing steam.

What kind of setup is this?

Could it be…

The turntable started spinning the record.

A steam-powered gramophone?

What is this amazing machine?

Li Banfeng's eyes widened in shock. Everything he'd learned during his over ten years of schooling and higher education was once again being challenged.

After about a minute, the record's speed gradually stabilized. Shopkeeper Feng lowered the stylus onto the spinning record.

「Butterfly~ take flight~

My heart is no longer here.

In the cold, endless night,

Who will wipe away my tears? ♪」

The song it played was "Wild Grass and Blossoms Bloom Again in Spring."

What a beautiful song.

1. Emperor Guan was not a real emperor. He's Guan Yu, a famous general from the late Eastern Han dynasty. After death, he was deified and is worshipped as the deity of war and protection. Emperor Guan is a title he was given by his worshippers. ☜

2. Guan Yu was a significant character in the 14th-century novel Romance of the Three Kingdoms. This part refers to this story, where Guan Yu passes through five fortified checkpoints and slays six generals to reunite with his sworn brother, Liu Bei. ☜

3. There’s a wordplay here that doesn’t work in English. In Chinese, the morning glory flower’s name is 喇叭花. 喇叭 means horn/trumpet, 花 means flower, i.e. flower that looks like a horn/trumpet. So, what the author wrote was that Li Banfeng saw metal horn flowers, but no, they’re actually just horns. ☜

4. The large opening at the end of a horn. ☜

Chapter 28: Forming a Group

"How much is this gramophone? I want it!"

Li Banfeng had taken a liking to this gramophone. Just the fact that it was steam-powered and so intricately engineered made it a rare sight, even in Yuezhou.

If he brought it back to Yuezhou and sold it as a collectible, he would definitely make a fortune. Even if he didn't sell it, he would be delighted to own such a marvelous item.

Shopkeeper Feng wiped the gramophone, seemingly reluctant to part with it. "This is a handcrafted piece from the Amikan Kingdom. There are only two of them in all of Puluo. I honestly don't want to sell it."

Li Banfeng could hear the implication behind those words. It was going to be expensive.

"Name your price, Shopkeeper Feng."

Shopkeeper Feng hesitated for a moment, then he raised two fingers.

Chubby Qin's eyes went wide with shock. "You want twenty thousand?"

Shopkeeper Feng shook his head. "Two hundred thousand Huan yuan!"

"Two hundred thousand!" Chubby Qin gave a baffled smile. "Shopkeeper Feng, do you really take us for fools?"

Shopkeeper Feng replied, "It's worth every cent. No bargaining on this one!"

Li Banfeng's gaze lingered on the gramophone.

It was encased in a vintage rosewood cabinet, with three brass horns at the top. A slow stream of steam was still puffing out of the small horn on the right.

「The forest flowers have withered,

And my heart has been buried deep.

When the spring swallow returns one day,

Where will I be? ♪」

The touching melody lingered in Li Banfeng's ears, making him want the gramophone even more.

However, two hundred thousand was far beyond his reach.

He had worked odd jobs to pay for his tuition and living expenses. In his whole life, he had never even had more than ten thousand yuan in savings.

Li Banfeng shifted his gaze to Chubby Qin, and Chubby Qin immediately widened his eyes and said, "What are you looking at me for? I've never even seen that much money in my life. In my village, two hundred thousand is enough to build a house!"

Li Banfeng thought, A sickle costs five hundred, and a house costs two hundred thousand? Is he joking?

Of course, that sickle wasn't just any sickle, and who knew what kind of "house" Chubby Qin was referring to.

Nevertheless, Li Banfeng couldn't take his eyes off the gramophone. Based on his estimation, this machine would fetch at least five hundred thousand back in Yuezhou. This was going to be the biggest investment of his life, and he was extremely confident it was a good one.

Looking at Shopkeeper Feng, Li Banfeng straightened his back and said, "Save this gramophone for me. I'm definitely buying it!"

Shopkeeper Feng looked into Li Banfeng's eyes and sensed that those words carried a special weight for the young man.

He felt that Li Banfeng really would come back and buy the gramophone.

"Sure, I'll hold it for you," Shopkeeper Feng promised.

I'm definitely buying it!

Those words were indeed special to Li Banfeng.

At the orphanage, when he saw other kids eating popsicles outside, he had said, "I'm definitely buying one."

Back in middle school, when he saw his classmates wearing basketball shoes, he had said, "I'm definitely buying a pair."

In college, when he saw his classmates using their phones, he had said, "I'm definitely buying one."

Eventually, he had bought the popsicle, the shoes, and the phone.

Li Banfeng would definitely buy this gramophone too.

"Brother, let's go! We're running out of time!"

Chubby Qin pulled Li Banfeng away from Feng's General Store.

The shop assistant sighed and said, "Boss, when are we ever going to sell that thing?"

Shopkeeper Feng thought about it for a moment. "That guy just now, the one surnamed Li—I think he genuinely wants to buy it."

"What's the use of wanting it?" The shop assistant shook his head several times. "Two hundred thousand? He probably doesn't even have twenty on him!"

Chubby Qin went to a blacksmith's house and bought an axe.

The combined length of the handle and blade was seventy centimeters.

Li Banfeng frowned. "You're bringing that thing up the mountain?"

"I heard that only the fiercest people dare climb Mount Bitterfog. With this axe, I'll scare off at least half of them."

Carrying the axe, Chubby Qin went to the market, bought around ten kilograms of rations, filled two large water flasks, hired a carriage, and took Li Banfeng westward toward Mount Bitterfog.

They traveled for a whole day. Of the ten kilograms of rations, Li Banfeng ate less than half a kilogram, while Chubby Qin ate nearly five kilograms.

By midnight, they had reached the vicinity of Mount Bitterfog.

Li Banfeng looked out into the distance. All he saw was the hazy night; there was no sign of a mountain.

Chubby Qin found an inn and rented two bed spots in the communal sleeping area.

Despite having selected and paid for the communal sleeping area, he found it quite ridiculous. He felt a bit ashamed that he had dragged Li Banfeng into this situation with him, so he made a point to explain himself to Li Banfeng.

"I wanted to get us a private room, but this damn inn raised its prices. A room's a hundred, but it's only twenty for a spot on the shared bed. We're all brothers. Let's just squeeze together."

Li Banfeng wasn't picky. He lay down on the bed and pretended to fall asleep.

It was one giant wooden plank bed, crammed with over twenty people. They were snoring, grinding their teeth, talking in their sleep, making all kinds of noises that people made in their sleep.

Once Chubby Qin fell asleep, Li Banfeng quietly slipped out of the inn. He hid his house key in a small grove nearby and entered the Pocket Dwelling.

Nothing beats my own place.

Since his days at the orphanage all the way to his last days at university, Li Banfeng had never had a room of his own. Now, he was comfortable even sleeping on this ice-cold floor just because it was his.

This time, Li Banfeng didn't oversleep. When he stepped out of the Pocket Dwelling, dawn was just breaking.

Chubby Qin was already up, looking around for him. "Brother Li, where did you go?"

Li Banfeng replied vaguely, "Went to pee. Brother Chubby, should we sleep a little more?"

Chubby Qin said anxiously, "Sleep? What are you saying? We need to hurry and climb up the mountain!"

Li Banfeng followed Chubby Qin westward. They walked for an hour, but the fog in the distance never lifted, and there was still no sign of a mountain.

There were quite a number of people on the path. Chubby Qin held his axe and carried their rations, staying alert and scanning the people around them.

A bald man in his thirties with a scar at the corner of his left eye was rushing along the path and happened to bump into Chubby Qin from behind.

Chubby Qin staggered, nearly falling. He looked up at the bald man.

The bald man turned back and looked at Chubby Qin, his eyes glinting menacingly. "Are you blind? Don't you know you're in the way?"

Chubby Qin instantly snapped. "You're the one who ran into me, yet you're fucking insulting me?"

The bald man stepped toward Chubby Qin. "So what if I insulted you? It's not like I fucking hit you. Are you just itching for a beating?"

Chubby Qin raised his axe and waved it in front of the bald man.

The bald man spat a mouthful of saliva on the ground and pulled out a machete from his waist. It was over a third of a meter long and threatening in his hands.

Li Banfeng stood quietly on the side. Without a word, he wrapped his right hand around the handle of the small sickle he'd bought.

The small sickle was hidden in his pocket, unnoticed by both the bald man and Chubby Qin.

Right then, a man in his early thirties with neatly parted hair stepped between the bald man and Chubby Qin. He had on a gray plaid suit and a pair of gold-rimmed glasses.

"Gentlemen, if you've got strength to spare, save it for the mountain. Why waste it showing off here?"

Truth be told, Chubby Qin didn't want to fight. He was in a rush to climb the mountain and gather flowers. However, he had been too prideful to simply let the matter go.

The bald man glanced at the man in gold-rimmed glasses before sliding it back into its sheath hanging on his waist.

Since both sides had backed down, the matter was left at that.

The bald man continued on his way, and Chubby Qin put his axe away as well.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses lowered his voice and said to Chubby Qin, "I'm guessing this is your first time climbing the mountain, brother?"

Chubby Qin gave a dismissive snort. "I've been to Mount Bitterfog more times than I can count. No way it's my first!"

It was indeed his first time, but he couldn't let anyone know that.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses chuckled. "Whether it's your first time or not, I'll give you a word of advice. That bald guy is not a good person. Once we're on the mountain, he'll surely pull something on you."

Chubby Qin spat on the ground and said, "What, you think I'm scared of him?"

The man in gold-rimmed glasses raised his brows. "It's easy to dodge a sword in the open, but an arrow in the dark is another matter. It's not just you. Seeing as I spoke up for you, I might end up paying the price for it once we're up there."

"I'm not afraid of him anyway," Chubby Qin said, gripping his axe tightly.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses looked at Li Banfeng, who kept his head down and said nothing. With that one glance, the man in gold-rimmed glasses had Li Banfeng pegged as meek and honest.

The three of them walked together for a while. A few flags gradually came into view through the fog up ahead.

There were yellow ones, green ones, and even purple ones.

Li Banfeng asked, "Whose flags are these?"

Chubby Qin shot Li Banfeng a glare, warning him not to reveal their inexperience.

Chubby Qin didn't know where the flags came from either, but Li Banfeng asking that out loud was as good as admitting it was their first time climbing Mount Bitterfog.

In truth, the man in gold-rimmed glasses had already figured out it was their first time. Gazing at the flags, he muttered, "The Bai[1], Huang[2], Ru[3] , and Liu Families—all the major medicinal trading houses are here."

Chubby Qin had heard of these medicinal trading houses, but he didn't understand why their people had come all the way out here.

Once people harvested speckled-snake chrysanthemum, they would sell it to the medicinal trading houses anyway. Why not just stay home and wait for business to come to them? Why wait at the foot of Mount Bitterfog?

The man in gold-rimmed glasses knew the reason but didn't explain. "Gentlemen, let's not go any further. Let's wait here for news."

"Wait for news?" Chubby Qin frowned. "The speckled-snake chrysanthemum only blooms for a day. Every moment we waste here is one less flower we can gather."

The man in gold-rimmed glasses shook his head and pulled out a folding fan from his waist pocket. "Do you know when the speckled-snake chrysanthemum actually blooms?"

Chubby Qin stated loudly, "It blooms today!"

The man in gold-rimmed glasses fanned himself and said slowly, "It does indeed bloom today, yes. But the specific time of when it blooms is uncertain. If it blooms at daybreak, that's today. If it blooms at noon, it would still be today, and of course, if it blooms at dusk, that would still be today. If you go up now and it doesn't bloom until dusk, wouldn't you be breathing in toxic fog all day for nothing?"

Chubby Qin smirked. "I'm a cultivator. I'm not afraid of toxic fog."

"I know you're a cultivator, but even so, there's no point in wasting your strength," the man in gold-rimmed glasses said, looking out at the dense fog in the distance. "The toxic fog on Mount Bitterfog will kill an ordinary person in about three hours. A beginner cultivator who has yet to enter a cultivation layer might last seven or eight hours at most. Judging by your build, you must be a Gourmandism Cultivator, right?"

Chubby Qin didn't respond. After all, cultivators shouldn't be casually revealing their cultivation path and level to others.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses smiled. "If you're a Gourmandism Cultivator, and you've eaten your fill, you should be able to last over ten hours. Wait until the flowers bloom before heading up. As long as you know where to go, it will be easy for you to gather even seventy or eighty flowers."

At eighty-five yuan per flower, seventy to eighty flowers could be sold for more than six thousand yuan in profit.

Hearing that, Chubby Qin rushed forward, but the man in gold-rimmed glasses snapped his fan shut and used it to block Chubby Qin's way.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses frowned as he said, "Didn't I tell you? It's not time yet. Wait until the flowers bloom before going up. You have no idea how many people die up there every year because of this same reckless behavior."

Chubby Qin huffed angrily. "Then do you know when it's going to bloom?"

The man pointed his fan toward the distant flags. "I don't, but they do. Every year, the medicinal trade houses hire two scouts to climb the mountain and check. Once the flowers bloom, the scouts will send a signal, and the medicinal trade houses will send their people up. We just need to follow them."

Chubby Qin was stunned. "The medicinal trade houses send people up too?"

The man in gold-rimmed glasses chuckled. "If they don't send people up, how are they going to get the speckled-snake chrysanthemum to make Speckled-Snake Pills? If a major medicinal trading house can't even produce Speckled-Snake Pills, wouldn't their reputation go down the drain?"

Chubby Qin looked around. "With this many people going up the mountain, and everyone selling to them, why would they be worried about not being able to buy enough?"

The man in gold-rimmed glasses fanned himself and explained, "If there were only one medicinal trading house buying, they wouldn't have to worry, and they'd set the price themselves. However, all the major medicinal trading houses in Medicine King Gully want to buy the herb. This means there's competition, so the sellers get to set the price."

Chubby Qin still didn't understand. "Didn't they already say it's eighty-five per flower?"

The man in gold-rimmed glasses shook his head. "That's just the base price. Once people descend from the mountain, things will change. If you owned a medicinal trading house and didn't have even one speckled-snake chrysanthemum in your inventory, and the sellers demanded a high price, would you buy it? But if you had already sent people up and brought down three to five hundred flowers, at least you'd have some in hand. Even if you make fewer pills, you won't be at the mercy of sellers."

The man in gold-rimmed glasses was clearly an insider. From the way he spoke, it was obvious he had intimate knowledge of the business dealings associated with Mount Bitterfog.

As he spoke, several people quietly gathered around to listen. None of them had ever been to Mount Bitterfog before and wanted to learn from his experience.

He wasn't stingy with his knowledge and went on and on. He shared a great deal of practical information, especially the identifying traits of the speckled-snake chrysanthemum.

Partway through, the man in gold-rimmed glasses suddenly stopped talking. His eyes were fixed on the flags of the medicinal trading houses in the distance.

Beneath those flags, several men were heading into the thick fog.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses turned to the others and said, "The medicinal trading houses are sending their people up now. It's time for us to move too."

Chubby Qin stepped forward eagerly.

However, the man in gold-rimmed glasses warned, "Don't follow them too closely. Down here at the foot of the mountain, they'll follow the rules for the sake of their trading house's reputation. But once we're up on the mountain, they won't hold back. Gathering flowers is tough work; stealing is a lot faster."

Hearing that, Chubby Qin tightened his grip on his axe again. The others around them grew tense too.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses said, "If you trust me, let's form a group. That way we can all look out for each other."

1. White ☜

2. Yellow ☜

3. This transliteration clashes with the first Lu Family that appeared (The pinyin for both 鲁 and 陆 are the same). We have decided to translate 鲁家 (Lǔ Jia) as Ru Family. ☜

Chapter 29: Good Flowers Are Hard to Gather

Chubby Qin didn't mind forming a group. After all, he wasn't even close with Li Banfeng. They had only met once at the Peddler's.

He had looked after Li Banfeng this whole time precisely because he wanted to form a partnership with Li Banfeng.

So, upon meeting someone as experienced as the man in gold-rimmed glasses, Chubby Qin had no reason to refuse forming a group with him and immediately agreed.

Once he agreed, the four other people who had been listening in agreed to join in as well.

With someone this knowledgeable and sincere as the man in gold-rimmed glasses, there really was no reason to say no.

Of the four people, one was a middle-aged man in his fifties. He went by the name of Old Smoke Cannon and claimed to be a level-one Smoking Cultivator. He never let go of his tobacco pipe.

The second was a voluptuous and charming woman in her twenties. She went by the name of Nectarine and claimed to be a Physique Cultivator. She had just entered the path, so she didn't have a level.

The third was a girl around fourteen or fifteen. She went by the name of Leafy and claimed to be a Medicine Cultivator. She was also a beginner with no level.

The last of the four people was a young man in his early twenties. He went by the name of Tiger Lamb and claimed to be a level-one Martial Cultivator.

Their names were clearly fake. As for their cultivation paths and levels, who knew if they were telling the truth.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses had already guessed Chubby Qin's cultivation path, so he simply admitted it. "People call me Chubby. I am a Gourmandism Cultivator. No level."

When it was Li Banfeng's turn, he answered, "I'm Bai Sha. I'm a Hedonism Cultivator. No level."

Chubby Qin was stunned. He stared at Li Banfeng for a long moment.

Back when they first met, Li Banfeng had introduced himself as Li Qi. Nevertheless, it didn't matter which of the two was his real name.

But since when was he a Hedonism Cultivator? Chubby Qin thought in shock. Isn't he a Wanderlust cultivator?

Is he lying to the man in gold-rimmed glasses? And did he tell me the truth?

The man in gold-rimmed glasses was curious too. He asked Li Banfeng, "You're a Hedonism Cultivator? Did you come alone?"

Hedonism Cultivators typically appeared in pairs, as being together made it convenient for their cultivation.

Li Banfeng pointed at Chubby Qin and said, "I came with him."

Everyone's eyes fell on Chubby Qin. After a moment of silence, Chubby Qin untied the bundle on his back, revealing the rations inside.

He told the group, "I'm a Gourmandism Cultivator. I can eat two and a half kilos of food for one meal, sometimes five. I really am a Gourmandism Cultivator, I'm not lying to you. I'll prove it right now."

The man with the gold-rimmed glasses addressed the group. "My name is Mountain Splitter. I'm a level-one Wanderlust Cultivator. I usually like to traverse through famous mountains and rivers, so I've been to Mount Bitterfog a few times before. If there's anything I can warn you of along the way, I'll be sure to speak up."

Tiger Lamb said, "Elder Brother Mountain Splitter, we all trust you. You'll be our leader. After we gather the flowers, according to the rules of the road, we'll give you twenty percent as your share. What do you think?"

As soon as he said that, the others grew displeased.

Who said you could decide for the group? You didn't even ask us first!

Mountain Splitter shook his head. "I can't take your flowers. You'll be risking your lives to gather them. You don't owe me anything. If I watch your back and you watch mine, that's enough. That's the give and take of being part of a group."

Hearing that, the group gained even more respect for Mountain Splitter, and they entrusted him to be their leader.

Through the thick fog, they vaguely made out the employees of the medicinal trading houses. Mountain Splitter led the group at a slow pace behind them.

Up ahead, there was a stone bridge. It was a little over a meter wide, enough for two or three people to walk side by side.

"This is called the Bridge of No Regrets." Mountain Splitter pushed his glasses back up the bridge of his nose. "At this point, you may begin to feel some discomfort in your throat. That's because you've already inhaled the toxic fog. Once we cross the Bridge of No Regrets, we'll be in the region officially known as Mount Bitterfog.

"Everyone, I don't know whether you shared your real cultivation levels, but let me give you a sincere piece of advice. If you are not a cultivator, do not proceed any further. Every year, there are always ordinary folk who try their luck on Mount Bitterfog. Not a single one of them has ever returned alive. I'm not trying to block your path to fortune. I'm just urging you to think twice before it's too late to turn back."

The group exchanged glances but said nothing.

Tiger Lamb let out a cold laugh. "Elder Brother Mountain Splitter, since you've already said it so clearly, anyone who still doesn't understand is simply looking for death."

He then turned to Leafy and said, "Little girl, I can tell you're not a cultivator. If you ask me, I think you ought to turn back now while you still can."

Leafy was the only child in the group. Lowering her head to hide her flushed cheeks, she said, "I really am a Medicine Cultivator. I understand the principles of medicine. I can be of help."

Tiger Lamb glanced at Old Smoke Cannon. "And you, you're just a bag of old bones. Even if you're a cultivator, what good will you be once we're on the mountain?"

Old Smoke Cannon chuckled. "Whether I'll be of any use, you'll see as we go."

None of the group members showed any intention of retreating, so the group of seven crossed the bridge together.

Once they got to the other side of the bridge, Li Banfeng felt his throat tighten, and he broke into a coughing fit.

Unlike the others who had steadily prepared for their cultivation journey over a long period, Li Banfeng had entered the Daoist path by receiving Heavenly Light. The difference between their physical constitutions was beginning to show.

Mountain Splitter frowned. "Brother, are you really a cultivator?"

Li Banfeng nodded. "I am. I'm a Hedonism Cultivator."

Old Smoke Cannon chuckled. "Hedonism Cultivators have weak bodies. It's only natural that you can't withstand the bitter fog."

Hearing that, everyone turned to look at Chubby Qin.

Chubby Qin puffed out his chest. "I'm a Gourmandism Cultivator."

Mountain Splitter turned to Li Banfeng and said, "Brother, if you find you can't hold out on the mountain, then head down as soon as you can. Don't let greed cost you your life."

Leafy reached into her chest pocket and pulled a palm-sized cloth pouch. From it, she took out a piece of candy.

"Big Brother, have a medicated candy. It works."

Medicated candy?

What is that?

Li Banfeng stared at the candy, hesitating whether to eat it.

Seeing his hesitation, Leafy put one in her mouth first. Then she held out another to him. "Big Brother, I'm not a bad person."

If Li Banfeng refused again, it would seem unreasonable.

So, he popped the candy into his mouth. The slightly sour but cooling juice from the candy slid down his throat, instantly alleviating the dryness and irritation.

The coughing has stopped. This thing is really effective.

Li Banfeng thanked her and continued forward with the group. After walking for over half an hour, they finally reached the foot of the mountain.

There was a winding dirt path leading up the mountain, stretching far above. They had a visibility of no more than twenty or thirty meters, which was still far from enough to make out the mountain's full form.

By this point, Nectarine, the voluptuous beauty, had started coughing. She asked Leafy for a piece of medicated candy. "Little sister, give me one too. Once we've sold the flowers, I'll pay you for it."

The mountain path was narrow and steep, forcing everyone into a single file.

Li Banfeng happened to be walking right behind Nectarine. He had seen many pretty young women before, but he had rarely seen women with a voluptuous figure like Nectarine.

Her hair was styled with finger waves, and she wore a cheongsam with both red and green in its design. Her shoulders and back had smooth, graceful lines, and she had such a slender waist that it looked as if he could easily wrap a hand around it.

The two peach-like curves below her waist were especially alluring. They were impressively round; there was quite an exquisite charm to their size.

No wonder she goes by the name of Nectarine, a glossy peach.[1]

That peach of hers is truly perfect.

She is right in front of me, her hips swaying with every step…

Is she really here to gather flowers?

Why would someone wear a cheongsam to climb a mountain?

It's obviously impractical for such terrain!

She is clearly trying to distract me!

Li Banfeng tried his best to tear his gaze away from those curves, but Nectarine suddenly turned and smiled at him.

Her eyes curved upward into crescent moons, brimming with charm. That smile alone made Li Banfeng's heart tense with desire.

"Little brother, are you really a Hedonism Cultivator? I've heard Hedonism Cultivators can go at it seven or eight times a day without a problem."

Li Banfeng replied calmly, "You flatter me, Elder Sister."

Nectarine's rosy lips trembled slightly as she whispered, "Why don't we… 'compare notes' some other day?"

Li Banfeng shook his head. "My cultivation level is still too low. I fear I wouldn't be a match for you, Elder Sister."

Nectarine glanced at Chubby Qin walking behind Li Banfeng. She pursed her lips into a teasing smile. "I almost forgot, your Dao partner is right here. You prefer someone round, not flat."

Chubby Qin wiped the sweat from his forehead. "I'm a Gourmandism Cultivator…"

Wanderlust Cultivators had a very good sense of distance. By Li Banfeng's calculation, they had walked a little over two and a half kilometers when a black and white flower appeared by the side of the mountain path.

The flower looked very peculiar. Each petal was narrow at the top and wide at the bottom. They were white with black spots, each one resembling a snake's head covered in scales.

Speckled-snake chrysanthemums!

Mountain Splitter had described the speckled-snake chrysanthemum's features earlier. Li Banfeng recognized it immediately; this flower was exactly as Mountain Splitter had said.

Chubby Qin recognized it too. Overjoyed, he was about to step forward to take it. However, a man burst out of the bushes beside the path. With a pair of long-handled shears in hand, the man snatched the flower first.

Chubby Qin was furious that the flower had been stolen. However, the thing that infuriated him more was the thief himself.

He was the bald man that Chubby Qin had fought with before starting the climb.

It's this fucking guy again!

Chubby Qin raised his axe again, this time truly intending to strike the bald man.

The bald man sneered with disdain. "You're as stupid as a pig. You can't even eat shit while it's still hot! What are you doing with that axe? Go on, try swinging it!"

Mountain Splitter held Chubby Qin back, and in the blink of an eye, the bald man disappeared into the undergrowth.

Chubby Qin gnashed his teeth. "That son of a bitch, I swear I'll hack him to pieces!"

Mountain Splitter pulled him back. "It's just one flower by the path. Don't let it bother you. Once we reach the right spot, there'll be plenty more."

Chubby Qin still felt like going after the bald man. "And where's that exactly?"

Mountain Splitter glanced up the mountain path. The group from the medicinal trading houses was fast on their feet, and their figures had already vanished from view.

Mountain Splitter said, "Let's move faster. See if we can still catch up to them."

Old Smoke Cannon lit his pipe. He took a few hard drags and exhaled a stream of white smoke tinged with yellow.

The smoke drifted forward swiftly. Old Smoke Cannon bit down on the smoke pipe, savoring the taste.

"They haven't gone far. At the fork up ahead, they went right."

Tiger Lamb was surprised to hear that. "Old man, don't just make something up because you don't know the answer."

Old Smoke Cannon tapped his pipe twice. "If you don't believe me, then don't follow us."

Mountain Splitter believed Old Smoke Cannon, for there truly was a fork in the road ahead.

The group turned right at the fork and walked for over an hour. Beyond the dense forest, a clearing finally appeared by the mountain path.

"They're here," Old Smoke Cannon said. He stepped into the clearing, with Mountain Splitter following closely behind.

As a Wanderlust Cultivator, Li Banfeng could have walked at the very front. However, he deliberately slowed his pace, his eyes seemingly fixed on Nectarine's peach-like curves.

After searching the clearing for a while, Li Banfeng noticed a black and white flower at his feet.

He had been staring at Nectarine's swaying butt for so long that he thought his eyes were playing tricks on him.

Li Banfeng bent down for a closer look. He wasn't mistaken; it really was a speckled-snake chrysanthemum.

He took out his sickle. Following the method Mountain Splitter had explained at the foot of the mountain, Li Banfeng pinched the flower's stem and cut it an inch or so above the root.

I've finally obtained my first speckled-snake chrysanthemum!

1. In Chinese, Nectarine directly translates to "oil peach". It's because it has a smooth, glossy skin, unlike the hairy skin of a normal peach. ☜

Chapter 30: A Zone One Must Not Linger

The man in gold-rimmed glasses said that a few flowers by the roadside were nothing worth fussing over. Once they reached a good place, there would be plenty of fine blossoms to pick.

The way he said it made Li Banfeng think that when they reached the good location, the speckled-snake chrysanthemum would be like wheat, yielding an armful with a single swing of the sickle. But when they did reach the good location, Li Banfeng realized that there were not that many flowers. Even after searching for more than two hours, he had only gathered five.

Some speckled-snake chrysanthemums had long stems and could be cut down directly with a sickle. Others had stems that were barely visible, with petals pressed against the ground, forcing him to dig them out carefully with a trowel.

By Li Banfeng's estimation, finding even a single speckled-snake chrysanthemum within a ten-meter diameter was already considered lucky. As the mountain mist grew thicker, his visibility worsened, and he could see no farther than ten meters ahead.

The flowers were hard to find, and the competition was fierce.

People from the medicinal trading house were nearby. They were more experienced and moved more quickly. They were not the only ones either, since there was competition even within this hastily assembled team.

Old Smoke Cannon really was a Smoking Cultivator. He said he was a level-one Smoking Cultivator, and that did not seem to be a lie. The smoke acted as his eyes, allowing him to see wherever it drifted. In just two hours, he had gathered thirteen speckled-snake chrysanthemums.

The little girl Leafy was skilled as well. As a Medicine Cultivator, she had a special sense for medicinal herbs and managed to collect ten.

The voluptuous beauty Nectarine was not as skilled. In the same two hours, she had picked only three. She was doing even worse than Li Banfeng.

Seeing Nectarine, Li Banfeng could not help grumbling inwardly. What is with her constantly swaying around in front of me? She is wearing a cheongsam, bending over with her butt right in my face, swaying it back and forth. How am I supposed to focus on picking chrysanthemums like this? Could it be that she actually wants me to pluck her chrysanthemum?[1]

The man in gold-rimmed glasses did know his stuff, but he wasn't particularly skilled either. He had only gathered six flowers, only one more than Li Banfeng.

Like Li Banfeng, Tiger Lamb gathered five flowers as well.

The most useless of them turned out to be Chubby Qin, who had only managed to gather a single flower. He grew so anxious that he stomped his feet in the grass.

Tiger Lamb laughed at him from the side. "With just one flower, you can't even make half a pill. I bet when you get down the mountain, no one will want to buy it!"

Chubby Qin snapped back in annoyance. "Who says it won't sell? It's not like they'll refine pills using only my flower. Of course they'll mix it with the others!"

"You can't even refine half a pill with a single flower?" Li Banfeng asked.

"No, you can't," The girl Leafy said as she located another speckled-snake chrysanthemum. As she plucked the flower, she said, "I was told that you would need five speckled-snake chrysanthemum flowers to refine a superior-grade pill."

The beauty Nectarine chimed in, "And I heard you also need quite a lot of supplementary ingredients."

Leafy thought for a moment and said, "I remember that refining a Speckled-Snake Pill doesn't require supplementary ingredients, but the control of fire is especially difficult. You have to switch the level of fire four or five times, and the intensity is different each time. Moreover, each medicinal trading house has its own method of refining pills."

Nectarine let out a sigh and said, "When it comes to pill recipes, if you don't know them, they're worth their weight in gold. But once you know them, they turn out to be nothing but nonsense. Too bad we don't have the skill. We can only risk our lives for a bit of money.

"Little Sis, fate has brought us together. Since we have met, we can be considered friends. You should study principles of medicine well. In the future, when we collect medicinal herbs and sell them to your shop, at least give us a little more money."

When Leafy heard this, she blushed and continued searching for speckled-snake chrysanthemums with a grin.

Nectarine's words had struck right at her heart. It was her dream to open a medicine shop.

Li Banfeng quickly did the math in his head. It took five speckled-snake chrysanthemums to refine one pill. At eighty-five coins per flower, the cost of the flowers alone came to four hundred and twenty-five coins.

The cost of a Speckled-Snake Pill was not low. Even without supplementary ingredients, once labor was added, it would still be nearly five hundred coins per pill.

Running a medicinal trading house isn't an easy business either! Li Banfeng thought.

"How much can a Speckled-Snake Pill sell for?" Li Banfeng asked.

"Ten thousand," Chubby replied simply.

Li Banfeng froze on the spot. Ten thousand? Ten thousand! Is this a joke? A single pill sells for ten thousand! This is outrageous profit!

While Li Banfeng froze on the spot, Big Sis Nectarine let out a laugh and said, "What's wrong? Too expensive? Medicinal trading houses thrive on exorbitant returns. Even at this price, only wealthy families can afford them. Ordinary people wouldn't even have a chance to buy one.

"Think about it. With ten thousand, you can buy a single day of cultivation. With three million six hundred and fifty thousand, you could buy a year. Thirty-six million five hundred thousand would buy you ten years.

“Ten years of cultivation equates to a level of cultivation. With three hundred million, you could buy ten levels. And ten levels would mean tripling your lifespan. Don't you think that's worth the money?"

"Whether it's worth it or not is another matter. Big Sis, when you talk, stop pointing your butt at me."

Nectarine turned her head, narrowed her eyes, and smiled at him. "Do you like it? It's plump and fair. Don't you want to give it a squeeze? Give me one flower, and I'll let you squeeze to your heart's content."

While Nectarine was saying that, Li Banfeng was thinking about how a single pill yielded a profit twenty times of the cost.

He found a speckled-snake chrysanthemum, examined it for a moment, and asked, "Instead of buying such expensive pills, why not just eat the flower directly? One only costs eighty-five."

Leafy shouted, "Elder Brother Bai Sha[2], don't be reckless! You can't eat these. Speckled-snake chrysanthemums are highly poisonous. If you eat even one, you'll die!"

Li Banfeng was stunned. "The speckled-snake chrysanthemum is poisonous? Then isn't the Speckled-Snake Pill poisonous too?"

Leafy and Nectarine both turned to look at him as though they were staring at an illiterate fool.

Leafy explained, "The Speckled-Snake Pill is poisonous too, but not as severely. Still, you can't eat too many."

Nectarine said, "Elder Brother Bai Sha, how can you know so little? Are you even a cultivator? If you don't actually have cultivation, you'd better head down the mountain before you get yourself killed. I was only giving an example when I said three hundred million for ten levels of cultivation earlier. Don't take it seriously. If anyone really ate that many Speckled-Snake Pills, they would probably die several times over in the process."

Chubby Qin called out from the side, "Hurry up and keep looking for flowers. What's with all the chatter? Focus on what's important!"

Chubby Qin was reminding Li Banfeng not to expose himself any further. Still, he had a point. He should focus on the more important matter. Speckled-snake chrysanthemums only bloomed for a single day before they withered.

Yet, that wasn't the most pressing concern. Li Banfeng was starting to feel dizzy. He was finding it harder and harder to resist the bitter fog.

Old Smoke Cannon, on the other hand, stayed focused on the task. While the others were still talking, he picked another speckled-snake chrysanthemum and slipped it into his cloth pouch.

He exhaled a mouthful of yellow smoke and the smoke lingered for a moment before he sensed something. Following the sign, he quickly stepped forward and spotted yet another speckled-snake chrysanthemum ahead.

Taking out his scissors, he skillfully snipped the stem. Just as he was about to put the flower into his pouch, the bald man who had snatched flowers by the roadside earlier suddenly appeared in front of him, reaching out to grab it.

Old Smoke Cannon stayed calm and steady. He swung his smoking pipe against the back of the bald man's hand. With a sharp sizzle, it seared off a strip of skin and flesh.

The bald man howled in pain and snarled, "Old bastard, you've lived long enough!"

Old Smoke Cannon chuckled. "You're the one who's lived long enough to dare steal from me."

"Steal? I saw that flower first!"

"I don't give a shit who saw it first? It's in my hand now. If you've got the guts, come and take it!"

Without another word, Old Smoke Cannon slipped the flower into his cloth pouch and raised his smoking pipe, fixing the bald man with a calm, indifferent gaze.

The bald man let out a sharp whistle, and three more men stepped up beside him. He hadn't come alone.

For the first time, a flicker of panic crossed Old Smoke Cannon's face.

"Old man, put down that pouch and I'll spare your life today," the bald man warned as he drew a machete. The three behind him followed suit, each pulling out a weapon.

Old Smoke Cannon hesitated for a moment. He had no intention of fighting head-on. And so, he opened his pouch and said to the bald man, "There are twenty-two flowers inside. I'll give you half, and we'll call it even."

"I told you to leave it all behind. Don't you understand? You would rather risk your life than lose money?" The bald man sneered, raising his blade and swinging without hesitation.

The little girl Leafy was so frightened that she hid behind the man with the gold-rimmed glasses.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses called out, "Friend, we can talk this over!"

Tiger Lamb also stepped forward. "Let's be reasonable. Don't start a fight just yet!"

The two tried to stop the fight, but the bald man paid them no mind. He swung his blade again and again with each strike aiming at Old Smoke Cannon's head.

Surrounded on all sides by the four people, Old Smoke Cannon had no chance to take even a single puff from his pipe. Just as a blade was about to cleave his head, the bald man suddenly drew back. A fresh cut had appeared across the back of his hand.

Chubby Qin had made his move.

Since they were a team, they had to watch each other's backs. Chubby Qin always followed the rules, and besides, he had long wanted to teach this bald man a lesson.

When the bald man saw only Chubby Qin charging forward, he ordered his men to keep surrounding and attacking.

Seeing the situation, Tiger Lamb glanced at the man in gold-rimmed glasses, who in turn swept his gaze across the others. The little girl Leafy cowered behind him, too scared to move.

Nectarine kept her eyes fixed on the bald man. There was no knowing what she planned to do.

Meanwhile, Li Banfeng spotted a speckled-snake chrysanthemum and moved to pick it, acting as if the fight nearby had nothing to do with him.

Tiger Lamb cast another glance at the man in gold-rimmed glasses and whispered, "That's enough."

Still, the man in gold-rimmed glasses kept his gaze fixed on the bald man.

Though the bald man's group held the advantage at four against two, their attacks grew strangely frantic. Their grips shook on their hilts, and their blades fell in crooked arcs, like the clumsy swings of novices who had never fought before.

What's going on? Why are they so panicked? There's nothing here worth panicking over.

The bald man's eyes gleamed and his cheeks flushed. He wasn't in a state of panic. He was excited and so excited that he was losing control of his body.

Seeing this, the man in gold-rimmed glasses rushed forward and kicked him square in the chest, knocking him back several steps.

Tiger Lamb barreled in and sent one of the men sprawling with a single punch. He shouted at the others, "We showed you respect, and you trampled it underfoot! Do you really think we are that easy to bully?"

The bald man lifted his blade and growled, "I advise you not to meddle in things that aren't your business."

With a stern face, the man in gold-rimmed glasses adjusted his glasses."We formed a team and agreed to watch each other's backs. That makes this our business."

Chubby Qin ripped open his pouch and wolfed down a whole jin of rations. Then he bellowed at the crowd, "Come on, fight me! Let's see if I can cut you all down!"

Old Smoke Cannon raised his smoking pipe and inhaled deeply. Wisps of yellow-white smoke slowly drifted out, spreading through the air.

Realizing that the situation was bad, the bald man led his group and fled away.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses let out a long sigh and turned to the others. “Everyone, hurry and pick the flowers. They won’t dare come back.”

Old Smoke Cannon tucked away his pipe, sat down for a moment to catch his breath, then went back to picking.

Chubby Qin, still fuming, plopped down beside Li Banfeng and muttered, "Brother Li, why didn't you help me out?"

Li Banfeng shoved the speckled-snake chrysanthemum he had just picked into Chubby Qin's hands. "Pick quickly. Gather a few and let's get out of here. Something's not right."

With that, Li Banfeng set a stone on the ground. He had been clutching it the entire time. Only then did Chubby Qin realize that Li Banfeng had already been prepared to act.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses spotted a speckled-snake chrysanthemum and had just picked it when Tiger Lamb quietly stepped up beside him. "Why didn't you let Third Brother strike first just now? If you give Third Brother a chance to rob them all, they will work even harder to pick more flowers. Aside from that old man, the rest are all weaklings."

The man in gold-rimmed glasses placed the chrysanthemum into his cloth pouch and lowered his voice. "There's something off about that one called Bai Sha."

"What's wrong with him? He's just a useless fool who is greedy for money but too scared to meddle in anything."

"He's a Hedonism Cultivator. They use techniques that can cloud the mind. We need to stay on guard."

Tiger Lamb snorted. "He doesn't look like one to me. I've never seen a Hedonism Cultivator who would travel with another man."

The man in gold-rimmed glasses frowned. "There are plenty of things you haven't seen."

Tiger Lamb still refused to believe it. "When that fat kid was getting beaten up just now, I didn't see him lift a finger. What kind of Hedonism Cultivator would just watch his partner take a beating?"

"How do you know he didn't make a move?" The man in gold-rimmed glasses brushed the dirt from his hands. "Third Brother was struck by one of his techniques. Didn't you see? He couldn't even hold his blade steadily."

"That was a Hedonism Cultivator's technique?" Tiger Lamb was stunned. "I thought Third Brother just panicked. So what's your plan? Are we dropping this?"

"Of course not. We'll just switch places later. Hedonism Cultivators have weak bodies. He won't last long and will have to descend the mountain early. When he's alone, we'll have Third Brother finish him off. The rest of us will deal with the others."

With that, the man in gold-rimmed glasses rose to his feet and went off in search of the next speckled-snake chrysanthemum.

1. Chrysanthemums in Chinese is slang for butthole… Do I need to explain what this kind of innuendo it implies? ☜

2. Nickname translation for the cigarette brand Bai Sha in chapter 2 ☜

Chapter 31: Bronze Lotus Flower, What Are You Trying to Do?

As dusk crept over the forest, the people from the medicinal trading house packed up their baskets and wandered off toward the next clearing as almost all the speckled-snake chrysanthemums here were plucked away.

Old Smoke Cannon took a deep puff on his pipe and exhaled a mouthful of smoke before he continued following the people from the medicinal trading house.

The group trekked for over an hour before the people from the medicinal trading house led them to another good spot. This grove was secluded and quiet, and the trees stood far enough apart. To their surprise, there were even more flowers than there were at the first site. Yet even though there were more flowers around, everyone moved more slowly as they gathered them.

The fog on Mount Bitterfog was eating away at everyone and even the people from the medicinal trading house grew weak and sluggish.

Chubby Qin was affected the least, while Li Banfeng suffered the most. His hands and feet tingled with numbness, and his vision blurred in waves. By nightfall, the fog had become even heavier, and their situation became increasingly difficult.

Nectarine carried a pocket watch, and when she checked the time, she saw that it was already past nine. She was struggling to hold on as well, so she said, "Why don't we head back? This trip has already been worth it."

Nectarine had picked thirty-five speckled-snake chrysanthemums in total, which was worth two thousand nine hundred seventy-five yuan. This amount was enough for her to break even.

Li Banfeng had picked two more stalks than Nectarine, exactly as he had expected. Even so, he felt that if they didn't head down soon, their lives would be at risk.

Chubby Qin, however, refused to leave. He had only plucked sixteen flowers, the fewest in the group. At the medicinal trading house's price, he would earn just over thirteen hundred yuan. After deducting his travel expenses, he would still make a small profit, but not much. And so, he simply couldn't accept this.

The speckled-snake chrysanthemums at this second site were easier to find than those at the first. Chubby Qin was also in better shape than the others. By his own estimate, he could endure the toxic fog until dawn. To him, this was the perfect chance to catch up and gather more.

Chubby Qin refused to leave, and Leafy wouldn't leave either. She kept herself going with medicated candies, and for now her body showed no signs of major issues. Old Smoke Cannon had already finished two more pouches of tobacco. In his words, as long as he could smoke, he could keep going.

Since none of them were leaving, Nectarine refused to go either. She, too, was unwilling to give up. Seeing how she was struggling to even shake her buttcheeks, Li Banfeng really wanted to persuade her to stop.

However, he no longer had the strength to speak. The dizziness was so bad he felt that if he opened his mouth, he'd probably throw up.

Besides, Nectarine was just an acquaintance. The one he was truly worried about was Chubby Qin. Yet Chubby Qin refused to leave no matter what. In the end, Li Banfeng had no choice but to head down the mountain alone.

Before he left, Leafy stepped forward and advised, "Elder Brother Bai Sha, if you really are a Hedonism Cultivator, then you will need to rest for at least half a month. Hedonism Cultivators have weak constitutions[1], so this kind of toxic fog harms them the most. If you follow another Daoist path, you'll need at least three to five days of rest. But if you're a Homebound Cultivator, then just ignore what I said."

Li Banfeng was just catching the crucial part when Nectarine suddenly interrupted. "What about Homebound Cultivators?"

Leafy's eyes went wide. "Big Sis, don't tell me you're a Homebound Cultivator?"

Nectarine only laughed. "I was just asking casually."

Leafy lowered her voice and said, “Even a Homebound Cultivator without a dwelling spirit only needs to go home and sleep for seven or eight hours to get rid of this poison. If they have one, it will take even less time. This is the strength of Homebound Cultivators. As long as they can get home, they have nothing to fear.”

Nectarine let out a soft sigh. "If only I were a Homebound Cultivator…"

Leafy shook her head. "Big Sis, Homebound Cultivators usually don't go out so easily. Could it be that you really are…"

As Leafy spoke, she kept staring at Nectarine, seemingly very curious about her Daoist path.
