Nectarine reached out and caressed Leafy's cheek. "Once we're done with this job, come visit me at my home. Then you'll know whether I'm a Homebound Cultivator or not."

Li Banfeng had no mood for all this chattering. He leaned close to Chubby Qin's ear and whispered, "Just keep picking flowers here. Don't move to another spot. Once you've picked a few more, head down the mountain right away!"

Chubby Qin casually answered in agreement. Since there were plenty of flowers here, he was not planning on switching to another location. However, there was no way he would leave easily. His plan was to endure and stay until tomorrow morning.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses had said that while the blooming time of the speckled-snake chrysanthemum was uncertain, once it did bloom, it would last for a full day and night.

The flowers that had bloomed around seven in the morning would not wither until seven the next day. This meant that he would be able to pick another twenty flowers during the night.

If he waited until dawn to head down the mountain, the worst he faced was a few days of vomiting and diarrhea, something he could recover from with just a couple of hearty meals.

Seeing Chubby Qin so focused on picking flowers, Li Banfeng left the grove, stepped into his Pocket Dwelling as he hid his key in a secluded spot along the mountain path.

He didn't head down the mountain, as Leafy's words had reminded him that it would be safer to recover in his Pocket Abode. He decided to rest there first. Only once he had mostly regained his strength would he descend the mountain; otherwise, he might run into danger along the way.

Something felt wrong along the mountain path. There was a serious problem.

Everyone's behavior so far had been reasonable. For a group of strangers, looking out for one another within limits made sense. The man in gold-rimmed glasses had acted in ways that seemed logical, yet Li Banfeng couldn't bring himself to trust him.

However, one thing stood out as illogical during this flower-harvesting trip: they had been attacked by the same person three times in a row.

On Mount Bitterfog, everyone was after speckled-snake chrysanthemums, so being robbed was within his expectation. But to be robbed by the same person three times? It made no sense at all.

Li Banfeng untied his cloth bag, tossed it aside, and lay flat inside the room, trying to piece together the sequence of events.

The first time we ran into that bald man on the road, it might have been a coincidence. But then we met him again on the road up the mountain. How did that happen? Was that another coincidence? And then, the man showed up a third time to rob us… how could that be explained?

No, it doesn't make sense. That bald man must be following us. But on Mount Bitterfog, with visibility this poor, how could he tail us without being noticed? Tracking anyone here should have been nearly impossible.

If Old Smoke Cannon weren't with us, we wouldn't even be able to follow the people from the medicinal trading house. Yet that bald man managed to follow us the whole way? Is someone in his group skilled at tracking… or did someone leave signs for them to follow?

As these thoughts spun in his head, the burning pain wracked his body, making it impossible to concentrate. The pain was overwhelming. The dizziness that came with it made him feel like puking.

This fog on Mount Bitterfog really is killing me, Li Banfeng muttered inwardly. No! I can't throw up in my house, at least not on the floor!

The house had no ventilation. If he vomited on the floor, the stench would linger with no telling how long it would last.

Yet Li Banfeng could no longer hold it back anymore! He tore open the cloth bag, dumped out the speckled-snake chrysanthemums, and vomited into it until nothing but bile came out.

After emptying his stomach, he felt a little better. He tied the bag shut, tossed it aside, and collapsed to the ground, sinking into a deep sleep.

He didn't know how long he slept, but suddenly a strange smell jolted him awake. Inside the Pocket Dwelling, there shouldn't have been any scent besides dust.

Could it be the smell of the speckled-snake chrysanthemums? Li Banfeng wondered. While he was outside the house, he hadn't noticed the chrysanthemums giving off any distinct scent. After all, the mountain fog was so thick and choking that it could have smothered the flowers' scent.

Now that he was in his Pocket Dwelling, with no other odors interfering, he could smell the chrysanthemums clearly. It had to be the speckled-snake chrysanthemums, for he detected a unique floral fragrance.

I forgot! Leafy said the flower is poisonous. Even swallowing one stalk would kill you. Could the fragrance be poisonous too?

Feeling startled and alarmed, Li Banfeng quickly got up. He had scattered the speckled-snake chrysanthemums carelessly around the room, but now he needed to find a proper place to store them.

He took out a match and lit a candle.

Oddly enough, his body no longer ached after that nap. His head no longer felt dizzy, and he felt almost fully recovered. But the very next moment, his scalp prickled, as if currents of electricity were rolling from his forehead to the back of his head.

Where are my speckled-snake chrysanthemums? Where are all thirty-seven that I went through hell to gather? I can't even find a single one!

The loss of the flowers and money wasn't the biggest issue. Li Banfeng was the only one who could enter his Pocket Dwelling. So who could have stolen them?

I couldn't possibly have eaten the flowers while I was sleeping, right? But the flowers are not edible!

Li Banfeng searched everywhere but couldn't find even a single petal.

The candlelight illuminated only a small circle, and as he moved forward without watching his step, he tripped on something and nearly fell. Lifting the candle, he glanced back and saw a bronze lotus flower lying on the ground.

Wait… is this the bronze lotus flower? Li Banfeng thought.

The bronze lotus flower was still lying in the same spot on the ground, but its state had changed. It had bloomed!

Li Banfeng could clearly see the center of the flower and the seedpod within it. Inside rested a single lotus seed, perfectly ripe.

He carefully plucked it, and the lotus instantly snapped shut, nearly clamping down on his hand. Still, he managed to get the seed. In the center of his palm, the bluish-green seed trembled lightly.

Li Banfeng had no idea what the seed was or how he should handle it.

Suddenly, it swelled to the size of a fist before bursting apart with a loud bang.

The sudden burst startled Li Banfeng. He jerked his hand back as eight beads, each no larger than a pinky nail, shot out and scattered across the floor.

Li Banfeng picked one up and examined it. The bead had a white base with black speckles, as though some kind of scale was embedded within.

What is this thing?

1. The raw uses the term 体虚 (tǐ xū), which refers to physical weakness caused by a deficiency in qi, blood, or yin and yang. However, the term also carries a cultural nuance. When someone is described as having this kind of weakness, it can imply that they have overindulged in sex, depleting the kidney's yang qi and leaving the body in a weakened state. ☜

Chapter 32: Time to Make a Move

At five in the morning, Nectarine finally reached her limit. She crouched on the ground, vomiting nonstop. "No, I have to go down the mountain…"

She struggled as she tried to get up and staggered as she made her way out of the grove. Ever since Li Banfeng had left, she had wanted to leave too, but each time she saw the others still picking flowers, she couldn't bring herself to go.

The toxic fog was slowly eating away at her, and her efficiency had dropped drastically. In the past eight hours, she had managed to gather only three speckled-snake chrysanthemums as she would spend most of her time resting.

She knew she was dizzy. At this moment, her vision blurred, and even walking was a struggle. Yet seeing the others continue to gather flowers filled her with a gnawing, bitter discomfort.

This was human nature. Whenever people saw others making a profit, they felt as if they were losing out. It was like having a pool of gold right before her, and all she needed to do was grit her teeth and scoop up more.

She had endured until now, but she truly couldn't go on any longer. As she walked down the mountain path, she vaguely heard footsteps behind her.

Nectarine was startled. She clutched the pouch at her waist and snapped her head around, but the sudden motion was so fierce that her vision swam and she collapsed to the ground.

Someone really was chasing after her. It was Leafy.

Leafy rushed over and helped Nectarine up. She reached into her robes, took out a small porcelain vial, and handed it to her. "Big Sis, I have some Cooling Powder here. Take a little, it'll help. If you don't trust me, I'll eat some first…"

Nectarine smiled, accepted the vial, and swallowed a dose. After resting for a moment, she really did feel much better.

"I've been eating your medicated candies and powders all day. I truly feel that I owe you," Nectarine said as she opened her cloth pouch and took out five speckled-snake chrysanthemums. "Please take these. It isn't much, but I don't have many to spare."

Leafy shook her head. "I've picked more than you, Big Sis. I already have sixty-one stalks. I don't need yours. Big Sis, let's go down together. I'll need your help along the way."

Nectarine pinched Leafy's cheek with a bright smile. "Let's go together then."

The two of them started walking down the mountain, but less than an hour later a figure stepped out to block their path.

Leafy was startled and immediately ducked behind Nectarine.

Nectarine, who had long experience roaming the jianghu, remained composed. She first scanned the mountain road, then called out to the man blocking their way ahead. "Big Bro, are you looking for speckled-snake chrysanthemums? I can point you to a good spot with plenty of fine flowers."

From within the dense fog, the man let out a laugh. "I think the perfect spot is right in front of me. I see two very fine flowers."

The voice sounded faintly familiar.

Nectarine laughed in response. "Big Bro, your words are really sweet. If you say I'm a flower, I'll shamelessly accept it. But this girl is still so young. She is at most a flower bud. Would you really stoop so low as to lay a hand on her?"

As Nectarine spoke, she reached out to Leafy, signaling her to pass over the Cooling Powder. After taking a dose, she felt a little better, but she knew there was no way around this obstacle.

The man stepped forward two paces, and his shiny bald head gleamed in the dim night light. It was that bald man again!

"I won't bother wasting words on you two," the bald man said as he wiped his cleaver. "Hand over the flowers, and I'll let you both live."

While he was talking, three lackeys emerged from behind him.

Leafy was terrified, clutching the cloth pouch at her waist with both hands.

Nectarine gently patted her, signaling her to stay calm. At the same time, she wondered if the girl had brought any poison. A medicine cultivator might not be skilled in combat, but they should at least know how to use medicine.

One might wonder why Nectarine didn't simply do as the bald man said and leave the speckled-snake chrysanthemums behind to save her life. But having ventured through the jianghu, she knew exactly what kind of people they were dealing with.

They were just two women here in the remote mountain area.

Even if they did hand over the flowers, there was no guarantee they would live. And even if they were to die, it would not be an easy death.

Nectarine let out a soft laugh, gave the bald man a playful wink, and said sweetly, "Brother, listen to yourself. As a man, can you really harden your heart to bully two weak women like us?"

The bald man chuckled. "I suppose I can't quite bring myself to. How about this? Why don't you two try pleasing me together? Whoever manages to satisfy me, I will reward her with a flower."

"Brother, how could one be enough? If I put in more effort, why not reward me with more?" Nectarine twisted her body. Every curve radiated allure. Her voice was especially seductive, slipping into the ear like a honey-soaked feather gliding along the spine.

The bald man's heartbeat quickened, and so did the heartbeats of his three lackeys.

"You bitch! So you were the Hedonism Cultivator earlier! You're the one who made a move today!" the bald man spat. "Trying to play tricks on your daddy? This papa will make you taste real pain today!"

All four of them charged at once, and Nectarine drew two short blades from her waist.

…

In the small grove, Old Smoke Cannon packed the last of his tobacco into his pipe, lit it, and drew in two heavy breaths. "Alright, guys, I've gathered enough. Until we meet again."

He had collected a total of ninety-one speckled-snake chrysanthemums, making him the one who collected the most.

However, his body could no longer hold out. He had used tobacco resin to protect his throat and filter out much of the toxic fog. Even so, his age caught up with him. After nearly twenty hours on Mount Bitterfog, he had reached his limit.

Just as Old Smoke Cannon was about to leave, Tiger Lamb stepped forward to block his path. "Old man, don't be in such a hurry. Dawn hasn't come yet. Stay a while longer."

Sensing something was off, Old Smoke Cannon fixed him with a hard stare. "Why? Are you planning to rob me?"

Tiger Lamb grinned. "No need to make it sound so harsh, old man. We're teammates, aren't we? We should look out for each other. If you carry that many flowers down the mountain, something bad is bound to happen. Why not let us keep them safe for you? We'll return them once we're off the mountain."

"Give them to you for safekeeping?" Old Smoke Cannon laughed. "I appreciate the thought, but we were just strangers who crossed paths by chance. Our fate ends here."

Tiger Lamb stepped in to block his path. "Old man, leave the bag. I really do mean this for your own good."

The old man gave a cold laugh. "Brat, I've known for some time that you and that one with glasses are up to no good."

"When that bald man came earlier, the two of you only shouted from the sidelines without lifting a hand, like a pair of crosstalk comedians[1] putting on an act. When the fight was nearly over, you rushed in pretending to be heroes. You are working with the bald man, aren't you?"

Old Smoke Cannon said this loud enough for Chubby Qin to hear.

At this moment, Chubby Qin was standing at the same spot with a dumbfounded expression. He had no idea why Tiger Lamb and Old Smoke Cannon were arguing. But after hearing Old Smoke Cannon's explanation, he began to understand a little.

Old Smoke Cannon went on, "On the way up the mountain, that bald man tried to rob us once. When we reached the flower field, he tried to rob us again. You pretended to play the good guy, but in truth you were using him to test our strength.

"If we were weak, you would have let the bald man rob us once. You knew that once we lost the flowers, we would risk everything to make up the loss. Then by nightfall, you would swoop in and clean us out. If we proved strong, you would have the bald man hold back and then pick us off one by one when we scattered at night.

"Chubby boy, that one called Bai Sha who came with you probably died on the way down. Those two girls are likely dead as well. If we don't act now, we'll end up dying at the hands of these two brats."

Hearing this, Chubby Qin quickly tucked away his bag and hefted his axe.

The man with the golden-rimmed glasses adjusted his frames, smiling at the two of them. "Don't be rash. Listen to my advice.

"Old Smoke Cannon, you have sharp eyes, but it's a pity you're too greedy. With your cultivation, if you had left earlier, we wouldn't have been able to stop you. But you endured until now. I bet you can hardly stand, can you?

"It's clear you're a man who has roamed the jianghu, but this must be your first time on Mount Bitterfog. Do you really think that resin can filter out all the toxic fog here? If that were true, Mount Bitterfog would have long been the territory of Smoking Cultivators."

Old Smoke Cannon chuckled. "The resin can't block it all, but it can filter most of it. That's still far better than the way you've been stubbornly holding out. If you don't believe me, try it for yourselves."

The man in the golden-rimmed glasses sighed, took out a handkerchief, and wiped his lenses. "I shouldn't waste my breath on fools like you. It's far too tiring."

Tiger Lamb drew his dagger and shouted, "Finish him off and be done with it. If he wants to die, who can stop him?"

Old Smoke Cannon turned his gaze toward Chubby Qin.

Chubby Qin was still hesitating, trying to decide the right moment to act, when Tiger Lamb suddenly lunged forward and kicked him square in the face.

As a Gourmandism Cultivator, Chubby Qin possessed extraordinary physical abilities when he was well-fed. He could run, fight, endure blows, and even resist poisons to some extent.

However, when it came to each ability on its own, he was never the best. In fighting, for instance, he was clearly inferior to Tiger Lamb, who was a Martial Cultivator.

The kick landed squarely on Chubby Qin's face, catching him completely off guard. Tiger Lamb followed up with a thrust at his stomach, but Chubby Qin managed to dodge, though only barely. Even so, Tiger Lamb's fist smashed into his chin. Chubby Qin staggered and toppled to the ground, and another brutal kick to the face knocked him unconscious.

Tiger Lamb was about to finish him off when a cloud of yellow smoke swept in from behind. He hurriedly dodged, only for a spray of sparks to flash across his face.

Both Tiger Lamb and the man with the golden-rimmed glasses had only been bluffing when they claimed to be first-level cultivators. Old Smoke Cannon, however, truly was one.

The man with the golden-rimmed glasses couldn't handle Old Smoke Cannon alone, and Old Smoke Cannon wasn't about to give Tiger Lamb another chance to stab Chubby Qin.

He actually admired the fat kid. During the clash with the bald man, Chubby Qin had been the only one to truly fight. Once he dealt with the man in glasses and Tiger Lamb, there was still the chance of running into the bald man on the way down. Therefore, it would be useful to keep this fatty as an ally.

Upon realizing it was impossible to kill Chubby Qin quickly, Tiger Lamb rushed to join forces with the man in glasses. Old Smoke Cannon fought them with smoke and fire from his pipe. At first, he held the upper hand and thought he could finish them both within the time it took to smoke one pouch. But even when he was done smoking the pouch of tobacco, he still hadn't won.

At this point, the situation had turned dire for Old Smoke Cannon. Without smoke, a smoking cultivator's strength would drop by an entire tier.

The old man fought as he retreated. The man in glasses brandished his folding fan and pressed his attacks even more fiercely.

Something was off. The man with the golden-rimmed glasses had been on the mountain all day as well, yet he showed no signs of poisoning.

Old Smoke Cannon was flanked front and back, with nowhere left to retreat. At last, he exhaled the final breath of smoke he had been holding in, sending it at the man in glasses like a blade.

The man in glasses flicked his fan, scattering the smoke. However, dispersing it did nothing. The smoke was poisonous, and Old Smoke Cannon had released a deadly toxin from his false tooth along with it.

Tiger Lamb clamped a hand over his nose and mouth, stumbling back again and again as tears streamed from his eyes and snot poured from his nose.

The man in glasses coughed twice but remained mostly unaffected. Old Smoke Cannon froze. Why hasn't this poisonous smoke taken him down? Is he a Poison Cultivator? Shit!

Even after Old Smoke Cannon had used up his last puff of smoke, he still had not found a chance to escape. Just then, he caught a strange fragrance carried on the wind from the flicking fan.

Oh no! There is poison on that fan! Old Smoke Cannon's vision blurred as he realized he had been poisoned!

1. The raw uses the term 相声 (xiàngsheng) and 一捧一逗 (yī pěng yī dòu). Xiangsheng is a traditional Chinese comic dialogue performance, often called crosstalk, where two performers rely on quick banter, wordplay, and jokes. This dynamic, called 一捧一逗 (yī pěng yī dòu), is essentially one performer playing the straight-faced role while the other delivers the punchlines and antics. Together, they create the rhythm and humor that define xiangsheng. ☜

Chapter 33: I Was Terrified

Chubby Qin opened his eyes. The sounds of fighting still rang in his ears.

He turned his head and saw that Old Smoke Cannon was still struggling against the man in gold-rimmed glasses. At this moment, his body was covered in wounds.

When Chubby Qin saw Old Smoke Cannon's sluggish movements and Tiger Lamb waiting for the chance to strike from behind, he thought, No! I need to help now, or he won't last much longer.

Chubby Qin grabbed his axe and was just getting up when the man in gold-rimmed glasses slipped behind Old Smoke Cannon and yanked him up by the hair.

As Old Smoke Cannon was yanked upward, he was forced to look up and Tiger Lamb took this opportunity to dart forward and slashed Old Smoke Cannon's throat with a dagger.

Blood sprayed out, and Old Smoke Cannon's body went limp, hitting the ground. Tiger Lamb stared at the blood and burst into wild laughter, coughing a few times between fits.

The toxic fog had eaten away at his body, and Old Smoke Cannon's smoke poison had weakened him further, leaving him in bad shape.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses, however, was in very good condition. As a Poison Cultivator, he had a strong resistance to the fog.

Of course, that didn't mean he could stay on Mount Bitterfog forever. With his current cultivation, he couldn't endure the toxic fog for too long, but a single day would be completely fine for him.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses pulled out a handkerchief, wiped the blood from his hand, and walked up to Chubby Qin.

"I am sure you heard what I said earlier. I hate talking to fools. I don't even want to waste any words on them. Compared to that old man, you don't seem like one. Since you are young, I am sure you won't be that stubborn. If you hand over the flowers, I'll let you live."

"No!" Chubby Qin clutched his pouch tight.

"Why are you even doing this?" Tiger Lamb laughed. "I counted for you. You picked twenty flowers. If you sold them down the mountain, you would get a little over a thousand yuan. Do you really think it's worth losing your life for that?"

"I won't give them to you!" Chubby Qin tightened his grip on his axe. He knew his life was worth far more than a thousand yuan, but he refused to let anyone take what was his so easily.

"I showed you respect, and you trampled it underfoot!" Tiger Lamb roared as he rushed in and kicked Chubby Qin in the chest.

This time, Chubby Qin was ready. He caught the blow on the haft of his axe, then swung back with a heavy strike.

Tiger Lamb dodged, but only barely. The smoke poison had weakened him, and in a one-on-one fight he would no longer have been a match for Chubby Qin. However, he didn't have to fight alone as the man in gold-rimmed glasses stood beside him.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses walked up to Chubby Qin, fanning himself lightly. "Brother, let me give you some advice. Leave the flowers and head down the mountain."

"In your dreams!" Chubby Qin spat. "I actually trusted you! You are such a cunning and vicious bastard! You've been tricking me this whole time!"

"Cunning and ruthless?" The man in gold-rimmed glasses shook his head. "I would say that I have been honest enough."

"Bullshit!" Chubby Qin spat. "Running into a bastard like you is just my rotten luck!"

"It's not bad luck. If you didn't meet me, you would have encountered someone else," the man in gold-rimmed glasses replied with a smile. "With that big axe of yours, it's clear this is your first time on the mountain. From the moment you left the inn, plenty of people already had their eyes on you."

Chubby Qin froze as he suddenly recalled Shopkeeper Feng's warning at the general store. No wonder he had told him not to bring the Azure Dragon Saber up the mountain.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses continued, "I know it's hard for you to accept this outcome. You worked all day to pick those twenty flowers, and now you're losing them. Anyone in your place would feel the same.

"But think about it. Do those twenty flowers really belong to you? Do you deserve them? Do you have the right to make money on your very first trip up Mount Bitterfog? You didn't know the way, you didn't know the rules, and you didn't even recognize a speckled-snake chrysanthemum. I taught you all that.

"At least next time you come, you'll know where to find flowers, which ones to pick, how much you can harvest, how much you can carry, and who you can trust. You've learned so much, and you've kept your life. All it cost you was a little over a thousand yuan. Do you really think that isn't worth it?"

Hearing this, Chubby Qin pulled out a piece of dried ration and shoved it into his mouth. It was his last piece and his final reserve of strength. No matter what the man in gold-rimmed glasses said, he would never hand over his speckled-snake chrysanthemums.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses felt a twinge of disappointment. He had planned to wait until Chubby Qin gave up resisting, then take his life with ease. But since Chubby Qin refused to yield, he would have to change tactics.

"Lil' bro, I'll give you five minutes to think it over." The man in gold-rimmed glasses opened his folding fan and waved it slowly. A faint fragrance drifted out from a hidden mechanism within the fan's ribs.

The fragrance spread, but Chubby Qin didn't notice a thing. He had no idea the man in gold-rimmed glasses was a Poison Cultivator, since he had fainted when Old Smoke Cannon found out about it.

"I'll say it again. Saying even one more word to fools is a waste of my energy. I trust you're not a fool." The man in gold-rimmed glasses was still releasing poison when he suddenly noticed someone running toward them from the distance.

"Chubby Bro, my friends! I found a great spot! There are tons of speckled-snake chrysanthemums there!"

Chubby Qin turned and saw Li Banfeng running toward them.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses froze and took a step back, while Tiger Lamb quickly drew his dagger.

Why is he back? Didn't he already head down the mountain?

Li Banfeng rushed up to Chubby and said, "We've hit the jackpot! There's a bamboo grove just ahead, and speckled-snake chrysanthemums are growing all over it. Hurry, let's go take a look!"

Chubby Qin grabbed Li Banfeng by the arm. "Brother, something bad has happened."

"What is it?"

"Old Smoke Cannon is dead."

"Dead? How did he die?"

"They killed him!" Chubby Qin pointed to the corpse lying in the distance.

When Li Banfeng saw the bloodstains on the ground and Old Smoke Cannon's body, his legs gave out at once.

"Wh-what happened? Wh-what are you doing? Weren't we supposed to be teammates? Weren't we supposed to watch each other's backs?" Li Banfeng stammered in terror, barely able to speak.

Chubby Qin grabbed Li Banfeng. "Brother, it's no use saying that now. From the start, they were after our things. Let's fight them together!"

"F-fight… fight them…" Li Banfeng trembled all over.

Tiger Lamb laughed. "Big Bro, you said we should watch out for this guy. But look at him. Don't you see what a coward he is?"

The man in gold-rimmed glasses sighed. "Seems I misjudged him. Tell me, you are Bai Sha, right? You said you found a good spot?"

"I-I did…" Tears welled up in Li Banfeng's eyes.

"You really did?"

"I really did. I'm not lying," Li Banfeng said as he untied the pouch at his waist. It was stuffed full. "I picked all of these over there."

The man in gold-rimmed glasses smiled. "You've done us a great service. Squat here for now, and later you can take us there."

"O-okay…" Li Banfeng stood frozen in a daze.

Tiger Lamb stepped forward and kicked Li Banfeng. "I told you to squat. Didn't you hear me?"

Li Banfeng hurriedly squatted down on the ground.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses smiled at Chubby Qin. "Well then, what now? Your friend's useless."

"You are a fucking coward!" Chubby Qin spat. Seeing Li Banfeng squatting on the ground, wiping away his tears, he knew he couldn't count on him. He tightened his grip on his axe, ready to fight the man in gold-rimmed glasses to the death.

Tiger Lamb rolled up his sleeves, ready to help the man in gold-rimmed glasses finish Chubby Qin off.

Li Banfeng was still sobbing as he crouched on the ground. "Weren't we supposed to be teammates? We agreed to watch each other's backs. How could you kill someone? Why do you have to kill people?"

Whoosh!

Tiger Lamb suddenly felt a chill at his ankle, and before the pain set in, a warm liquid was already trickling down it.

Someone had cut him, and he was bleeding. The gash was deep, severing his tendons. In that instant, Tiger Lamb realized that he could no longer move his feet

Tiger Lamb looked down at Li Banfeng, who was still crouched on the ground, wiping away his tears as he cried, "Isn't it the speckled-snake chrysanthemums you want? There are plenty over there. I'll give them all to you… Just don't kill me."

After saying that, Li Banfeng pulled out his pouch and seemed to be handing it over to the man in gold-rimmed glasses.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses froze for an instant, then saw Li Banfeng suddenly whip the pouch straight at him.

It wasn't aimed at his hand, but at his head. Within five meters, Li Banfeng's aim never failed. The pouch shot toward his face, and the man in gold-rimmed glasses instinctively raised a hand to block it. But the pouch had been left open, and its contents burst out, spilling all over the man's face.

The vomit was sour and sticky, and the man in gold-rimmed glasses couldn't open his eyes for a moment.

"I've given you all the flowers. Please, just spare me." Li Banfeng sobbed as he stood up, revealing the small sickle hidden in his sleeve. With a swift motion, he slashed Tiger Lamb's throat.

Tiger Lamb didn't react at all. Li Banfeng, as a Wanderlust Cultivator, was incredibly fast, and as a Homebound Cultivator, his presence was difficult to detect.

Having just emerged from his dwelling, his combat strength was at its peak. On top of that, the effect of Mount Bitterfog's toxic fog had mostly worn off, leaving Li Banfeng in a far better state than Tiger Lamb. But most crucial of all, Li Banfeng's state of mind was something Tiger Lamb could not understand.

Tiger Lamb was not the only one. Even Chubby Qin could not understand what was happening to Li Banfeng. He was crying and begging for mercy while at the same time swinging his blade and killing. His actions defied all sense of logic.

Tiger Lamb clutched his throat and collapsed to the ground.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses had just finished wiping vomit from his eyes. He didn't even realize what had happened when the world suddenly went dark.

Li Banfeng had pulled the pouch back over his head while crying, "Please don't kill me! I'll give you all my flowers!"

Even as he sobbed pitifully, he swung his sickle in a passing motion, as if it were done without thought.

"Let go! I won't kill you, I swear I won't!"

The man in gold-rimmed glasses struggled with all his strength, but Li Banfeng clutched the pouch tightly over his head, leaving him unable to break free.

"I really gave you the flowers, so please don't kill me." Li Banfeng sobbed endlessly as the sickle came down, piercing through the pouch and hooking into the man's skull.

"I won't kill you! I swear I won't!" The man in gold-rimmed glasses couldn't break free. With his brain injured, he began to lose control of his limbs!

Li Banfeng yanked the sickle free and hooked it into his head again. "I told you I gave you the flowers. Why do you still want to kill me?"

"I really didn't want to kill you, I swear I didn't!" the man screamed hoarsely.

What is even going on? the man in gold-rimmed glasses thought. He couldn't figure it out. His brain had been badly damaged and he had lost the ability to think.

"And you still insist on killing me?" Li Banfeng wept, but even through his tears he kept swinging the sickle. It plunged in and out of the man's skull again and again.

"I never meant to kill you!" the man's voice cracked and warped.

"Can't you just not kill me?"

Splurt! The sickle drove into his skull again.

"I swear I won't kill you!"

"Please don't kill me!" Li Banfeng sobbed as he ripped the sickle free once more. Crack!

"I… I…" The man in gold-rimmed glasses could no longer form words. He tried to lift his fan, but when it rose, it only shook uncontrollably.

Li Banfeng drove the sickle into his skull six times in succession. The pouch turned red, and a pale white fluid oozed out, dripping in thick streaks.

The man in gold-rimmed glasses convulsed all over.

Li Banfeng sobbed, "I'm so scared. Why do you all have to kill me?"

Chubby Qin stood frozen, at a loss for words.

Li Banfeng's body was thin and frail, hardly the kind that looked capable of fighting. Even Old Chen of the Dark Star Bureau could not figure out how he could win while outnumbered five to one and even cause serious injuries. But if he could have witnessed this moment, there was a chance he would then understand.

Watching Li Banfeng hack again and again until the man's skullcap lifted, Chubby Qin quickly urged, "Brother Li, I think he's already dead."

"Dead?" Li Banfeng blinked and stopped crying. His expression and his voice became normal again. It was as if nothing had happened a moment ago.

He then looked at Chubby Qin and said with complete seriousness, "Brother, you know… I was really terrified just now."

Li Banfeng said he was terrified, and he said it with absolute seriousness.

Chubby Qin felt dazed and cautiously asked, "Brother Li… are you mentally crazy or something?"

Li Banfeng shook his head firmly. "I'm not crazy. I went to get it checked at a hospital, and the doctor said I am not crazy."

Chapter 34: Don't Scare Me Again

Chubby Qin looked at the corpse on the ground, then at Li Banfeng. Li Banfeng just said that he had been terrified just now, but Chubby Qin had no idea how to comfort him.

"I was really scared he would kill me, so I killed him first. That way, he couldn't kill me," Li Banfeng said, giving Chubby Qin what sounded like a perfectly logical explanation.

Chubby Qin felt that… he couldn't find any flaw in that explanation.

Li Banfeng walked over to Old Smoke Cannon's corpse, untied the cloth pouch attached to him, and counted the harvest inside. There were ninety-one speckled-snake chrysanthemums in total, which was worth about eight thousand yuan if sold.

"All this killing for less than eight thousand yuan?" Li Banfeng thought of the prices in Medicine King Gully. A life shouldn't be this cheap.

Chubby Qin sighed. "You're a foreigner, so you don't understand the rules here. Money is too hard to earn and far too easy to spend. In some remote places, people will kill for just a hundred yuan, not to mention on Mount Bitterfog, a region of peril where human life is as worthless as grass."

Li Banfeng handed him the pouch. "Take this."

Chubby Qin shook his head. "You killed those two bastards and avenged Old Smoke Cannon. His belongings rightfully belong to you."

"Hold onto it for now. We'll talk about it once we're down the mountain," Li Banfeng said, handing the pouch over to Chubby Qin.

Chubby Qin noticed Li Banfeng's empty hands and asked, "Where are your flowers?"

"That's a long story," Li Banfeng replied, pulling a few black-and-white beads from his pocket. "Brother, do you recognize these?"

Chubby Qin took the beads, and his eyes lit up. "These are Speckled-Snake Pills! I've seen them before. Where did you get them?"

They were indeed Speckled-Snake Pills, just as Li Banfeng had guessed. Since he dared to take them out and show Chubby Qin, he had already prepared an explanation.

"On the way down the mountain, I ran into an old woman. She said she was from a medicinal trading house and wanted to trade pills for my speckled-snake chrysanthemums. Ten stalks for one pill."

At first, Li Banfeng had considered asking for five stalks per pill, but that would only cover the cost, and no one would agree to it. Offering double the cost seemed far more reasonable.

Hearing this, Chubby Qin stared at the Speckled-Snake Pills in his hand, then turned to Li Banfeng. "You made the trade?"

"I did!" Li Banfeng nodded.

"All of them?"

"All of them!" Li Banfeng said proudly. "Brother, tell me, did I strike a good deal or not?"

"I'd say this deal… was probably a scam." Chubby Qin handed the pills back to him. "Bro, I may not know how to tell a real pill from a fake, but I can tell when the numbers don't make sense. A single Speckled-Snake Pill sells for at least ten thousand. Ten stalks of speckled-snake chrysanthemum go for only eight hundred fifty. Who would ever make that trade with you? You were almost certainly scammed."

"Is that so?" Li Banfeng smacked his lips, put the pills away, and offered no further explanation.

He couldn't explain. He couldn't tell Chubby Qin that these pills hadn't come from the old woman at all, but had popped out from the bronze lotus flower.

Chubby Qin let out a long sigh. "This trip to Mount Bitterfog has made me understand one thing. We are still far too naive and inexperienced. This is money that we're not qualified to earn yet. I got duped by that man in glasses, and you by that old woman. Earlier, you said there was a place with plenty of flowers. Was that true?"

Li Banfeng gave a cold laugh. "Of course it wasn't true. I made it up when I saw they were about to attack you, hoping to lure them after me. I thought if they followed, I could deal with them on the road. I didn't expect that they would want to kill you first, so I had no choice but to take them out here."

Chubby Qin nodded. "You're sharper than me. Honestly, you're much sharper. Nevertheless, you don't have to feel sad. You were scammed out of your speckled-snake chrysanthemums, but Old Smoke Cannon's speckled-snake chrysanthemums are all yours now. Let's hurry down the mountain."

Just as the two were about to leave the grove, a flicker of firelight suddenly appeared in the distance.

Chubby Qin froze. "Torches."

Li Banfeng's eyes flashed as he realized that things were about to turn bad.

The people coming their way were most likely with the man in gold-rimmed glasses, and it was probably that bald guy. He wasn't much of a fighter, but he had more people with him. If they wanted to win, they had to strike first.

Li Banfeng said to Chubby Qin, "Find somewhere to hide, and do it as quickly as possible. When I make a move, that's when you act. I'll hide the bodies first."

Chubby Qin said, "I'll hide with you! There's no time. Forget the bodies."

Nevertheless, Li Banfeng dragged the two corpses deeper into the forest. Chubby Qin grew anxious as he watched. The trees here were too sparse. How could anyone hope to hide a body in a place like this?

Li Banfeng couldn't find anywhere to hide the bodies, and the torches were closing in fast. When he saw Chubby Qin scramble up a tree, he slipped out of sight, pulled out a key, and unlocked the door to his Pocket Dwelling. In one swift motion, he hurled the two corpses inside.

It felt inauspicious to store corpses in his house, but he had no choice.

Then he stepped out of the Pocket Dwelling, put away the key, and crouched in the grass to watch in silence.

The bald man entered the forest first, carrying Leafy over his shoulder. She was bound tight and sobbing the whole way. Two men followed behind, carrying Nectarine. Her body was covered in wounds. If not for the faint rise and fall of her chest, Li Banfeng would have thought she was dead.

They threw Leafy and Nectarine into a clearing. One of the bald man's lackeys stepped forward, pinched Leafy on the cheek, and said, "Quit crying. What are you crying for? I know how deep I can go. I promise it won't hurt!"

As he spoke, he reached for her, but the bald man barked, "Don't you dare! Didn't I tell you to wait until Big Bro comes back?"

"Do we really have to wait for Big Bro for everything…" the lackey complained.

The bald man kicked him. "With me here, even if it's just a single grain of rice, Big Bro eats first, not to mention a woman."

The lackey didn't dare say another word. Then, the other lackey spotted Old Smoke Cannon's corpse.

"Third Brother, this old guy was killed by Big Bro, right?"

The bald man told two men to keep watch over Leafy and Nectarine, then went to examine Old Smoke Cannon's body himself.

"Judging from the cut, this must be Second Brother's work. However, this old thing has been poisoned and this is definitely Big Bro's way of killing." The bald man spoke with pride, pointing out the differences between the techniques of Tiger Lamb and the man in gold-rimmed glasses.

Li Banfeng wasn't in the mood to listen to the bald man go on about differences. The man in gold-rimmed glasses and Tiger Lamb were already dead.

At this moment, he only cared about one thing, which was that the group had split up. The bald man and his three lackeys had separated into pairs.

With the sickle in hand, Li Banfeng crept toward the two guarding Leafy and Nectarine.

One of them was groping at Nectarine, trying to take full advantage of her. The other slipped his hand into Leafy's clothes, and when her crying grew too loud, he quickly covered her mouth.

Closer. I just need to move a little bit closer to them.

Li Banfeng had no way to attack from a distance. For his ambush to work, he needed to get close enough. He gauged the distance and figured he was still about twenty meters away, which was still a little too far. He crept forward a few more steps until he was roughly fifteen meters away.

I just need to move a little bit closer.

Crack!

Li Banfeng's foot came down too hard, crunching a dead leaf. At once, he held his breath and pressed himself against a tree, staying perfectly still.

The two lackeys had probably noticed him. The ambush seemed to have failed. If all four of them swarmed him, he would need a plan to withstand the first wave and then let Chubby Qin come down to assist.

If things really didn't work out, he could always run. After all, he wasn't that close to the two women. But even after thinking for a moment, Li Banfeng didn't hear any movements.

Li Banfeng peeked at the two from behind the big tree and pleasantly discovered that the two lackeys were still groping Nectarine and Leafy. They did not notice Li Banfeng at all.

This was thanks to the gift of a Homebound Cultivator known as Being Ignored.

It did not mean he ignored others, but that others could not sense his presence. They had overlooked Li Banfeng entirely and hadn't heard his footsteps at all.

In the desolate forest, the crack of a dry leaf was nothing unusual. Their attention stayed fixed on the two girls.

Since they hadn't noticed, Li Banfeng pressed forward. With only ten meters left, he stopped sneaking.

He suddenly burst out from behind the tree. Relying on the speed of a Wanderlust Cultivator, he reached one of the lackeys in less than a second. Before the lackey even saw his face, Li Banfeng had already slit his throat with a single slash.

The other lackey ducked behind Leafy. Li Banfeng pushed her aside and swung again. Sparks burst as the sickle struck, but it failed to cut through the man's skin.

Nectarine cried out, "He's a Physique Cultivator!"

The bastard had a natural suit of armor!

Li Banfeng struck again, but his sickle couldn't even cut through the lackey's skin. By now, the bald man and another lackey had already rushed over.

He was in dire straits, about to face three attackers at once. Fear overtook him. His body trembled, and his eyes turned red.

The bald man glanced at the dead lackey on the ground, then at Li Banfeng.

"Well, you little bastard, you really are a mongrel," the bald man said this with eyes that gleamed coldly as he tilted his head. "You dared lay your hands on my men! Today I'll chop off your hands, scoop out your brain, and make you eat it yourself!"

Hearing this, Li Banfeng turned and bolted. He wasn't fast enough. When he reached a tree, the Physique Cultivator with armored skin blocked his path.

The bald man and his lackey followed unhurriedly.

Li Banfeng's face contorted in terror, his knees bending as though he were about to kneel.

The bald man walked forward slowly, each step deliberate, until he stopped beneath the tree. The smile on his face carried a chilling menace. "Kneel! Kneel properly, and I'll give you a quick death. Kneel, I said! Are you deaf? Can't you hear me?"

Bang!

The bald man heard a dull thud.

Chubby Qin had leapt down from the tree.

The bald man had no idea why Chubby Qin had jumped down and he certainly had no idea why Li Banfeng had run straight to this tree.

Chubby Qin's axe landed squarely on the lackey's head. Skull fragments and scattered brain matter splattered across the bald man's face.

The bald man's twisted grin vanished, for he saw that Li Banfeng had raised his sickle.

As a Martial Cultivator, the bald man reacted with lightning speed. He raised his dagger, dropped into a horse stance, and braced with all his strength. A sharp, agonizing pain drove him to his knees.

Li Banfeng still held his sickle high, but holding a blade and bringing it down on someone were two very different things. He raised the weapon with his right hand and kicked with his left foot straight into the groin. The movement flowed seamlessly, and because the bald man had crouched into a horse stance, the outcome was brutal.

The bald man knelt before Li Banfeng. Inside, he cursed, Why is this bastard so cunning and vicious?

Only the lackey with natural skin armor was left. Like everyone else, this lackey had endured a whole day on Mount Bitterfog, and his condition was far from ideal.

Chubby Qin, however, was in better shape. A Gourmandism Cultivator's resilience was sturdier than that of a Physique Cultivator.

Chubby Qin swung his axe again and again, hacking with relentless force.

The lackey held out behind his armored skin as long as he could, but at last it failed. The axe bit from his left shoulder to his right ribs, cleaving half his body apart.

At the same time, Li Banfeng hooked his sickle beneath the bald man's chin. The bald man's jaw quivered as he stammered, "I just… just wanted to scare you. I didn't mean to kill anyone. I wouldn't kill anyone. I swear I wouldn't…"

"You just wanted to scare me?" Li Banfeng blinked and asked with complete seriousness. "Is that really true?"

"It's true!" The bald man's jaw was pinned and couldn't nod, but his eyes shone with sincerity.

"Then don't ever do that again. I get frightened so easily," Li Banfeng told him earnestly.

For a moment, the bald man's eyes lit with hope. "I'll never scare you again, never-"

Before he could finish, Li Banfeng pulled out his trowel, drove it into the man's skull, and pried his head open.

Chapter 35: Where Did the Corpses Go?

Chubby Qin glanced at the bald man's corpse. His skull cup had been ripped off, and his brain matter was spilling everywhere. Chubby Qin felt sick looking at it.

"Brother Li, why are you always so ruthless, striking at the skull every time?" Chubby Qin asked.

Li Banfeng explained, "I'm softhearted. I can't bring myself to hit their vital spots. The skull is tough and can take a hit. If I strike anywhere else, I'm afraid it'll hurt them."

Isn't the skull a vital spot? What kind of twisted logic is that? Chubby Qin thought.

Chubby Qin felt his comprehension ability was lacking and he couldn't understand Li Qi's reasoning. He turned his gaze to the two women lying in the forest.

Leafy was crying. He couldn't tell how bad Nectarine was doing, but she was definitely still alive.

Leafy fed Nectarine two pills, and she gradually regained the ability to move.

When Nectarine woke up, she couldn't believe what she was seeing. Bai Sha and Chubby Qin had taken out four people, the bald man and his three underlings, that easily…? Just how high are the cultivation levels of these two young men?

Nectarine asked Leafy what happened, but Leafy had no idea too. She had been so terrified earlier that she did not see the fight clearly.

However, Chubby Qin didn't understand how it had happened either, even though he had been involved in the fight.

The entire fight happened so quickly. The man in gold-rimmed glasses had a total of six people in his group, but in the blink of an eye, they were all gone. Chubby Qin hadn't even had time to process what had happened.

Which Daoist paths did those six belong to? What techniques did they have?

One of them had armored skin, so he was obviously a Physique Cultivator. However, Chubby Qin had no impression of what kind of cultivators the others were because Li Banfeng simply hadn't given them any chance to act.

Chubby Qin had thought that the man in gold-rimmed glasses was the most cunning and ruthless person he had ever seen.

However, looking at Li Banfeng now, Chubby Qin wasn't so sure whether the man in gold-rimmed glasses truly counted as cunning and ruthless.

The bald man and his three underlings had stolen many speckled-snake chrysanthemums, totaling 176 stalks. Among them were Nectarine's 38 stalks and Leafy's 61 stalks.

Nectarine and Leafy were deeply grateful that they were even able to make it out alive. They didn't expect to get their flowers back either, but Li Banfeng returned the flowers to them.

The remaining 77 stalks and Old Smoke Cannon's 91 stalks were split evenly between Li Banfeng and Chubby Qin.

Chubby Qin stared blankly at Old Smoke Cannon's corpse.

If it weren't for Old Smoke Cannon, Chubby Qin would have surely died at Tiger Lamb's hands. Regardless of what Old Smoke Cannon's intention might have been at the time, it could not be denied that he had saved Chubby Qin's life. Chubby Qin didn't want to just leave his body out in the open like this, left to be torn apart by wild animals and rot under the elements.

Li Banfeng handed a spade to Chubby Qin. "Be quick. We need to head down the mountain."

While Chubby Qin dug a pit, Li Banfeng carefully searched the corpses of the bald man and his underlings.

The bald man had over a thousand yuan on him, while the underlings carried three to five hundred each. Altogether, it came to more than two thousand two hundred yuan.

Since both Nectarine and Leafy had suffered losses, Li Banfeng gave each of them three hundred yuan as consolation. He split the remaining money with Chubby Qin; they each got eight hundred yuan.

The bald man's machete was a pretty good weapon, so Li Banfeng kept it for himself. The weapons of the three underlings were ordinary, so he let the others pick from them freely.

As for whatever he might be able to loot from the man in gold-rimmed glasses and Tiger Lamb, he would have to wait until he was back in the Pocket Dwelling to find out. In any case, since Li Banfeng had taken those two down by himself, he decided to keep all of their belongings for himself.

After burying Old Smoke Cannon, Chubby Qin carried Nectarine on his back, and the four of them descended the mountain together.

As they walked along the mountain path, Nectarine was bleeding very badly. The intense metallic stench of her blood made Chubby Qin's nose twitch in displeasure.

"You don't seem like a Physique Cultivator," Chubby Qin commented, suspecting Nectarine had lied about her Daoist path. "Physique Cultivators can endure a beating. How did you end up this badly injured?"

Leafy knew the truth but said nothing. After all, a cultivator's Daoist path was a private matter.

However, Nectarine unexpectedly revealed it herself. She smiled and said, "I'm a Hedonism Cultivator. We were just strangers who had gathered together by chance, so I lied when we introduced ourselves."

"So you're a Hedonism Cultivator." Chubby Qin sighed. "Looks like those assholes took advantage of you."

Chubby Qin realized that Nectarine had likely suffered much more than just a beating in the hands of the bald man's underlings.

"No, they didn't take advantage of me. They wanted to keep me for their boss," Nectarine said with a bitter laugh. "It would have been better if they had. I can't defeat them on my feet, but if we had fought lying down, not one of them would have survived."

Li Banfeng gave a derisive snort. "You're this badly injured, but you're still so sharp-tongued."

Nectarine's eyes gleamed with a suggestive look as she turned to Li Banfeng. "You think I'm bluffing? My abilities aren't just talk. Aren't you also a Hedonism Cultivator? Why don't we compete with each other on our skills?"

Li Banfeng turned to Chubby Qin. "I already have a partner."

Chubby Qin looked at Leafy. "I'm a Gourmandism Cultivator. I can eat a lot."

Nectarine let out a sigh, then she looked at Li Banfeng. "You've been picking chrysanthemums[1] all along this time. Don't you want to try something different? Personally, I think flat is better than round."

Leafy flushed bright red, the color spreading from her cheeks down to her neck.

Li Banfeng was about to speak, but then he saw Chubby Qin explaining to Leafy, "I'm a Gourmandism Cultivator. I'm really a Gourmandism Cultivator. I can eat a lot. Later, I'll treat you all to a meal, and I'll eat it in front of you so you can see."

The four of them made their way to the foot of the mountain. Nectarine took out a pocket watch and saw that it was already eight o'clock.

They had already traversed the mountain for more than ten kilometers. Li Banfeng had sensed long ago that he had already met his daily foundational cultivation requirement as a Wanderlust Cultivator. In fact, he had met it in excess.

Mount Bitterfog was a region of peril. According to Li Banfeng's senses as a Wanderlust Cultivator, walking a half a kilometer here was equivalent to walking 1.5 to 2.5 kilometers in the Inner Gully. That meant he had accumulated enough to cover his cultivation requirement for the next two days as well.

Li Banfeng found that regions of peril seemed to be quite important for his cultivation.

Why is that?

Li Banfeng was dizzy from inhaling the toxic fog, and he could not remember the reason for its importance.

After sleeping in the Pocket Dwelling last night, he had also completed his homework as a Homebound Cultivator.

If I can't remember the reason, then so be it. For now, I just need to exchange the speckled-snake chrysanthemums for money and buy a train ticket to get back to Yuezhou.

As for how exactly he should make the exchange, Li Banfeng was still thinking about it.

He definitely didn't want to sell the flowers just as they were; he wanted to sell them as pills.

That will all depend on the bronze lotus flower's capabilities.

Crossing the Bridge of No Regrets, Li Banfeng's group left the toxic fog behind, and the four of them gulped down the fresh air desperately.

Nectarine vomited twice, while Chubby Qin sat by the bridge gasping for breath, sweat streaming down his deathly pale face.

Leafy took out all her Cooling Powder and distributed it to the group. After resting for a little over half an hour, Nectarine managed to stand up and walk on her own, albeit with difficulty.

It was proof that cultivators had superior bodies; injuries healed quickly. However, they would not be able to purge the toxic fog they had inhaled that swiftly.

They hobbled along, finding it extremely difficult to keep walking. Li Banfeng had only recently left the Pocket Dwelling, but even he felt his legs going weak beneath him.

Leafy reminded everyone, "Big Bros and Big Sis, when you get home, make sure to rest well for a while. The toxic fog is very harmful to the body, and you mustn't take it lightly."

Li Banfeng sighed. "Is all this really worth it just for a few thousand yuan?"

Nectarine snorted. "If you were rich, you wouldn't have risked your life with us. We've all got cheap lives, so why bother thinking such nonsense?"

Chubby Qin glanced at her. "We risked our lives because we've got nothing else. But with your looks and figure, do you really need to do the same? Plenty of men would be willing to provide for you for life."

"I don't need anyone to provide for me!" Nectarine gave a cold snort. "This old woman can support herself! And I can earn money to provide for my partner too!"

Li Banfeng frowned. "Your partner needs you to provide for them?"

"I want to do it, and I've got the ability!" Nectarine shot Li Banfeng a glare. "If you could provide for your partner, would you still have him out here risking his life with you?"

Chubby Qin broke out in a sweat. "I'm a Gourmandism Cultivator… How can I convince you…"

"I know you're a Gourmandism Cultivator. That's nothing to be ashamed of," Nectarine said with a smile. "My partner isn't a Hedonism Cultivator either. She's a Homebound Cultivator."

Leafy suddenly understood. "No wonder you asked me about Homebound Cultivators earlier, Big Sis. That must be really tough on you. It's inconvenient for Homebound Cultivators to get a job since they would have to leave home."

Nectarine shook her head. "That's not the main reason why I want to provide for my partner. She's a young lady. I don't want her exposed to the public eye."

Li Banfeng finally understood why Nectarine was so protective of her partner. "So, your partner is a young lady."

Chubby Qin and Leafy nodded in understanding. "So, it's a young lady."

A few steps later, Li Banfeng turned his face toward Nectarine, the revelation finally sinking in. "Your partner is a woman!"

"So what if she is? What's wrong with that?" Nectarine frowned slightly. "You think round is better than flat. I think flat is better than long. What's wrong with that?"

Li Banfeng nodded. "Nothing wrong with that."

Nectarine turned and pinched Leafy's cheek gently. "Tell me, little sister, am I right?"

"Big Sis, that… uh… Yes, you're right…" Leafy muttered, edging away from her.

Chubby Qin was in a confused daze. "Well… I'm a Gourmandism Cultivator."

As they continued walking, the number of people they saw on the road increased gradually. Li Banfeng grew more alert, worried that someone might try to snatch their flowers.

He suspected that no real laws or restrictions were being enforced in this place.

Nectarine, on the other hand, was rather at ease. "Relax. Once you're at the foot of the mountain, everyone has to follow the rules. At the very least, they can't make a move openly. Otherwise, they'll never be able to set foot in Medicine King Gully again!"

Amid the thick fog, banners of all colors fluttered.

The major medicinal trading houses were right before them. It was finally time for them to make some money.

Chubby Qin's and Nectarine's eyes lit up in joy. Even the normally shy Leafy couldn't hide her excitement.

The managers of the trading houses had already set up stalls to purchase flowers. However, when they approached, Li Banfeng heard a heated argument.

"Twenty-five yuan? Do you think you're buying cabbages?"

What's being bought for twenty-five yuan? Li Banfeng wondered.

Leafy looked at the bag of speckled-snake chrysanthemums, unable to believe it was real.

Chubby Qin went up and asked around. He returned soon after, grinding his teeth in anger.

"Let's go. We're not selling!"

The people nearby shouted along with him. "We're not selling! We'd rather let them rot in our hands than sell!"

"This is daylight robbery! We absolutely won't sell!"

The manager of the Liu Family's medicinal trading house bared his yellow teeth in a grin. "Don't sell if you don't want to. If you think I'm offering too little, then go try the others."

It was the same with the other trading houses.

The proprietress of the Chu Family's medicinal trading house touched up her makeup as she spoke. "Twenty-five yuan per stalk. That's the price this year. If you want to sell them to me, do it now. If not, I'll be closing up my stall."

Why has it dropped to twenty-five yuan per stalk? Wasn't it eighty-five before? Li Banfeng wondered.

The situation was like this.

The employees of the major trading houses who had gone up the mountain had discovered a cliff covered in patches of speckled-snake chrysanthemums.

They harvested the flowers and went down the mountain, dividing the harvest among the trading houses. Each trading house obtained over a thousand stalks.

With over a thousand stalks in their inventory, none of the trading houses were desperate to buy more stock. Instead, the trading houses conspired together and pushed the buying price down.

However, they had pushed it far too low.

Four stalks made one Speckled-Snake Pill. At twenty-five yuan per stalk, the total cost for one pill would be one hundred yuan. However, the pill sold for ten thousand; the profit was a hundred times the cost.

Nectarine sat on the ground in silence for a long while.

Leafy lowered her head. Tears streamed down her face uncontrollably.

Chubby Qin stomped the ground in a rage. "I won't. I won't fucking sell to them! I don't believe there aren't any other buyers out there."

"Don't be an idiot!" Nectarine said through gritted teeth. "If you don't sell to them, who else can you sell to? All the major trading houses of Medicine King Gully are here!"

Chubby Qin glared at her. "Then we won't sell the flowers in Medicine King Gully. We'll go further away!"

Nectarine sneered. "What nonsense. Where else could you go? Have you ever even left Medicine King Gully?"

Chubby Qin plopped down on the ground. "Then I'll just wait for the Peddler to come!"

"The Peddler?" Nectarine let out a bitter laugh. "That man with the gold-rimmed glasses said it already. Once the speckled-snake chrysanthemums are picked, they rot within a day. We can't not sell them today; they'll be worthless tomorrow."

Chubby Qin snorted. "It's not like he was a good person. Who knows if he was telling the truth?"

Leafy sobbed. "But he really was telling the truth. Tomorrow, these flowers will be rotten, and they'll be useless."

Nectarine sighed. "Forget it. Let's just sell to them. A small profit is better than nothing. I've got thirty-eight stalks here. I can sell them for nearly a thousand yuan. Better than going away empty-handed. Our lives are cheap, after all!"

"Whatever! I'm still not selling to them!" Chubby Qin threw his bag on the ground, growing angrier the more he thought about it.

After a moment of thought, he suddenly turned to Li Banfeng.

He leaned close to Li Banfeng and asked quietly, "Brother Li, do you still have that pill?"

Li Banfeng nodded and handed him a Speckled-Snake Pill.

Chubby Qin glanced around. Then he led the group to a secluded spot. He held out the Speckled-Snake Pill to Leafy. "Little Sister, take a look. Is this real?"

Leafy was a Medicine Cultivator. She couldn't make Speckled-Snake Pills herself, but she could distinguish real Speckled-Snake Pills from fakes.

After examining it for a while, her eyes widened in shock. "Big Bro Fatty, this is indeed a Speckled-Snake Pill. Where did this come from?"

Chubby Qin shifted his gaze to Li Banfeng.

Li Banfeng said, "I ran into an old woman on the mountain. She said she'd trade pills for chrysanthemums. One pill for ten stalks."

"Ten stalks?" Leafy uttered, finding it hard to believe.

Nectarine felt the same way.

Ten stalks could only make two pills. That old woman would barely be breaking even with that trade.

However, the trading houses were raking in a hundredfold profit!

Leafy began to doubt her own judgment. "This pill must be fake…"

Chubby Qin snapped, "Didn't you just say it was real?"

"I'm afraid I misjudged. Who would ever trade it off for such a low price? Maybe I should just…" Leafy glanced toward the banners of the trading houses.

"I believe you!" Nectarine handed her bag to Li Banfeng. "You saved my life, and you returned my flowers. Whether it's real or fake, I trust you. Even if it's all a loss, I'll accept it!"

Chubby Qin also handed his bag to Li Banfeng.

Leafy hesitated for a moment, then she gave her bag to Li Banfeng as well.

Chubby Qin looked back at Mount Bitterfog. "I wonder if that old woman's still up there. Brother Li, could you help us…"

Chubby Qin didn't feel right asking Li Banfeng alone to make another trip up Mount Bitterfog. "Brother Li, I'll go with you."

Li Banfeng raised his brows. "I'm just going there to try my luck. You'll just add to my troubles if you come along."

Retracing his steps, Li Banfeng returned into the dense fog and opened the Pocket Dwelling.

He knew this was risky and could draw unnecessary suspicion to him.

But if he held on to the speckled-snake chrysanthemums without making a move, that would raise suspicion as well.

Furthermore, Li Banfeng had no connections or helpers, so there would only be more trouble in the future if he tried to sell the Speckled-Snake Pills alone.

Judging from the faces of the people running the trading houses, there was no telling what they might do. Li Banfeng truly didn't want to sell the Speckled-Snake Pills to the trading houses.

He would help Chubby Qin and the others and see if they could come up with a good alternative.

In any case, once he was done with this, he would return to Yuezhou. He would never come to this place again and would never encounter these people again.

Li Banfeng entered the Pocket Dwelling with the bags of speckled-snake chrysanthemums in his hands. He intended to figure out how to open the Arcane Crimson Lotus, but to his surprise, it was already open.

There were three lotus seeds on top of the seven-hole lotus core.

Where did these three lotus seeds come from?

Li Banfeng froze in shock.

He lifted a candle and looked around, noticing that something seemed to be missing from the room.

What's missing?

Where did the corpses of the man in gold-rimmed glasses and Tiger Lamb go?

Chapter 36: Corrosion Pill

Li Banfeng raised a candle and searched for the corpses of Tiger Lamb and the man in the gold-rimmed glasses.

However, he didn't find any bodies, only a pile of odds and ends in the corner and some tattered clothes. The way the fabric had been torn showed that an external force had ripped the garments from their bodies.

The items in the clothing were still there and Li Banfeng even found both of their wallets.

The cloth bag filled with speckled-snake chrysanthemums also remained, and all the flowers were perfectly intact.

Beside the bronze lotus flower, Li Banfeng also found the gold-rimmed glasses, a wooden spectacles case, and an iron tobacco tin. However, the folding fan of the man in the gold-rimmed glasses was missing.

Li Banfeng had been quite intrigued by that folding fan.

The folding fan gave off a strange fragrance, and both its material and craftsmanship were unusual. When closed, it could be wielded like an iron rod. When opened, its surface could serve as both a blade and a shield.

Where did the fan go? Did the bronze lotus flower swallow it?

And what about the bodies? Were they eaten too?

Maybe the bronze lotus flower devoured the two corpses and even took the folding fan.

But why would it want a weapon? Could it really wield one and fight?

These questions flickered through his mind.

Previously, when the bronze lotus flower had devoured speckled-snake chrysanthemums, it refined them into Speckled-Snake Pills.

And now, it devoured the corpses and the weapon. But why didn't it touch the speckled-snake chrysanthemums collected by Tiger Lamb and the man in the gold-rimmed glasses?

Maybe after feasting on flesh, it doesn't want vegetables anymore. So it ate the fan to pick its teeth, Li Banfeng thought. To him, this logic made perfect sense.

He then wondered, What could be inside the three lotus seeds?

Could it be that it refined the man in the gold-rimmed glasses and Tiger Lamb into pills as well?

Li Banfeng carefully plucked the three lotus seeds from the seven-holed lotus core, and the lotus immediately closed.

Two of the lotus seeds swelled in his palm. After about ten seconds, they both burst open, each releasing a crimson bead.

These beads were slightly larger than the Speckled-Snake Pills, their color a pure red, glowing with crystalline brilliance under the candlelight.

What kind of pills are these? Li Banfeng wondered.

As he pondered, the third lotus seed burst open, revealing a single bead the color of rust. It not only looked like rust but also felt like it.

Resting in his hand, it carried a heavy, coarse resistance that left a lingering, unpleasant drag against his skin.

The bead didn't just resist his touch. It pricked with pain, leaving his palm sore. Li Banfeng flung it aside and saw a red mark where it had pressed. Along with the sting came a faint numbness.

Am I Poisoned?

Li Banfeng didn't know how to react when the red mark suddenly split open, and a thin trickle of blood seeped out.

As the blood flowed, the sting and numbness faded away.

A Homebound Cultivator possessed strong regenerative abilities within his home. That bleeding just now had to be his body's way of purging the poison. However, Li Banfeng hadn't expected that a mere touch of the rust-colored bead could cause such harm.

If he had held it longer, or even swallowed that rusty-looking pill, would he have died on the spot with no chance of healing? Even with the regenerative abilities of a Homebound Cultivator within his own dwelling, recovery would surely be impossible.

In the spirit of seeking the truth, should I test it myself?

At that thought, Li Banfeng gave a cold laugh and muttered, "Am I crazy?"

Li Banfeng emptied the wallets of the man in the gold-rimmed glasses and Tiger Lamb. He wrapped the two pills in the torn clothing on the ground, placed them separately into the wallets, and then crouched down to study the bronze lotus flower.

He decided to call the round crimson bead and the round rust-colored bead the Red Pill and the Corrosion Pill for now.

The Red Pills most likely came from the corpses of the man in the gold-rimmed glasses and Tiger Lamb, while the Corrosion Pill probably came from the vanished folding fan.

Although Li Banfeng did not understand the principle behind the transformation, he knew that the bronze lotus flower had turned both the corpses and the fan into pills. As for the uses of these two kinds of pills, he had no idea for the time being.

What concerned Li Banfeng most was the principle by which the bronze lotus flower chose its pill-refining materials. Previously, it had refined the speckled-snake chrysanthemums, so why had it lost interest in refining these flowers now?

Li Banfeng couldn't help but wonder, Could it one day take an interest in me and turn me into a pill?

He carried the bag of speckled-snake chrysanthemums to the bronze lotus flower, but the lotus showed no reaction.

He then thought, So why did the speckled-snake chrysanthemums I picked earlier turn into Speckled-Snake Pills? What was different about last night when I returned?

While he was pondering, dizziness crept over him again. This was the usual reaction to inhaling the toxic fog. He would first feel dizzy, then the urge to vomit.

Vomit! The thought struck him like a sudden flash of light.

Li Banfeng suddenly remembered something. The first thing he had done after returning to the pocket dwelling was vomit.

Because he could not figure out a suitable place to do that, he puked directly into the pouch that had held the flowers. Before that, he had already dumped the speckled-snake chrysanthemums out of the bag.

Could it be that the bronze lotus flower can't sense the speckled-snake chrysanthemums when they are inside the bag?

At that thought, Li Banfeng opened the bag and dumped out the more than forty stalks of speckled-snake chrysanthemums that the man in the gold-rimmed glasses had collected, placing them near the bronze lotus flower.

The moment the flowers touched the ground, the petals of the bronze lotus flower began to quiver.

Screech~

A metallic screech rang out as the seven petals of the bronze lotus opened one by one.

Once again, Li Banfeng was struck by the ingenious mechanism.

By now, he truly could not tell whether modern civilization or this strange technology was more advanced.

When all seven petals had fully unfurled, Li Banfeng finally saw the seven-holed lotus core clearly.

A moment later, he sensed the air within the Pocket Dwelling shifting in an unnatural way. It spiraled inward, forming a vortex that drew everything toward the lotus core.

The forty or so chrysanthemums on the ground slowly lifted under its pull. Caught in the current, they drifted through the air, swirling up and down as if the entire room had become an otherworldly dance floor.

This must have been exactly what happened last night, but I slept too soundly to notice it!

By instinct, Li Banfeng took two steps back, afraid the vortex might drag him into the lotus core.

In total, forty-three stalks of speckled-snake chrysanthemums were drawn into the lotus, and then the petals closed once more.

So the items really did need to be within the range of the bronze lotus flower for it to sense and respond.

Wait… did I just say range of sight? That doesn't sound right. Can the bronze lotus flower even see?

As he pondered, a sharp clink reached his ears. He lifted the candle up and looked down. After searching for a long while, he realized that the Corrosion Pill had fallen on the ground.

I thought I just placed it into my wallet? Li Banfeng thought as he picked up the wallet and examined it. A hole had burned through the bottom, and the leather around it was charred with bluish-black scorch marks.

Corroded? Does the Corrosion Pill have corrosive properties too?

What would happen if someone swallowed it?

Even a Poison Cultivator would likely be unable to withstand it, let alone a Homebound Cultivator.

As these questions ran through his mind, Li Banfeng’s curiosity only deepened! He then wondered if the iron box could withstand the corrosion.

Using a piece of rag as padding, he carefully placed the Corrosion Pill into the iron tobacco tin.

The result was clear. It couldn't! The corrosion was even more severe than with the wallet.

The moment the tobacco tin touched the Corrosion Pill, it began to rust at an alarming rate. By the time Li Banfeng took the pill back out, the box was already eaten through with rust, its shape barely recognizable.

If the iron box can't withstand the corrosion, can a wooden box? Li Banfeng wondered again.

He placed the Corrosion Pill inside the spectacles case and watched closely. When he saw that the pill did not corrode the wood, he finally felt relieved and set the case down in a corner of the room.

He waited silently beside the bronze lotus flower. After about half an hour, the petals opened once more, revealing a new lotus seed within.

Li Banfeng cupped the seed in his hand. The moment it left the lotus core, it swelled at a frightening speed.

Bang!

With a muffled explosion, the seed split apart, releasing a pill mottled in black and white.

It was a Speckled-Snake Pill!

Li Banfeng counted carefully. There were ten pills in total.

Previously, thirty-seven stalks had been refined into eight pills. At that ratio, the bronze lotus flower used about four and a half stalks to produce one pill, far surpassing the methods of medicinal trading houses.

This time, fewer stalks had been consumed and the yield was higher, most likely because the speckled-snake chrysanthemums gathered by the man in the gold-rimmed glasses were larger in size.

Li Banfeng then dumped out all the speckled-snake chrysanthemums, including those collected by Chubby Qin, Nectarine, Leafy, Old Smoke Cannon, Tiger Lamb, the bald man, and his lackeys.

In total, three hundred and thirty-two stalks were drawn into the lotus core.

Once again, after more than half an hour, the petals opened. This time, three lotus seeds lay within the lotus core.

Li Banfeng plucked the seed and waited in silence for them to burst one by one.

A few minutes later, he gathered the shattered husks scattered across the floor and found himself holding a total of seventy-six Speckled-Snake Pills.

Plus the eighteen Speckled-Snake Pills that he had refined with his own flowers as well as the glasses guy's flowers, he had a total of ninety-four pills.

Money!

This was stacks upon stacks of money!

Li Banfeng had bought two cloth bags at the general store, but in the end he only used one to store the pills. The other was soiled with vomit.

After packing the pills into the clean bag, he went through the belongings left behind by Tiger Lamb and the man in the gold-rimmed glasses.

The man in the gold-rimmed glasses had two thousand six hundred and thirteen yuan in cash, while Tiger Lamb carried three thousand five hundred and twenty-eight and a half.

By now, Li Banfeng had no interest in such meager sums. He only kept the money as a token of respect for the dead. After all, they had crossed paths, and even became teammates. The least he could do was look after what they left behind!

After resting in the Pocket Dwelling for over two hours, Li Banfeng left Mount Bitterfog and made his way to the gathering point of the medicinal trading houses.

There, cultivators clutching bags of speckled-snake chrysanthemums were still locked in heated arguments with the members of the medicinal trading house. They shouted that they wouldn't sell, yet not a single one of them walked away.

Who else could they sell to if not the medicinal trading house? All they could do was hold out, hoping to squeeze out a slightly better price.

As one middle-aged cultivator grumbled, "Even thirty would be fine. Twenty-five is just daylight robbery."

Knowing that Chubby Qin and the others were still waiting in a secluded spot, Li Banfeng was totally not in the mood to watch them squabble.

Meanwhile, back in the secluded spot, Chubby Qin was filled with so much anxiety. Li Banfeng had been gone for almost three hours!

He paced back and forth, rubbing his hands together as he muttered, "I never should have let him go alone! With the whole mountain to search, how could he ever find that old woman? And he already spent the whole night up here. It might kill him!"

Leafy lowered her head and murmured, "If Elder Brother Bai Sha really traded for pills, I'd be content with just half."

Nectarine muttered, "That old woman… what sort of woman could she be? Could it be that he ran into Lucky Star?"

When they finally spotted Li Banfeng's figure, everyone's faces lit up with joy. However, Li Banfeng was not in the mood for thanks, nor did he have time for empty pleasantries. Without hesitation, he went straight to handing out the pills.

"Nectarine, you handed in thirty-eight stalks and I exchanged four pills for you."

Nectarine blinked in surprise. "Wait… I thought I was two stalks short of four pills?"

"I made up the difference for you," Li Banfeng said. "Think of it as rounding it off."

Hearing that, Nectarine looked at Li Banfeng with both gratitude and guilt.

Li Banfeng then took out six more pills. "Leafy, you had sixty-one stalks. That number's too far off, so I can't make up the difference. I can only round it down."
