Nevertheless, Leafy was overjoyed. She quickly uncorked the bottle and began checking the pills for authenticity.

Chubby Qin was the next one up, and his share turned out to be a bit trickier to calculate. He had originally collected only twenty stalks of speckled-snake chrysanthemums, which should have earned him two pills at most.

However, Li Banfeng had chosen to divide both Old Smoke Cannon's harvest and the flowers taken from the bald man's group with him. That alone pushed his total up to eighty-four stalks. When added to his own twenty, it came to one hundred and four.

Even then, Li Banfeng didn't stop there. Remembering how Chubby Qin had looked out for him and taken care of him throughout their journey, he added another eleven pills on top of the share.

Chubby Qin's hands trembled uncontrollably as he clutched the pills.

"One pill is worth ten thousand! Come on, let's sell them right now!" he blurted out.

Leafy quickly stopped him. "Big Bro Fatty, where do you plan on selling them?"

"To the medicinal trading houses, of course! Aren't they right there?" Chubby Qin replied without hesitation.

Leafy shook her head firmly. "We can't sell them to the medicinal trading houses. We can't explain where these pills came from."

Chubby Qin frowned. "What do you mean we can't explain it? Aren't they, well, you know…"

The truth was indeed hard to put into words, and even if they did explain it, no one would believe them.

Leafy said, "I used to be an apprentice at a trading house. They'll never accept pills without a verifiable source. If they start digging for answers, we will actually get into trouble."

She's right. Selling to the trading houses is out of the question. Li Banfeng stayed silent, quietly watching as the group worked to find a solution.

Chubby Qin's excitement faded, and his shoulders slumped. "So what now? Even with all these pills, we're just stuck holding onto them."

Nectarine let out a light chuckle. "Who said they'll go to waste? There are plenty of people out there who'd kill for pills like these. The richer they are, the more desperate they get. Even at ten thousand a pill, they still might not be able to buy one. If you sell to them, they will even think of how kind and good you are. A deal like this is something you can only dream of."

Li Banfeng's frown eased.

So there is a solution, but this solution doesn't really suit me.

What Nectarine meant was to sell them in private, directly to those who wanted the pills.

Li Banfeng, however, was unfamiliar with both the place and the people here. There was no way he could find suitable buyers on his own, so he turned to Chubby Qin, hoping he knew of buyers they could approach.

Chubby Qin scratched his head. "But this isn't something we can sell overnight."

Nectarine asked in surprise, "What's the rush? Pills won't spoil in a day or two!"

"Well, that's true, but still…"

Not wanting to argue further with Chubby Qin, Nectarine turned and cupped her fists to Li Banfeng. "Words can't express how grateful I feel. I owe you for these pills, and I owe you for my life as well.

"If you ever come to Pricklebamboo Village in the Outer Gully, ask around for You Xuetao and you will find me. If you ever need my help, just tell me. Even if it means walking through fire and blades, I will say yes without batting an eye!"

Her nickname, Nectarine, was very similar to her real name, You Xuetao[1].

She had given her address. If Chubby Qin couldn't find a way to sell the pills, they could always seek out Nectarine later.

Leafy bowed to Li Banfeng. "Brother Bai Sha, my name is Xue Yingying. If you ask for the Old Xue family in Threeshore Village of the Outer Gully, you'll find me."

Nectarine took out two pills and said, "We are unable to repay this debt now, so please accept these as a token of my gratitude."

Leafy also took out two pills. "I too wish to offer a token of thanks."

Li Banfeng waved his hand, refusing to take them.

The two women thanked him profusely, but if they had known Li Banfeng had received sixty-five pills, who knew what they would have thought?

Nectarine gazed at him. Indeed, she had her mind on him. A thought stirred in her heart. As a Hedonism Cultivator, her thoughts were often direct and unrestrained.

If I could do it with a man like him, he surely wouldn't turn out to be the lumpy kind. He'd be long and lasting.

Leafy's thoughts, however, were more complicated. She hadn't seen clearly what had happened earlier, but she knew the bald man's group had died at his and Chubby Qin's hands！

She knew that this was a man capable of great deeds. If he supported her, there was a real chance that she could open a medicinal trading house in the future!

The two women eventually took their leave. Meanwhile, Li Banfeng remained where he was, pondering about how he would return to Yuezhou Province.

Logically speaking, with so many pills in hand, he was no longer in need of money. Yet until they were sold, they were worth nothing. The question was how he could sell them quickly.

Li Banfeng glanced at Chubby Qin, who also seemed in urgent need of money.

After sitting quietly for a while, Chubby Qin's anxiousness gradually eased. He felt that Nectarine's words made sense.

"There's no rush to sell the pills. That woman was right. These are valuable goods. I can not only sell them for a good price, but also use them to make plenty of friends. So there's really no need to hurry!"

You're not in a rush to sell the pills anymore? That won't do! Li Banfeng thought.

He sat down beside Chubby Qin and urged, "But I am in a hurry! I am in urgent need of money!"

Although Li Banfeng had taken quite a bit of cash from the man in the gold-rimmed glasses, bringing these pills back to Yuezhou Province would be of little use.

Chubby Qin asked curiously, "What do you need money for so urgently? Want me to lend you some first?"

Li Banfeng waved his hand. "I don't want to borrow money. I just want to sell the pills as quickly as possible."

"You want to sell the pills as quickly as possible…" Chubby Qin thought it over and understood Li Banfeng's reasoning. Li Banfeng had come from the outer provinces, and here in Medicine King Gully he was a stranger. He hardly knew anyone, much less any wealthy or influential people.

Chubby Qin tapped his chin, thinking it over. "There is a place where you could sell your pills quickly, but…you'd have to let them go at a discount."

Li Banfeng's eyes lit up. "How much of a discount are we talking?"

1. In Chinese, (You Xuetao) sounds very close to (Youtao), which means "nectarine." Both names also share the character , meaning "peach." ☜

Chapter 37: Selling the Goods

After resting for a day at the inn, Li Banfeng and Chubby Qin hired a carriage and set off.

Li Banfeng thought Chubby Qin would take him somewhere special, but Chubby Qin brought him to Inner Gully instead.

While standing on the street, Chubby Qin reminded him, "Brother, that man is far too sly. Don't listen to his sob stories or excuses. Set a price in your mind, and don't sell for less than that."

Li Banfeng had already set a price. He expected at least five thousand per pill and would definitely not sell for less. The logic was simple. If something worth ten thousand could not even sell for half, he would rather swallow it himself. After all, he wasn't short on travel money.

He also recalled the Peddler's reminder that a cultivator must never miss a day of practice. If special circumstances kept him from going out or returning to his Pocket Dwelling, a Speckled-Snake Pill could still cover a day's worth of cultivation. If the sprinter Qiao Yuesheng had possessed enough of them, he likely wouldn't have met such a miserable end.

Having made up his mind, Li Banfeng asked, "Aren't you coming with me?"

Chubby Qin shook his head. "I'm not in a rush to sell. These pills are too valuable to let go cheaply. I was planning to bargain with you, but the last time I went to buy weapons from him, I spoke too rashly and ruined my relationship with Shopkeeper Feng. I won't cause trouble for you this time. Once you're finished, I'll wait at the bun shop on the street corner."

Chubby Qin spoke as if it were some great secret, yet the place he chose turned out to be none other than Feng's General Store.

That said, without Chubby Qin's guidance, Li Banfeng never would have guessed that a general store would actually take in pills.

When he entered the store, Shopkeeper Feng greeted him with a bright smile. "Trader Li, you're back. How was the harvest this time?"

Li Banfeng smiled in return. "Not bad. I've come to sell the harvest."

Shopkeeper Feng blinked in surprise. "Trader Li, we don't take speckled-snake chrysanthemums. I don't have any pill recipes."

"It's not speckled-snake chrysanthemums. Let's talk somewhere private."

Shopkeeper Feng led Li Banfeng into the back room, where Li Banfeng first took out one thousand three hundred yuan. "I'll clear the debt first! Five hundred for the sickle and eight hundred for the trowel!"

Shopkeeper Feng accepted the money with a smile. "Trader Li, how generous of you! So then, what goods have you brought me?"

Li Banfeng took out a Speckled-Snake Pill and handed it to Shopkeeper Feng.

Shopkeeper Feng put on his reading glasses and studied the pill for a long while before finally nodding. "Good quality. Better than what you'd get from the medicinal trading houses."

Li Banfeng took a sip of tea and smiled. "Shopkeeper Feng, you truly know your stuff."

Shopkeeper Feng also smiled, then handed the pill back to Li Banfeng.

Li Banfeng was taken aback. "What do you mean? You won't take it?"

Shopkeeper Feng shook his head. "It's not that I won't take it. I just need to make the rules clear first. This isn't a medicinal trading house, but a general store. I can do the deal, but it's not my trade. If you sold it to a trading house, you could get twelve thousand. I can't offer you that price here.

"I can't refine pills myself, and when it comes to buying and selling, I have to leave room for profit. If I keep them in stock, who knows when they'll sell? So the price will have to be lower, and you might not be happy with it."

Li Banfeng nodded. Complaints and excuses were only natural, and he had already prepared himself for them. Still, no matter what was said, he would never sell for less than five thousand.

"Name your price," Li Banfeng said.

Shopkeeper Feng raised five fingers. Li Banfeng first thought he meant exactly five thousand, but then heard him say, "Fifty percent! At most, I can offer you half their value, which is at six thousand yuan per pill."

Shopkeeper Feng was being honest. This was the rock-bottom price and the highest price he could offer. Since the pills were of unknown origin, he would be taking on great risk. Even so, there was another reason he had not pushed the price lower. He had a request for Li Banfeng.

Li Banfeng nodded. Six thousand per pill was a price he found satisfying, yet he kept a straight face. "Six thousand is a bit low, but it depends on how many you're willing to buy."

Shopkeeper Feng stroked his mustache. "That depends on how many Trader Li wants to sell."

"Sixty-five pills. Can you handle that much?"

Shopkeeper Feng nodded. "I can take them all."

"Deal! Shopkeeper Feng, you're a straightforward guy!" Li Banfeng pulled out a bag and poured out the pills.

Shopkeeper Feng counted the pills, nodded, and said, "Trader Li, you're just as straightforward. With a deal this large, most people would ask to run a tab and settle later, but I'll give you the cash now.

Shopkeeper Feng beckoned to his clerk. The store only had one, a young man named Sheng. After receiving a few whispered instructions, Sheng slipped behind the shelves, passed through a small door, and soon returned carrying a leather suitcase.

Shopkeeper Feng set it down and opened it right in front of Li Banfeng. Inside were thirty-nine neat bundles of banknotes, each worth ten thousand. For a shop that looked modest on the surface, its resources clearly ran deep.

"Three hundred ninety thousand. Please, check it."

Li Banfeng chuckled. "Would I ever doubt you, Shopkeeper Feng?"

Even so, he carefully counted the money three times before closing the suitcase and putting it away.

Shopkeeper Feng accepted the pills with a smile. "Trader Li, thank you for doing business with me. The deal is complete, but let's be clear about the rules. I won't ask where your pills came from, and in return, you must not ask whom I sell them to or for how much."

"Don't worry! I won't break your rules."

Li Banfeng was in no mood to ask about these things. All he cared about was taking the money and getting out. He would head straight for the train station, buy a ticket, and be on his way back to Yuezhou as quickly as possible.

First, he needed to gauge the situation and see whether the people from the Dark Star Bureau would let him go. If he learned it was nothing serious, he would head straight to the hospital to find out what was truly up with He Jiaqing.

Just as Li Banfeng was about to leave with the case of money, Shopkeeper Feng called out, "Trader Li, wait a moment. I have been holding onto the item you wanted all this time."

With that, Shopkeeper Feng pulled down the curtain, and the gramophone appeared once more before Li Banfeng. It sat in an antique rosewood cabinet, fitted with three brass horns, one large and two small. The elegant design caught his eyes at once and made it impossible to look away.

"Trader Li, do you still want this gramophone?" Shopkeeper Feng observed him carefully, planning his next tactic in conversation.

Li Banfeng forced himself to look away from the gramophone. "I do want it, but I'll be traveling far. Carrying it along would be inconvenient."

Shopkeeper Feng looked confused. "You don't have to take it with you. Just leave it at home."

Li Banfeng lifted his tea bowl and took a slow sip. "But wouldn't it be a shame for such a fine piece to sit at home with no one to care for it?"

At those words, the clerk began signaling frantically to Shopkeeper Feng.

Shopkeeper Feng sighed softly. "Trader Li, you're a straightforward guy. Since you've taken a liking to this gramophone, I was planning to give you a discount and sell it to you for one hundred eighty thousand."

Li Banfeng drew in a deep breath. "Wait. Just wait a little longer."

Shopkeeper Feng picked up a record, set it on the gramophone, poured two fresh cups of water, and lit the wick beneath the machine.

Pop… crackle… pop…

The small horn on the right let out rhythmic bursts of sound. As the record spun, faint static spread through the air.

「Butterflies have flown~

Hearts are left alone…

In this cold and endless night~ Who will wipe the tears on my cheeks? 」

It's this same song again. Wild Grass and Blossoms Bloom Again in Spring.[1]

Li Banfeng was finding it hard to control himself.

Shopkeeper Feng sighed softly. "Yesterday, a customer took a liking to this gramophone. I told him it was already reserved for another guest, so I didn't sell it.

"Trader Li, if you delay any longer, I may not be able to hold it for you. I was waiting for your return so you could enjoy this tune, but the next time you come back, I'm afraid you won't hear it again, no matter how much you long for it."

Come back? Will I ever come back? Li Banfeng thought. Once he returned to Yuezhou, he planned to take any job he could find. He would settle into his Pocket Dwelling, free from mortgage and commuting pressure, and live a simple, happy life.

I don't think I will ever come back to Puluo Province…

With that thought, he suddenly rose to his feet, grabbed the money case, and walked out of the general store.

The sorrowful melody lingered in the shop. The clerk stamped his foot and muttered to Shopkeeper Feng, "Boss, I think he really wanted to buy it. We set the price too high."

Shopkeeper Feng let out several long sighs. "There's nothing I can do. If we try to sell it too cheaply, this thing just won't leave."

The clerk wore a bitter look. "What should we do, then? When will this thing ever be sold?"

Helplessness filled Shopkeeper Feng, and the little shop echoed with his sighs.

When the song ended and the needle clicked to a stop, Shopkeeper Feng extinguished the flame, released the steam, and was just about to cover the gramophone when Li Banfeng suddenly walked back in.

"One hundred sixty thousand. Do you sell or not?"

The clerk glanced at Shopkeeper Feng with eager anticipation.

Shopkeeper Feng paused in thought, then answered calmly, "Sold."

A few beads of sweat formed on his forehead, but he wiped them away with practiced ease, revealing nothing before Li Banfeng.

Li Banfeng immediately counted out one hundred sixty thousand and slapped it onto the table.

Shopkeeper Feng didn't even bother counting the money. He accepted it and told his clerk, "Sheng, prepare a cart and deliver the goods to Trader Li."

The clerk was about to prepare the cart when Li Banfeng suddenly said, "Wait a moment!"

The clerk was stunned and turned to look at Shopkeeper Feng. However, Shopkeeper Feng remained composed. "Trader Li, the deal is already done. It wouldn't be right to haggle further."

Li Banfeng shook his head. "I'm not bargaining. I want to buy something else."

He wanted to buy a bed. Since he planned to live long-term in his Pocket Dwelling, sleeping on the ground was out of the question.

Online shopping was cheaper and offered more styles, and he had originally intended to wait until he returned to Yuezhou. But after considering what the trip back would be like, he decided it would be better to buy one directly from the general store

He no longer wanted to sleep on the train. He didn't want to face the three-headed monster at the Three-Headed Fork again, nor did he want to struggle against the unbearable temptations at Girdle-Slipping Hollow.

He could simply live in his Pocket Dwelling. As long as he kept the key well hidden, he could avoid all the dangers of the train. With a bed, along with food and drink, even a breakdown would not disturb Li Banfeng's peaceful life.

"A bed…" Shopkeeper Feng was about to say there were none for sale. However, it just so happened that there was a four-poster wooden bed standing by the shelves in the back room.

The so-called four-poster meant a bed with four upright posts, a frame at the top, and railing in the middle. It measured at 1.5 meters wide and at 1.2 meters high. The structure resembled a small house, and lying on the bed gave an added sense of security.

Li Banfeng had already taken a liking to the bed. "Shopkeeper Feng, give me a price."

Yet Shopkeeper Feng didn't look at the bed. His eyes stayed locked on the gramophone. For a moment, it seemed to tremble ever so slightly, though perhaps it was only an illusion.

Oh no… this thing might not want to leave.

Shopkeeper Feng clenched his jaw, then spoke with forced cheer. "Trader Li, I admire your straightforwardness, and you've given me such good business today. Take this bed as my gift to you!"

Shopkeeper Feng didn't want to waste even another moment.

Li Banfeng quickly waved his hands. "How could I possibly accept that…"

"Trader Li, if you refuse, you'll be looking down on me!"

"That's not right. I can't just take such a fine bed for free. Name a price, and I'll also look at some bedding."

"Bedding is no problem. Sheng, bring out two sets of satin bedding for Trader Li."

Li Banfeng was completely baffled. "No, wait, Shopkeeper Feng. I came here to buy, not to take freebies. I was even planning to buy a couple of outfits…"

"Sheng, fetch the measuring tape and take Trader Li's measurements!"

Sheng stepped forward and silently measured Li Banfeng from head to toe. Soon after, he returned with a pure white suit.

"Trader Li, I've decided you're my friend. The suit is my gift to you as well."

"No, I don't usually wear white. It's too hard to take care of…"

"Then bring out the black one!"

"Black gets dirty easily too…"

"Then fetch the beige checkered shirt!"

Li Banfeng was left utterly bewildered by Shopkeeper Feng. "Forget it. I won't buy clothes. I'll just take two candles."

"Sheng, bring a bundle of candles and send Trader Li off with the candleholder from Ingris."

"No, wait—forget it, I won't buy anything else. I'll just take two records."

Shopkeeper Feng went to the shelves, gathered a dozen records in his arms, and laid them on the counter. "Take these, and I will also include two barrels of fuel, one barrel of engine oil, a kettle, two cups, a bucket, and a bundle of candle wicks. I also have a tool set with pliers and wrenches. If the gramophone ever has a small issue, you can repair it yourself."

"No, I… I mean…"

Before Li Banfeng could finish, Shopkeeper Feng placed a feather duster in his hands. "The gramophone cannot stand dust. Clean it regularly. Trader Li, I am straightforward by nature. If you do not accept these, I will get mad!"

Li Banfeng said nothing as there was nothing more he could say.

The clerk hired a carriage and began loading it piece by piece: first the bed, then the gramophone, followed by the bedding, clothes, fuel, candles, and bucket. Everything was piled onto the cart.

With a feather duster in hand, Li Banfeng stood in the doorway, appearing very much dazed. The clerk helped him onto the cart while Shopkeeper Feng called out, "Trader Li, safe travels! I hope we can do business again next time!"

With that, Sheng urged the driver, and the carriage rolled away.

Shopkeeper Feng and his clerk stood silently outside the shop, watching the carriage until it vanished from sight.

"Boss!" Chun Sheng's voice trembled slightly.

"Sheng!" Shopkeeper Feng's throat tightened with emotion.

"Boss!" Chun Sheng's eyes turned red.

"Sheng!" Shopkeeper Feng wiped the tears from the corners of his eyes.

"Boss, did we really sell it?" Chun Sheng asked, still feeling dubious.

Shopkeeper Feng, still being cautious, said, "Sheng, check the back room. I feel like we let it go too cheaply. Check carefully and make sure that thing hasn't returned."

Chun Sheng ran into the back and returned within the blink of an eye. "It didn't come back. It's really gone this time."

"Sheng!" Shopkeeper Feng was wailing out loud.

"Boss!" Chun Sheng exclaimed as tears streamed down his face.

"Sheng!"

"Boss!"

The two of them sat on the ground, wailing loudly.

At last, the clerk wiped his face and said, "Finally, we can do business in peace."

Shopkeeper Feng jumped to his feet, straightened his clothes, and declared, "To hell with business! Today we close the shop, head to the Descending Phoenix Hall, order a table of fine wine, call for two girls to keep us company, and enjoy ourselves for a few days first!"

1. Mentioned in Chapter 27 ☜

Chapter 38: A Song Before Sleep

Li Banfeng had the driver take the cart out to the wilderness outside of the Outer Gully and unload the goods.

Since Chun Sheng had already paid in full, the driver didn't ask any questions. He set the load down by the edge of the woods and drove off.

After making sure no one was around, Li Banfeng strained his muscles and hefted the four-poster bed onto his shoulder.

Wanderlust Cultivators were impressively strong. Even while carrying such a massive wooden bed, Li Banfeng managed to free up a hand to pull out his key.

The bed was made of solid wood, and it was extremely heavy.

That shop assistant, Chun Sheng, managed to lift the bed onto the cart all by himself earlier. Seems like he's no ordinary guy.

Li Banfeng brought the bed into the Pocket Dwelling. The space inside the Pocket Dwelling was quite small, only six or seven square meters. Now, with a bed that was one and a half meters wide, it felt a bit cramped inside.

He then carried the gramophone inside, wondering where he should put it.

Should I set it across from the bed so I can lie down and listen to music?

It will be nice if I can enjoy the music while watching steam puff out of the horn.

But once a record finished, he would have to get up to change it. And if the two cups of water boiled dry, he would need to get up to refill the horn.

If he had to keep getting out of bed while listening to music, it would spoil the experience.

So Li Banfeng placed the gramophone beside the bed instead. That way, he could change records, add water, put out the fire, or wind it up with just a stretch of his hand, without having to get up from the bed.

This is a good idea. I'll just keep it here.

Then he put on the bedding for the bed and dusted the place with a feather duster. He folded the clothes Shopkeeper Feng had given him, placing them neatly by his backpack for now.

There were still many things he needed to buy: a wardrobe, a storage cabinet, a table, and a chair to go with it...

His eyelids grew heavy as drowsiness settled over him.

After descending the mountain, he and Chubby Qin had rested at the inn for a whole day before returning to the Inner Gully. Now it was only just past dusk. He shouldn't be feeling this tired.

Could it be that the toxins from the fog are still lingering in my body?

That shouldn't be the case! Aren't Homebound Cultivators supposed to recover the fastest?

Nevertheless, he still had to go meet up with Chubby Qin, no matter how tired he felt. After all, he had been in Puluo for quite a while now, and if there was anyone he could truly call a friend, it was Chubby Qin.

When Li Banfeng reached the bun shop at the street corner, Chubby Qin was still devouring food. Several stacks of bamboo steamers were piled high on the table beside him.

"What took you so long?" Chubby Qin mumbled, his mouth filled with buns.

"I was negotiating prices with Shopkeeper Feng," Li Banfeng replied as he pulled out a chair and sat down. He asked the bun shopkeeper to bring out two more baskets of buns.

The bun shop wasn't big, but its buns were exquisite. Stuffed with beef, each bite released savory broth that filled the mouth with flavor. Paired with a bit of crushed garlic, it was authentic fare, simple and pure.

Li Banfeng ordered an earthen pot of wine. He had no idea which of the bun shop's wines were good; he hadn't even heard of the names on the menu. So he simply told the shop assistant to bring out their best.

Chubby Qin winced inwardly. He hadn't sold any pills yet, so even wine from a humble bun shop felt like a luxury to him.

"Brother Qin, don't worry. This meal is on me." Li Banfeng poured a cup, took a sip, and let the strong liquor linger in his mouth for a long moment before swallowing.

Seeing Li Banfeng's generosity, Chubby Qin asked quietly, "What price did you sell it for?"

Li Banfeng gestured with his hand. "Six thousand."

There was no need for him to hide it from Chubby Qin. After all, if Chubby Qin decided he wanted to sell the pills to Shopkeeper Feng, it would likely be at the same price.

Chubby Qin shook his head. "Brother Li, I hate to say it, but you sold them too cheaply. This isn't some ordinary good. You really don't understand how things work here."

"What do you mean?"

It was late, and no one else was in the shop. Chubby Qin lowered his voice to a whisper. "I ran into an acquaintance here earlier and probed about the market price, and he actually told me. The wealthy families in Puluo buy hundreds, even thousands, of pills every year. At the year's end, they distribute them as rewards to members of the younger generation who have made contributions to their family.

"In those large households, everyone fights tooth and nail for more pills to raise their cultivation level. And what if they don't have contributions? They have no choice but to buy them at inflated prices from the market.

“The market prices aren't the same as the ones set by trading houses. If the trading houses sell for ten thousand, then the market price would be at least twelve thousand. Sometimes, it's eighteen or even twenty thousand. You sold for six thousand. That's a huge loss."

Li Banfeng shook his head. "As long as the pills can be sold, it's money. If I can't sell them, what's the point of keeping them with me? I don't need those extra days of cultivation, and I don't know any rich young masters."

Chubby Qin sipped his wine. "How about this? After we rest for a couple of days, I'll take you around Medicine King Gully. We can't rub shoulders with the truly wealthy, but I know quite a few sons of smaller rich families."

Li Banfeng bit into a bun and chuckled. "I appreciate the thought, but I'm heading back to Yuezhou tomorrow."

"Yuezhou?" Chubby Qin had never heard of the place.

"Yuezhou is a place in the Outer Provinces. That's where I'm from."

"You're going back? Already?"

Chubby Qin looked disappointed at first, but after finishing two more buns, he seemed to come to terms with it.

"Fair enough. You're from the Outer Provinces. You've seen the wider world, so of course you wouldn't think much of a place like Puluo. One day, I'll go see the Outer Provinces myself. If I never visit such a great place, I'll feel like I've wasted my whole life."

Such a great place?

Was Yuezhou really a great place?

Li Banfeng did feel that Yuezhou was a great place, but if he were pressed to explain why, he wouldn't quite be able to put it into words.

They kept eating until the bun shop closed for the night. Then it was time for the two young men to part ways.

Chubby Qin said, "You know where my home is. If you ever come back to Puluo, you must come find me."

As the alcohol took effect and fatigue weighed down his body, Li Banfeng lay on his bed, ready to drift into sleep.

However, lying there, he felt as though there was something he hadn't done yet.

Did I not walk ten kilometers today?

No, I did.

Then what haven't I done?

Li Banfeng propped himself up halfway, took out the gramophone crank, and fitted it into the gramophone. Then he wound it to the fullest.

So that was it. I was missing my pre-sleep music.

He picked out a record. It was wrapped in stiff paper, with a colorful painted cover. The cover featured a woman in a long dress, dancing gracefully against a backdrop of red and yellow flowers.

Li Banfeng slid the record out, placed it on the turntable, and set it to play. Amid the faint crackle of static, the gramophone played a beautiful and elegant song.

「The southern wind brings a cool breeze!

The nightingale trills its gentle song!

Beneath the moon, flowers drift into dreams,

Yet only the night-blooming jessamine releases its fragrance! 」

It's "Fragrance of the Night"!

Li Banfeng had heard this song before, but he found it especially pleasant tonight.

Li Banfeng fell asleep before the song even finished.

It was for that very reason that he had chosen to use the wind-up crank instead of the steam engine. He had been worried that he might fall asleep at any moment.

Using the crank meant there was no need to worry about extinguishing the candle flame, so Li Banfeng let himself relax freely and sink into dreams.

「I sing for you, I long for you, night-blooming jessamine~ 」

The song was over.

The needle had reached its end.

The spring unwound fully, and the gramophone stopped running.

In the pitch-black Pocket Dwelling, the only sound was Li Banfeng's deep, steady breathing.

Splash~

There was the sound of water sloshing in the gramophone, as if someone were pouring water into the small horn on the left.

Crackle~

A match was struck, igniting the wick stuck in the container of fat.

Pop… crackle… pop…

The small horn on the right sprayed out puffs of steam, and the turntable of the gramophone began to spin slowly.

The stylus was set back to the starting point. The gramophone came to life with the crackle of static and played another song.

「 From the ends of the earth, the farthest seas~

I search and search, for one who knows me~

I sing while my beloved plays the guqin,

My love, our hearts are one~! 」

The beautiful vocals seemed to linger endlessly within the Pocket Dwelling.

Screech~

The petals of the Arcane Crimson Lotus slowly spread open, and a small whirlwind rose from within the lotus core.

The whirlwind swept past the gramophone, causing the song to falter slightly out of tune.

Around ten seconds later, the song gradually returned to normal.

The Arcane Crimson Lotus' petals trembled a few times before closing back up.

The gramophone kept playing its sweet tune.

「In this life, who doesn't cherish their youth?

I'm like a thread, and my beloved is like a needle,

My love, once we're joined, we can never be apart~! 」

Bathed in song, Li Banfeng slept soundly with a blissful smile on his face.

Chapter 39: Raising a Gale on Level Ground

Li Banfeng dreamed sweet dreams all night. Throughout all of them, there was always a goose feather teasing his ear.

When he stepped out of the Pocket Dwelling, Li Banfeng realized he had emerged in the afternoon again instead of during the morning. It dawned on him that he needed some way to tell the time from inside the Pocket Dwelling.

Nevertheless, once he returned to Yuezhou, problems like this would be insignificant, easily resolved just by having a cell phone.

I woke up late. I'm not sure if I'll be able to get a ticket.

Li Banfeng strode out of the Pocket Dwelling and walked a few hundred meters down the street. Then he suddenly felt a strange sensation run through his body.

He couldn't quite describe what it was.

Li Banfeng scanned the street for a vehicle. It could be a horse-drawn carriage or a rickshaw; he just needed a vehicle to transport him to the train station.

As he swept his gaze about, he noticed the other pedestrians occasionally glancing his way.

Why are they looking at me?

I'm wearing a suit and a fedora. I'm dressed pretty darn well today!

But well, they mean no harm. They're just curious.

Strange… How do I know they mean no harm?

Why am I having so many strange feelings today?

Indeed, the pedestrians bore Li Banfeng no ill will. However, the thing that caught their eyes wasn't his attire but his extraordinary speed.

Li Banfeng wasn't running; he was just walking. He had his hands in his pockets as he ambled casually down the street.

Yet, his feet took him on an unusual route that ordinary people found incomprehensible, propelling him forward at great speed. By the time he noticed something was off, he had already reached the end of the street.

I didn't mean to walk this fast…

Noticing how the houses on both sides of the streets flashed past his vision in a blur, Li Banfeng made a huge conscious effort to slow down his steps.

He truly hadn't been exerting any effort, nor did he feel the least bit tired.

It was as if his legs had motors installed. If he did not step on the brakes, his legs would simply keep going.

However, even when he stepped on the brakes, it was hard to control. Li Banfeng could stand still without issue, but the moment he took a step forward, it was as though he was moving at a speed of twenty miles an hour.

This isn't good. Why is it that I can't even walk normally anymore?

Did the toxic fog on Mount Bitterfog cause this?

Is this a temporary side effect or permanent damage?

Even as he did his best to restrain himself, Li Banfeng ultimately reached the edge of the Inner Gully in no time at all.

He had intended to find a quiet place and spend a bit of time focusing on controlling his steps. However, as he passed under a large banyan tree, he noticed there was a rickshaw parked beneath it.

A new plan emerged in his mind. He could take the rickshaw to the train station, buy a ticket, and board the train. Once he was inside, he could go into the Pocket Dwelling to practice controlling his legs.

As Li Banfeng considered that, his legs kept moving, carrying him several dozen meters forward. He went so fast that he had to backtrack, finally stopping in front of the rickshaw driver with much difficulty.

Li Banfeng asked, "How much to the train station?"

The rickshaw driver took a puff of his pipe, looked up at Li Banfeng, and grinned. "Do you even need a ride to get to the train station?"

Li Banfeng stared at the rickshaw driver for a moment.

He recognized the rickshaw driver. Monkey Qiu had hired him; he was the rickshaw driver with fire wheels.

He had taken Li Banfeng from the train station to where the Peddler had set up shop. The trip had cost Monkey Qiu a large sum of five hundred yuan.

The rickshaw driver's comment about Li Banfeng was right. The train station wasn't far from the Inner Gully, and at Li Banfeng's current speed, he could get there in the blink of an eye.

However, Li Banfeng didn't know the way.

The rickshaw driver stood up and pointed to the dirt road up ahead. "Follow this road north for 1.75 kilometers, then continue eastward for 1 kilometer. After that, head north again. Less than five hundred meters later, you should see an intersection. Keep walking for another kilometer, and you'll arrive at the train station."

Li Banfeng shook his head. "That's too troublesome. I'll just take the rickshaw there."

With the pipe still in his mouth, the driver said, "What's troublesome about that? You've already become a level-one Wanderlust Cultivator. How could you not find the way?"

Of course. That's what it is, Li Banfeng realized.

His bizarrely fast speed hadn't been a side effect caused by Mount Bitterfog's toxic fog. It was the result of becoming a level-one Wanderlust Cultivator.

As for why he had advanced a level in his Wanderlust Cultivation… well, that was because Li Banfeng had spent a whole day in a region of peril.

Back on Mount Bitterfog, the toxic fog had left him in such a confused daze that he had forgotten the Peddler's warning.

If he were to spend an entire day in a region of peril, he would advance a level in his Wanderlust Cultivation.

Mount Bitterfog was a region of peril, so that meant Li Banfeng had passed the Wanderlust Cultivator's trial and become a level-one Wanderlust Cultivator.

That was, of course, a good thing.

However, Li Banfeng couldn't advance too quickly. Otherwise, he would get backlash as a Homebound Cultivator.

Nevertheless, there was nothing he could do about that. The Pocket Dwelling couldn't produce a dwelling spirit, and without it, he wouldn't be able to advance his Homebound Cultivation. In the meantime, it was inevitable that his Homebound Cultivation would be suppressed by his Wanderlust Cultivation. The only thing Li Banfeng could do was try to narrow the gap between the two.

"Why didn't I sense it yesterday?" Li Banfeng muttered to himself.

The rickshaw driver asked, "When did you cross the threshold for the first level?"

"The day before yesterday, on Mount Bitterfog," Li Banfeng answered honestly.

The rickshaw driver chuckled. "You crossed the threshold the day before yesterday and advanced to the first level today. That's quick. Most people have to wait three to five days. Brother, you've got good fortune. Better hurry on to the train station."

Li Banfeng said to him, "I don't feel like walking. Since you've got your rickshaw here, why not take the business?"

The rickshaw driver froze for a moment. "You really want to ride my rickshaw? Do you know how much it costs? Others charge eight yuan, but I'll be taking two hundred from you."

"Two hundred? I'll pay it!" Li Banfeng pulled out two hundred yuan in notes and handed them over to the rickshaw driver.

He felt a certain kinship with this rickshaw driver, and he was certain that paying the two hundred yuan was the right choice.

The rickshaw driver rubbed the paper between his thumb and finger, then he grinned. "All right, but let me warn you. My rickshaw will get you there in the blink of an eye."

Li Banfeng made what seemed like a very unreasonable request. "Could you go a little slower?"

The rickshaw driver understood what he meant. "I can slow down, sure. But the slower I go, the higher the price. Five hundred for two marks on the dial. How's that?"

He was referring to the hour markings on a clock, which meant he would slow down his speed to make the trip last for around ten minutes.

Li Banfeng nodded. "Sounds good."

The rickshaw driver sighed. "With the meat delivered straight to my mouth, how could I not eat it? Get in."

Li Banfeng climbed into the rickshaw, and the driver began pulling it northward at a casual pace.

Li Banfeng asked, "What level of Wanderlust Cultivation are you?"

He could tell the rickshaw driver was a Wanderlust Cultivator. The first time he had been the rickshaw driver's passenger, the rickshaw driver had already revealed his abilities, which were unique to Wanderlust Cultivators. However, back then, Li Banfeng hadn't known what a Wanderlust Cultivator was.

The rickshaw driver didn't answer. "You think five hundred is enough to buy information about my cultivation? Two marks will pass quickly. Best ask me something else."

"Do Wanderlust Cultivators always walk so fast?"

"Do I look like I'm walking fast to you?" the rickshaw driver replied. He strode forward step by step at a pace that was no different from that of an ordinary man.

"Then why was I walking so fast?"

The rickshaw driver explained, "The walking method you were using earlier is called Raising a Gale on Level Ground. It's the ability of a level-one Wanderlust Cultivator. When a Wanderlust Cultivator first advances to level one, they can't control the force in their steps. It takes days of practice before you can exert and restrain that force at will."

"How should I practice?"

The rickshaw driver pointed at a hand pump by the roadside. "Ever used one of those? Before you advanced to level one, walking was like pumping water. Each pump with your arm would bring up a gush of water. Now, you have to hold that force back, let the stream that comes out be narrower than a hemp cord. Only then can you walk like an ordinary person."

Narrower than a hemp cord? Li Banfeng thought, considering it. Then he said, "That sounds hard to control."

"There's a trick to it. First, rein in the force at your hips, moving forward slowly step by step. Then rein it in at your knees, pushing forward along with the hips. Next, your ankles, then your toes. Restrain that force one step at a time, in a downward cycle through your body. If you've got a good level of perception, you can get the hang of it in a day."

Li Banfeng put on a bitter smile. "So after advancing, I have to learn how to walk all over again?"

"Don't treat it as a hassle. At first, you'll stumble plenty. But once you master this method of walking, it will be no problem for you to cover a distance of thirty to thirty-five kilometers in an hour."

At that speed, Li Banfeng would be able to complete his daily foundational cultivation requirement of ten kilometers in less than half an hour.

However, there was a problem…

"For a level-one Wanderlust Cultivator, is it enough to walk ten kilometers a day?"

"It's not enough!" The rickshaw driver shook his head firmly. "If you're walking in ordinary places, you need at least twenty-five kilometers a day."

Twenty-five kilometers?

That's not too big of a problem.

"What do you mean by ordinary places?" Li Banfeng asked.

"Major streets and small lanes. Those are ordinary places."

"And what places aren't counted as ordinary?"

"Go climb mountains. The steeper, the better. Bodies of water work too. The deeper, the better. At Orchid-Green Village, there's a swamp of muck. If you can walk two li through it, your daily cultivation is considered complete. At Sheep Fork Mountain, there's a ridge called Ghostblade Ridge. Walk just half a kilometer there, and you'll complete a day's worth of cultivation and even accumulate enough for an extra day. The essence of Wanderlust Cultivation lies in danger. The more perilous the terrain, the faster you'll advance in your cultivation."

Li Banfeng said eagerly, "I would advance very quickly in my cultivation on Mount Bitterfog too, wouldn't I?"

The rickshaw driver shook his head. "I advise you not to go there. You have no idea how many people have lost their lives on that mountain—climbing up and down, and getting scattered in the toxic fog. The fact that you managed to get down alive means you're blessed, truly blessed. Moreover, you were able to endure the rays of Heavenly Light. Since you're blessed, you should cherish your life. Why throw your life away for a bit of money? It's not worth it."

"I'd be able to make progress with my cultivation and earn money at the same time if I climb Mount Bitterfog. After all, it isn't easy to earn money," Li Banfeng said. It was a statement that he knew all too well was true.

The rickshaw driver shook his head with a laugh. "There are plenty of easier ways to make money. Pulling rickshaws brings in pretty good money. Last time, when you were urgently in need of a ride to the Peddler's, I earned five hundred from that one trip. Today, I've earned another five hundred from you.

"I heard the major medicinal trading houses bought speckled-snake chrysanthemums at only twenty-five coins a stalk this year. You tell me, is it easier to earn money doing that or being a rickshaw driver?"

By his reasoning, it was indeed easier to earn money as a rickshaw driver.

The rickshaw driver laughed. "You people look down on this line of work, thinking pulling a rickshaw is disgraceful. But I look down on you lot who gamble with your lives. Seems to me you treat your lives as disposable."

Li Banfeng stopped arguing with the rickshaw driver about the two lines of work. Instead, he asked, "How do I find the right places to cultivate? Places with some peril but not too much?"

The rickshaw driver gave a brief reply. "Know your own capabilities and only go to regions of peril that are within your means, and keep a respectable distance from perilous people. Mount Bitterfog is a region of peril, and it's crawling with perilous people. That's exactly the sort of place you shouldn't go!"

"How do I distinguish a region of peril and a perilous person from ordinary ones?"

"Wanderlust Cultivators can sense danger. It's called Seeking Luck and Avoiding Calamity. That's also an ability unique to level-one Wanderlust Cultivators. It lets you discern not just regions of peril but perilous people as well."

Li Banfeng's eyes lit up. "So, at the first level of Wanderlust Cultivation, there are two abilities?"

Chapter 40: Seeking Fortune and Avoiding Calamity

Li Banfeng truly hadn't expected that he would have two abilities as a level-one Wanderlust Cultivator.

He uttered in surprise, "Seeking Fortune and Avoiding Calamity. How do I use this ability?"

The rickshaw driver said nothing and simply pulled the cart with his head down.

Just as Li Banfeng was about to ask again, a chill swept over him, making him shiver.

Somewhere deep in his subconscious, Li Banfeng felt an inexplicable fear.

He sensed that if he asked one more question, he would die at the hands of the rickshaw driver.

The rickshaw driver turned to face him and asked, "Are you afraid?"

Li Banfeng nodded. "I am."

The rickshaw driver chuckled. "I just directed killing intent toward you. Don't worry, I'm not actually going to kill you. I was just helping you test the ability. Seeking Fortune and Avoiding Calamity is the simplest ability to use. You came to ride in my rickshaw today. That is seeking fortune. When your heart pounds, your hands tremble, chills run through you, or you break out in goosebumps, those may all be signs of peril. If that happens, you must avoid the path ahead. That is called avoiding calamity.

"The ability is very easy to use, but there are always people who fail to grasp how it works, especially the careless ones who never pay attention to details like that. A Wanderlust Cultivator traverses through thousands of mountains and tens of thousands of bodies of water. You must be meticulous. You have to feel the weight of your life with every step you take, consider the pros and cons with every decision."

Li Banfeng was delighted by this ability. "So, as long as danger arises, a Wanderlust Cultivator will always be able to sense it?"

The rickshaw driver shook his head. "Nothing in life is that easy. It depends on your cultivation level, your enchanted artifacts, and even the Daoist path your opponent follows. If your opponent's cultivation is two levels above yours, they can hide their malice. If they intend to harm you, you won't sense it. You won't sense it either if they possess an enchanted artifact or ability that conceals malicious thoughts.

"There are also hidden regions that can conceal peril. If your cultivation level isn't high enough, you won't sense those either. And if you encounter a Homebound Cultivator of the same level, you won't be able to see through any of their thoughts, nor will you sense any of their malice."

Li Banfeng knew that Homebound Cultivators could avoid being noticed, but he hadn't expected that they would be able to conceal their own malice as well.

Indeed, it's impossible to guess what a shut-in is thinking.

I've got two abilities now! Raising a Gale on Level Ground and Seeking Fortune and Avoiding Calamity!

Li Banfeng had learned quite a lot from the rickshaw driver. The five hundred yuan he spent was well spent.

"Elder Brother, from what you said, it sounds like hearing you say this, it seems that Homebound Cultivators pose a bit of a challenge for us Wanderlust Cultivators," Li Banfeng said.

"A bit? Oh, it's far worse than that," the rickshaw driver replied with a bitter smile. "Let me put it this way. Usually, we avoid Homebound Cultivators, and they avoid us. But once you clash with one of the same level, you must be prepared to fight to the death. If we step into a Homebound Cultivator's dwelling, we're doomed. If we drag them into a region of peril, they're doomed.

"I once encountered a Homebound Cultivator, and he nearly forced me into his dwelling. I let him give me a savage beating, then I tackled him, charging all the way to the edge of a cliff. We Wanderlust Cultivators have a steady footing, so I could stand firm. But Homebound Cultivators can't do the same. He lost his balance. He fell off the cliff and died. That's how I won.

"Don't even think about a fight ending with both sides going easy. There's no such luck. Cultivators on these two Daoist paths are mortal enemies. Whoever relaxes even a little will lose their life!"

Li Banfeng seized the chance to ask, "Elder Brother, do you know what abilities Homebound Cultivators have?"

"Hard to say." The rickshaw driver shook his head. "Homebound Cultivators share some of the same abilities, but they have other varied abilities. The one thing they all have in common is that it's hard to identify what their abilities are. Every Homebound Cultivator I've met had tricks of their own. Homebound Cultivators are rare to begin with, and the temperaments of dwelling spirits are impossible to predict. That makes them very hard to deal with. If you encounter one, it's best to get away from them if you can."

Li Banfeng still wanted to ask a few more questions, but the rickshaw driver suddenly stopped walking. "We've arrived at the train station. Safe travels."

Just like that, they had arrived at the train station.

As Li Banfeng got off the rickshaw, he asked one more question. "Aside from Raising a Gale on Level Ground and Seeking Fortune and Avoiding Calamity, do Wanderlust Cultivators have other abilities?"

"Yes, we have plenty of abilities. But we've reached your destination."

Li Banfeng took out his wallet. "Take me a little further, or just stay and chat with me. I'll pay you. Just name your price."

The rickshaw driver laughed. "Save your money. Our fate together ends here today. If we're destined to meet again, I'll tell you more then."

After that, the rickshaw driver wiped the sweat from his face with a towel and pulled his rickshaw away from the station square.

Li Banfeng had walked out of this train station just several days ago, and now he was about to set off on another trip, this time back to Yuezhou.

When I get back, should I visit Jiaqing? But who knows if the person lying in the hospital is truly Jiaqing?

But… even if it is him, that would mean I would still inevitably get entangled with the Dark Star Bureau again.

That made Li Banfeng quite hesitant to get involved with He Jiaqing again.

Either way, I should buy a train ticket first.

Once I return to Yuezhou, I'll be able to… find a place to carry bricks for money.

Li Banfeng rubbed his forehead. What exactly is so good about Yuezhou?

The train station was very small, nothing more than a row of single-story buildings. The departure lounge was slightly taller, and right beside it was the ticket office.

Recalling what the rickshaw driver had taught him, Li Banfeng first controlled his thighs, then his knees, and lastly, his ankles and toes.

The walking method was correct, but it was difficult to figure out how to control the force in his legs and make it narrower than a hemp cord. When Li Banfeng lifted his leg, he shot forward straight into the ticket office.

Fortunately, the ticket office was empty. Li Banfeng managed to put the brakes on his step just in time, coming to a stop right in front of the ticket counter.

The ticket clerk was dozing off and hadn't noticed Li Banfeng's unusual entrance. Li Banfeng called out several times, "I want to buy a ticket!"

The ticket clerk rubbed her eyes and yawned. Then she asked, "Where to?"

"Yuezhou."

"Seat or sleeper?"

A seat was out of the question. He had to get a sleeper, and it had to be a first-class sleeper!

The ticket clerk inputted the information to print the ticket. "Train 1160, first-class sleeper, thirteen thousand yuan. The earliest one departs tomorrow night at nine o'clock."

Thirteen thousand!

That expensive?!

Li Banfeng froze. He hadn't brought that much money with him; most of his money was stored in the Pocket Dwelling.

Just as he was about to excuse himself to go grab the money from the Pocket Dwelling, the ticket clerk said, "Show me your travel permit first."

"travel permit? What's that?" Li Banfeng asked in confusion.

The ticket clerk frowned. "Without a travel permit, what right do you have to buy a ticket?"

Li Banfeng was puzzled. What right do they have to stop me from buying a ticket? What kind of rule is this?

At that moment, a man in a Gatsby cap entered the ticket office and silently fixed his gaze on Li Banfeng.

He had been waiting there for a long time.

He was Chu Yunlong, also known as the Ninth Patrol Captain. He was the leader of the ninth patrol squad under the Wind Watcher[1] in the second branch of the Medicine King Hall in the Jiangxiang Gang.

Boss Xiao had been right that Li Banfeng would definitely show up at the train station eventually.

Chu Yunlong slowly walked toward him.

Li Banfeng was still quarreling with the ticket clerk. "You need a travel permit just to ride a train? Why don't you just ask for a customs clearance document too? Is this train headed to the West to fetch Buddhist scriptures?!"[2]

He was shaking with rage. He was convinced that the ticket clerk was deliberately making things difficult for him.

"Have you ever even taken a train before? Don't you know the basic rules?" The ticket clerk got angry too. In her eyes, it was Li Banfeng who was being unreasonable.

While the two argued, Chu Yunlong stepped closer and closer.

With both hands in his pockets and a blank expression on his face, Chu Yunlong looked as if he were simply lining up behind Li Banfeng to buy a ticket.

In reality, all he needed was to touch Li Banfeng. Once he did, he could control him, force him to reveal the whereabouts of the Arcane Crimson Lotus, and then take his life.

However, there were still seven or eight meters between them. In the meantime, Li Banfeng and the ticket clerk's argument grew louder.

"When I got here, nobody mentioned anything about a travel permit. Why do you suddenly have so many rules?"

"I don't care where you're from. Without a travel permit, you can't buy a ticket. Complaining won't change that. Those are the rules. Buy a ticket if you want. If you're not buying one, then get lost!"

Perfect. Keep arguing like this, Chu Yunlong thought.

The corners of his mouth curved up slightly as he continued to approach Li Banfeng.

"What kind of rule is this? Show me your rules so I can see them for myself!" Li Banfeng roared, his emotions slipping out of control.

"Why should I show you? Who do you think you are?" the ticket clerk retorted, unwilling to back down.

"If you won't show me the rules, that proves there aren't any! I'll go in and grab the ticket myself. Let's see how you'll stop me!"

By this point, Chu Yunlong was standing right behind Li Banfeng, slowly reaching out his hand.

Keep arguing, don't turn around. Yes, just like that—wait!

What's going on?

Chu Yunlong's hand did not make contact with Li Banfeng.

Li Banfeng had leaped onto the counter, about to force his way into the internal part of the ticket office!

The ticket clerk was stunned. "How dare you! Just try coming in!"

However, Li Banfeng really was going in. He was not the type to silently accept a loss.

At that moment, both Chu Yunlong and the ticket clerk shared the same thought: Is this man insane?

Chu Yunlong couldn't make his move now, not while Li Banfeng was on top of the counter.

The ticket clerk quickly yanked the alarm, and a deafening bell rang out through the ticket office.

Li Banfeng stopped trying to break in. Crouching on the counter, he glanced back at Chu Yunlong, then toward the main entrance of the ticket office.

Two men in uniform burst through the doors. Li Banfeng didn't know them, but Chu Yunlong did.

They were station patrol officers.

The ticket clerk pointed at Li Banfeng and shouted, "He's here to snatch a ticket! He doesn't have a travel permit. He's trying to cross the border illegally!"

Without a word, the two patrol officers charged at Li Banfeng.

Chu Yunlong quickly stepped aside. He had no intention of getting caught up in the trouble.

Li Banfeng jumped down from the counter and shouted to Chu Yunlong, "Brother, run! What are you standing there for?"

"No, what…?" Chu Yunlong uttered. He blinked in shock, at a loss for words.

Then Li Banfeng bolted. He moved extremely fast, dashing past the two patrol officers and out of the ticket office in the blink of an eye.

Seeing that, the two patrol officers immediately turned to block Chu Yunlong instead.

Chu Yunlong licked his lips, watching as the patrol officers charged toward him. He didn't know what he should say to explain this away.

1. This is a position in the gang ☜

2. This is a reference to Journey to the West, one of the Four Great Classical Novels of Chinese literature. It tells the story of a Buddhist monk, Tang Sanzang, and his companions Sun Wukong the Monkey King, Zhu Bajie, a half-man, half-pig, and Sha Wujing, a sand demon, who are tasked with traveling from China to India to retrieve sacred Buddhist scriptures that will save the world. ☜

Chapter 41: The Jiangxiang Gang

The patrol officers were right in front of Chu Yunlong. What should he do now?

He could say, I don't know that man!

But it would be useless. The patrol officers would just drag him back to the station. And with his background not being entirely clean, he absolutely could not go to the station.

Should he try saying something else?

However, the patrol officers did not even give him the chance to speak. They swung their batons straight at his head.

Wham!

The blow sent blood streaming down Chu Yunlong's forehead.

Yet, he didn't dare fight back. Helpless, he dodged past the two officers and dashed out of the ticket office.

Once he got outside, Chu Yunlong found no sign of Li Banfeng anywhere.

Of course, he couldn't. The moment Li Banfeng rushed out of the ticket office, he had entered the Pocket Dwelling and tossed his house key into a patch of weeds just outside the ticket office.

"Where the hell did that little bastard run off to?" Chu Yunlong cursed.

Then suddenly, the back of his head exploded with pain.

Wham! Wham!

The patrol officers had followed Chu Yunlong out of the ticket office and swung their batons at his head again.

Clutching his head, Chu Yunlong rushed toward the overhead bridge, where the numerous branching paths gave him a chance to escape.

Chu Yunlong fled ahead, with the two patrol officers in hot pursuit.

Li Banfeng emerged from the Pocket Dwelling and watched the three people disappear into the distance. He followed them silently.

It was time to put his legs to the test. He couldn't walk too quickly or too slowly as he followed them. He had to keep just the right pace and distance.

In truth, Li Banfeng's control over his walking speed was still poor. He still couldn't properly restrain his steps. At one point, he closed in on the patrol officers, reducing the distance between them to less than ten meters. He nearly exposed his presence.

Luckily for Li Banfeng, he was also a Homebound Cultivator. Having just left his dwelling, he was shrouded in a special aura that allowed him to go unnoticed, causing the officers to overlook him.

Chu Yunlong, on the other hand, was so busy evading capture that he had not noticed that Li Banfeng was following them.

They ran through three alleys, going all the way to the outskirts of the town. By then, the patrol officers were exhausted from both running and beating Chu Yunlong. They finally gave up the chase.

Covered in blood, Chu Yunlong propped himself on his knees and panted heavily inside an abandoned house.

He couldn't comprehend Li Banfeng's earlier actions. He couldn't understand why Li Banfeng had tried to force his way into the inner part of the ticket office.

Li Banfeng wasn't from Puluo; he was from the Outer Provinces. Nevertheless, it didn't matter where he was from, where he had boarded the train, or which stop he entered Puluo at. A travel permit was mandatory. That was the rule.

Did Li Banfeng not know the rules?

Then how had he made it to Medicine King Gully?

The only possibility was if someone of great influence had broken the rules and bought the train ticket for him.

However, even if he did have someone powerful supporting him, he still shouldn't have forced his way into the ticket office.

In Puluo, breaking into the ticket office had extremely severe consequences.

As his breathing gradually steadied, Chu Yunlong calmed down and wiped the blood from his face.

Could there have been another reason for Li Banfeng's sudden outburst earlier?

Did he know I was about to take action on him?

Did he provoke the ticket clerk on purpose, just to slip away from me?

Judging from his speed, he's clearly a Wanderlust Cultivator. Wanderlust Cultivators have the ability to sense danger.

Which means his quarrel with the ticket clerk must have been an act!

He sensed my killing intent and deliberately made a scene to get away.

The brat's quite cunning!

Where could he have gone now?

He was empty-handed earlier. If he's not carrying a bag, that means he doesn't have the treasure on him.

If I were him, I would definitely move the treasure somewhere else right now.

So how can I catch him?

I should order my men to search the area around the train station. Li Banfeng wouldn't dare keep the Arcane Crimson Lotus on him. It's most likely hidden somewhere close by…

While Chu Yunlong was lost in his thoughts, he suddenly felt a small sickle, over ten centimeters long, press against his neck.

"Who are you?" Chu Yunlong was startled. His senses weren't the sharpest, but after so many years in the jianghu, no one should have been able to approach him so easily.

Unless my opponent's cultivation level is far higher than mine.

Or they're a Homebound Cultivator.

If their cultivation levels were too far apart, Chu Yunlong could only wait for death.

However, if the opponent was a Homebound Cultivator, Chu Yunlong wasn't too worried. He had ways of dealing with them.

"My friend, let’s put the weapon down first. We don’t need to destroy each other like the flood destroyed the Dragon King’s family[1]. Whatever the matter, we can discuss it—"

The sickle's blade sank into Chu Yunlong's neck, and a rush of hot blood spurted out.

Chu Yunlong was shocked. He had intended to talk things over, but the other party had struck without hesitation.

"Who sent you after me?" Li Banfeng asked calmly with the sickle in hand.

It's him! It's Li Banfeng!

Chu Yunlong was dumbfounded. How had Li Banfeng managed to get so close to him?

I didn't sense even the faintest trace of presence!

Is he a Homebound Cultivator?

Impossible. From the signs I saw earlier, he's clearly a Wanderlust Cultivator.

Could his cultivation level really be far above mine?

But Boss Xiao said Li Banfeng wasn't a cultivator before, that he was just a Bleater, a kid.

It seems that Boss Xiao's information isn't reliable.

The sickle blade dug a little deeper into Yun Chunlong's neck.

Li Banfeng asked, "Not talking?"

Chu Yulong was in too much pain and too fearful to lie. With how ruthless Li Banfeng was being, Chu Yunlong was convinced that he definitely wasn't some kid.

Maybe he tricked Boss Xiao.

Or more likely, Boss Xiao tricked me!

Li Banfeng was clearly a ruthless man, yet he'd been called a kid. Boss Xiao had pulled stunts like that before.

Li Banfeng pressed the sickle blade into Chu Yunlong's neck again, slowly cutting deeper. That was enough to make Chu Yunlong abandon any thoughts of resistance or stalling.

"It was an order from our ladle handle!"

"Don't spout gibberish. Speak so I can understand!" Li Banfeng said, twisting the sickle blade inside Chu Yunlong's wound.

Chu Yunlong no longer dared use gang slang. "It was our boss who sent me."

"Which group are you from? And who is your boss?"

"We're the Jiangxiang Gang. Our boss is Xiao Zhenggong."

Li Banfeng frowned. "Have I met your boss before?"

"I assume so? I don't really know…"

Chu Yunlong was telling the truth. He really didn't know whether Xiao Zhenggong had met Li Banfeng before.

Li Banfeng tightened his grip on the sickle, sinking its blade even deeper into Chu Yunlong's throat.

Anyone who had spent long enough in the jianghu knew there was no reasoning with someone like Li Banfeng. Chu Yunlong had no choice but to spill everything he knew.

"Our boss is from the Outer Provinces. He's part of the Dark Star Bureau. You might know him."

The Dark Star Bureau!

"What does your boss want with me?"

"He said you carry a treasure. He ordered us to kill you and bring it back," Chu Yunlong said, sliding his hand toward the knife at his waist.

He moved it as stealthily as he could.

In this situation, he had no choice but to risk everything on one strike.

Li Banfeng, however, was still thinking about Xiao Zhenggong.

So, the Jiangxiang Gang is after me because of the bronze lotus.

It seems Xiao Zhenggong is the "Deputy Xiao" that I overheard that man with lightbulb-like eyes mention over the phone at the train station!

He's the one who had ordered my execution!

Li Banfeng asked another question. "How many people does your Jiangxiang Gang have? Are they all hunting me down?"

Hearing that, Chu Yunlong didn't rush to draw his knife.

Li Banfeng hadn't even heard of the Jiangxiang Gang. In fact, he seemed to know nothing at all about Puluo.

Against someone like this, there was no need for Chu Yunlong to take a gamble with his life. He just needed to outsmart Li Banfeng.

Chu Yunlong said, "The Jiangxiang Gang has disciples all across Puluo. Together, there are over ten thousand of us. Boss Xiao has already given the order. Right now, every disciple of the Jiangxiang Gang is out looking for you. Friend, I can tell you're no ordinary man. Spare me, and I'll act as an informant for you inside the gang. Whenever there's news, I'll let you know ahead of time. As long as you act a little cautiously, they'll never be able to find you.

"Once the storm passes, I'll find a few friends to help you buy a ticket and send you back to the Outer Provinces. By then, even if our boss wants to go after you, he'll have to follow the laws of the Outer Provinces. It won't be like in Puluo, where he can kill you without any real consequences."

Chu Yunlong's hand was already wrapped around the hilt of his dagger. As a Martial Cultivator, he was slightly slower than a Wanderlust Cultivator. Nevertheless, as long as he got the chance to strike, Li Banfeng would surely die.

Li Banfeng nodded. "Once I return to the Outer Provinces, I'll change my name and hide my identity. Your boss will never be able to find me again."

Chu Yunlong responded in a sincere tone, "Leave it to me! I'll have the ticket ready within three days. We may have clashed at first, but now we've become friends, and I'll treat you as such with all my heart. Whether it's in the Jiangxiang Gang or in Medicine King Gully, ask around about the name Chu Yunlong, and you'll know what kind of man I am!"

"Your name is Chu Yunlong?" Li Banfeng said, clearly liking the sound of it. "Just hearing it, I can tell you're a good man!"

He can tell I'm a good man just from hearing my name?

That doesn't make any sense!

However, Chu Yunlong wasn't about to argue with him, of course. "I, Chu Yunlong, have long roamed the jianghu, and I've never betrayed a friend. Since fate has brought us together, I'll make sure you come to no harm, even if I must risk my life to do it!"

Hearing that, Li Banfeng was deeply moved. "Brother, things are very dangerous for you inside the gang. You must protect yourself. And above all, don't tell anyone about this matter!"

"Of course, brother. Rest assured. This is something only I, you, and the heavens and the earth know. No one else will ever hear of it."

Chu Yunlong was confident his plan was working. Li Banfeng's cultivation level wasn't low, and he was quite sharp, but he was ultimately just an outsider from the Outer Provinces. His heart was far from ruthless; his actions were not vicious enough. Li Banfeng had no chance of surviving in Puluo.

A few words were enough to trick him. He really was just a kid. Boss Xiao hadn't been wrong after all.

Good thing Chu Yunlong hadn't fought him head-on just now. It was better for Chu Yunlong to wait until Li Banfeng had put away his sickle first before striking him. That would be the safer course of action.

Once Li Banfeng moved the sickle away from Chu Yunlong's neck, Chu Yunlong would be able to take Li Banfeng's life in the very next second.

With that in mind, Chu Yunlong quickly added, "Brother, you have to trust me!"

"Brother, I trust you. I'll leave everything from here on to you," Li Banfeng said, easing the pressure on Chu Yunlong's throat slightly.

"Leave the rest to me. Brother, take care." Chu Yunlong tightened his grip on his dagger's hilt, waiting only for Li Banfeng to withdraw his blade so he could strike.

"Brother, take care of yourself!" Li Banfeng pulled back the sickle, slitting Chu Yunlong's throat in the same motion.

Clutching his neck, Chu Yunlong stared at Li Banfeng in horror.

Li Banfeng had withdrawn the sickle but only after cutting through Chu Yunlong's throat first.

When Li Banfeng eased the pressure on Chu Yunlong's throat a moment ago, he had simply been preparing to make the final cut.

How could this be?

Didn't he fall for it?

Shouldn't Li Banfeng be the one dying right now?

Why has it turned out like this…

This fucker is seriously ruthless!

If I'd known this would happen, I would have fought him head-on from the start!

As Chu Yunlong's vision began to fade, his eyes were filled with despair.

Li Banfeng comforted him, "Have a good journey. Don't be so nervous. It really hurts now, but the pain will pass soon.

"Don't worry. I definitely believe you'll keep your word. You can't even speak now, so you surely won't be telling anyone about this matter.

"Just relax a little more. That's right. Let go of that tension. Close your eyes. Stop staring at me like that. Honestly, I feel quite guilty about this."

1. I rephrased the translation so it would make sense without reading the ref note, but the idiom used here is a variant of the full idiom 认得一家人”, which translates to “A flood destroyed the Dragon King’s temple, leaving family members unable to recognize each other.” It refers to situations where members of the same group turn against each other due to misunderstandings. In this case, it’s probably referring to how Chu Yunlong thinks Li Banfeng is part of the underworld like him. The origin of the idiom is a myth about how the Dragon King of the Eastern Sea had mistakenly thought that his son had unleashed a flood that destroyed the Dragon King’s temple in the mortal world, causing the father and son to fight for three days. ☜

Chapter 42: Chu Yunlong's Gift

Looking at Chu Yunlong lying on the ground, Li Banfeng felt a pang in his heart. What a good man… He even said he would buy me a train ticket.

The wilderness was desolate, and wild beasts could appear at any moment. Li Banfeng couldn't bear to leave Chu Yunlong's body to rot in the open, so he resolved to take him into the Pocket Dwelling and refine him into a pill using the bronze lotus.

Inside the Pocket Dwelling, Li Banfeng lit the candlestick and laid the corpse beside the bronze lotus.

Srrrk~~

Upon sensing Chu Yunlong's presence, the bronze lotus stirred, its petals unfolding one by one.

Li Banfeng couldn't help but wonder, How did it sense Chu Yunlong? Was it because he possessed cultivation? But I possess cultivation too, so why didn't the bronze lotus try to refine me into a pill?

Could it be that it has already acknowledged me as its master? Or does it only recognize the dead and not the living?

Crackle~

What was that sound? Li Banfeng glanced at the gramophone, which seemed to have just released a puff of steam.

But I didn't light it…

As he was still puzzling over it, the candle flames flickered and a whirlwind swept through the Pocket Dwelling. Chu Yunlong's gray suit and white shirt were torn apart, and even the Gatsby cap on his head was ripped to shreds

The naked corpse twisted and warped until it melted into a paste-like essence that sank into the lotus core of the bronze lotus. The petals then folded shut, and its body glowed with halos of light as it began to refine the pill.

Tattered scraps of cloth littered the floor. Among them, Li Banfeng discovered a finely crafted dagger, still resting in its sheath. He drew it out, and its cold gleam cut through the air, a clear sign that it was a rare and exceptional weapon.

This is a good harvest, Li Banfeng thought to himself. He was about to examine the dagger more closely when a whirlwind swept through the Pocket Dwelling.

Once again, the whirlwind rose from the lotus core, and a powerful force tore the dagger from Li Banfeng's hands. He tried to grab it back, but he was too slow. The dagger dissolved into pulp and was swallowed by the lotus core along with everything else.

Is this how the gold-rimmed glasses guy's fan was swallowed? If the fan became a rusty red pill, then what would this dagger have turned into? Li Banfeng wondered.

By instinct, he clutched his sickle and trowel to his chest. Yet when he thought about it, those two tools had been near the bronze lotus many times, and it had never shown any interest in them.

He couldn't help but question, Just what standard does this bronze lotus use to choose its materials?

He couldn't save the dagger, and though he felt a twinge of regret, he kept searching through Chu Yunlong's belongings until he came across his wallet.

Worried that the bronze lotus might devour the banknotes inside, he carefully opened the wallet. Then he remembered, Wait… the bronze lotus never touched the two hundred thousand in cash that Shopkeeper Feng gave me. This lotus clearly has no love for money.

In Chu Yunlong's wallet, he found 1,226 yuan, 8 jiao, and 2 fen in banknotes, along with 6 silver dollars. Li Banfeng stuffed the notes into his pocket and tossed the silver coins onto the bed.[1].

After a moment, he picked one up and carefully examined it for a while. One side was stamped with the characters "Puluo," while the other bore the imprint of a flower.

What kind of flower is this? The petals looked a little strange.

He stared at it for a long while before finally realizing it was a snowflake.

As the bronze lotus went on refining the pill, Li Banfeng turned his thoughts to his situation and what he should do next.

The first problem was how to return to Yuezhou Province. He had no idea what a travel permit even was, and buying a ticket through official channels would be extremely difficult.

The second problem was the Dark Star Bureau. Even if he managed to get a travel permit, buy a ticket, and make it back to Yuezhou, would they really stop bothering him? There was no way they would let him go. As long as the bronze lotus was in his possession, Xiao Zhenggong would never stop pursuing him.

The third problem was whether he should hand over the bronze lotus in exchange for his own safety. Li Banfeng quickly pushed the thought aside as giving up the lotus wouldn't guarantee his safety at all.

Who can I give this thing to? Li Banfeng wondered. He Jiaqing? But He Jiaqing is most likely still unconscious lying on a hospital bed. Even if he woke up, can he even keep the bronze lotus flower safe? Should I give it to his Third Uncle? Or should I just hand it over to Xiao Zhenggong?

Those two men definitely want me to give them the bronze lotus, but they would never allow anyone to know they possessed it. The moment I handed it over, they would kill me and shut me up forever.

Xiao Zhenggong had already proven it through his actions. He had ordered the man with lightbulb eyes to execute Li Banfeng and ordered the Jiangxiang Gang to kill him and take the treasure back. From beginning to end, he had never intended to let Li Banfeng live. Li Banfeng could never hand over the bronze lotus, but he knew he needed to go back to Yuezhou.

Back in Yuezhou, Xiao Zhenggong had to hold himself back because of certain concerns. Here in Medicine King Gully, however, things were far more rampant and wild. Yet if Li Banfeng wanted to return to Yuezhou, he first needed a travel permit. Where could he obtain one?

Could Chubby Qin have a way? Could Shopkeeper Feng find a way?

Li Banfeng peeled off two jade-green lotus seeds. The first one burst open at once, revealing a rusty red iron bead identical to the Corrosion Pill he had seen before.

The sight of it made Li Banfeng's scalp prickle, and he flung the pill to the ground at once. Even with his quick reaction, a faint sting lingered in his palm. Its poison and corrosiveness were terrifying, yet he still felt an urge to test it for himself.

The previous Corrosion Pill had been refined from the fan, so this one must have come from the dagger. Li Banfeng carefully set it into the spectacle case, where the two Corrosion Pills sat quietly without reacting to each other.

After a while, the second lotus seed split open. As Li Banfeng peeled it apart, it burst with a flash, revealing two pills that glowed a deep crimson.

Two? When the man in gold-rimmed glasses and Tiger Lamb were refined, each had produced only one pill. Yet this time, refining Chu Yunlong alone had yielded two. Why?

The man in gold-rimmed glasses and Tiger Lamb were cultivators who had yet to reach level one. Did two pills get refined from Chun Yunlong because his cultivation was higher? If that's the case, then he was very likely a level-one cultivator.

But this first-level cultivator didn't seem that strong? Is it because I never gave him the space and time to show his skills?
